# WP3: `@gmt/db`

Reconciled on 2026-10-14 with spec revision 10 and the plan's rewritten "Cross-package contracts" ("Database" block and "Decisions taken at reconciliation", including round-2 item 14, plan commit 790d1ea).

## 1. Goal

Make `@gmt/db` the only place that touches SQLite: the complete spec §7 schema as one committed Drizzle migration, every repository and type in the plan's "Database" contract behind `createRepos`, the SQL-enforced `BudgetGuard`, the response cache, `app_lock`, the vendored airports and metro-code seed, and the maintenance job, each pinned by the spec §9 item 3 tests.

## 2. Scope

**In**

- Drizzle schema, one file per spec §7 table, under `packages/db/src/schema/`, plus `schema/index.ts`.
- The first and only migration, `packages/db/drizzle/0000_initial.sql` with its `meta/`, generated by `drizzle-kit` and committed.
- Every class, type and function in the plan's "Database" block, with those names and signatures, including `Repos`, `createRepos`, `RepoOptions`, `budgetConfigFromConfig`, `loadAirportsDataset`.
- `SqliteBudgetGuard`: every cap predicate in SQL inside `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, the billable rule at finalise, monthly windows per provider (billing day, UTC) and lifetime windows; per-execution caps from the execution's snapshot budget.
- `SqliteResponseCache`, `AppLock`, `seedAirports`, `sanityCheckAirports`, `runMaintenance`, and `backupDatabase` (decision 14: both bootstraps call it before WP12 exists).
- Vendored data under `packages/db/data/`: `airports.2026-10-01-a89e4e0.json` (from mwgg/Airports, MIT, `airports.LICENSE` beside it) and `metros.json` (IATA metropolitan codes from Wikipedia, CC BY-SA 4.0, `metros.ATTRIBUTION` beside it), plus the script that builds both.
- Repository rejections: non-integer money, timestamps not in `toISOString()` form, `provider_id = 'fixture'` ⇔ `source_kind = 'synthetic'` violations, synthetic rows when `RepoOptions.allowSynthetic` is false, unconverted prices where `price_pence ≠ price_native_minor`.

**Out**

- `openDatabase`, `pendingMigrations`, `applyMigrations` and the SQLite pragmas: WP0. WP3 adds the first migration and pins their behaviour against it.
- The backup CLI wrapper `scripts/backup.mjs` (WP12) and the boot order that calls `backupDatabase` when `pendingMigrations()` is non-empty and the file exists (WP8, WP4b).
- Heartbeat timers, the 60 s / 90 s wait choices, SIGTERM release: WP8 calls `AppLock`; WP13 tests "tool bootstrap starts no execution".
- Calling `reserve` before every HTTP attempt, cache lookups, `Retry-After`, FX egress (never ledgered, decision 2): WP4.
- Headroom for `skipped:budget` on resume, pre-start `usage + estimate ≤ cap`, the error table, selection, cabin-not-offered, parity and overrun flags, verdicts: WP7 (engine) and WP2 (core).
- The pure roll-up function `rollUp`: WP1. WP3 stores the same §6.6 table in SQL because the roll-up is recomputed inside each write transaction; WP7 adds a parity test between the two.
- Alert evaluation, dedup keys and digests: WP2 and WP11; WP3 stores rules and events and answers `priorFirings`.

## 3. Spec sections

- **§7 entire** (revision 10): every table, column, key, index and CHECK, including the revision-10 additions (`destination_notes_json`, `discovery_reason`, `verification_reason`, `verification_min_pence`, `search_executions.trigger`, nullable `provider_cache.provider_id`, `route_price_history.cabin` allowing `'unknown'`, cooldown default 336, metro codes from a separate attributed list); the transaction units; SQLite settings; idempotent migrations; retention; budget windows; `app_lock`; the airports seed with the 5,000-row sanity check and no network.
- **§5.3 "Cap predicates"**: billed vendors count `outcome IN ('pending','unknown') OR billable = 1`; free vendors count every non-cache row; `global-month` adds each enabled subscription provider's fixed fee once; a cap of 0 refuses when `current + this > 0` and zero-cost calls pass.
- **§5.3 "Billable rule"**: `searchapi` (and `fixture` under `syntheticAllowed`) billable iff HTTP 200; `travelpayouts` never; cache hits never. FX is not ledgered (§5.6, revision 10).
- **§5.3 "Crash between reserve and finalise"**: `pending` rows older than 10 minutes become `unknown` at boot and stay counted; every ledger row carries `tile_index` (NULL for verification).
- **§5.5 repository rejections**: synthetic rows refused unless allowed; `fixture` ⇔ `synthetic` both directions.
- **§9 item 3**: the db test list, each mapped to a test in section 9.

## 4. Interfaces consumed

Copied from the plan's "Cross-package contracts" (WP0 produces all of them).

```ts
// @gmt/core types/ids.ts
export type Iata = string & { readonly __brand: 'Iata' };
export type IsoDate = string & { readonly __brand: 'IsoDate' };
export type IsoDateTime = string & { readonly __brand: 'IsoDateTime' }; // Date#toISOString() form only
export const isoDateSchema: z.ZodType<IsoDate, string>;
export const isoDateTimeSchema: z.ZodType<IsoDateTime, string>;
export function asIata(s: string): Iata; export function asIsoDate(s: string): IsoDate; export function asIsoDateTime(s: string): IsoDateTime;

// types/provenance.ts
export type ProviderId = 'travelpayouts' | 'searchapi' | 'fixture';
export type SourceKind = 'authorised-cached' | 'scraped-aggregate' | 'scraped-search' | 'synthetic';
export type Cabin = 'economy' | 'premium_economy' | 'business' | 'first';
export type PointOfSale = string | 'unknown';   // ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 upper-case, or 'unknown'
export type FxInfo = { converted: false } | { converted: true; rateNano: number; rateDate: IsoDate; source: string };
export interface Provenance { providerId: ProviderId; sourceKind: SourceKind; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerObservedAt?: IsoDateTime; maxAgeHours: number | 'unknown'; fx: FxInfo; pointOfSale: PointOfSale; cabin: Cabin }

// types/definition.ts
export interface ResolvedSpace { origins: Iata[]; destinations: Iata[]; departureFrom: IsoDate; departureTo: IsoDate; minNights: number; maxNights: number }
export interface CellKey { origin: Iata; destination: Iata; departureDate: IsoDate; returnDate: IsoDate }
export interface Cell extends CellKey { nights: number }
export function cellKeyString(c: CellKey): string;        // `${origin}|${destination}|${departureDate}|${returnDate}`
export function parseCellKeyString(s: string): CellKey;
export interface ExecutionBudget { maxCalls: number; maxFreeCalls: number; maxCostMicroGbp: number }
export interface SearchDefinition {
  id: string; name: string; origins: Iata[]; destinations: Iata[]; window: WindowSpec; minNights: number; maxNights: number;
  cabin: Cabin; constraints: Constraints; verification: VerificationPolicy; budget: ExecutionBudget; providers: ProvidersConfig;
  destinationNotes: Partial<Record<Iata, string>>; onwardCostPence: Partial<Record<Iata, number>>;
  scheduleCron: string | null; scheduleTz: string; confirmedCostPence: number | null; enabled: boolean;
  createdAt: IsoDateTime; updatedAt: IsoDateTime; deletedAt: IsoDateTime | null;
}
export type DefinitionInput = Omit<SearchDefinition, 'id' | 'confirmedCostPence' | 'createdAt' | 'updatedAt' | 'deletedAt'>;
export type DefinitionSnapshot = SearchDefinition & { resolved: ResolvedSpace; resolvedAt: IsoDateTime; windowClamped: boolean; droppedAlwaysInclude: CellKey[] };

// types/execution.ts
export type ExecutionStatus = 'pending' | 'running' | 'paused_budget' | 'paused_error' | 'interrupted' | 'cancelled' | 'completed'
  | 'skipped_budget' | 'skipped_confirm' | 'skipped_window' | 'skipped_missed';
export const ACTIVE_STATUSES: readonly ExecutionStatus[] = ['pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'];
export const RESUMABLE_STATUSES: readonly ExecutionStatus[] = ['interrupted', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'cancelled', 'completed'];
export type Stage = 'discovery' | 'verification';
export type CellOutcomeStatus = 'not_run' | 'priced' | 'empty' | 'no_data' | 'error' | 'skipped';
export type SkipReason = 'not_selected' | 'budget' | 'provider_disabled' | 'departed';
export type NoDataReason = 'cabin-not-offered';
export interface CellOutcome extends CellKey {
  executionId: string; stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null;
  attempts: number; lastError: string | null; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime | null; minPence: number | null;
  cabin: Cabin | null; pointOfSale: PointOfSale | null; sourceKind: SourceKind | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null;
}
export interface RollUp { status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; reason: string | null; minPence: number | null }
export type SelectionReason = 'always_include' | 'top_n' | 'ceiling' | 'stride';
export interface Execution {
  id: string; definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; status: ExecutionStatus; stage: Stage | null;
  estimate: Estimate | null; cancelRequested: boolean; resumeAfter: IsoDateTime | null; autoResumeCount: number;
  startedAt: IsoDateTime | null; finishedAt: IsoDateTime | null; errorText: string | null; createdAt: IsoDateTime; trigger: 'manual' | 'schedule' | 'tool';
}
export interface Buckets { priced: number; empty: number; no_data: number; error: number; skipped: number; not_run: number; reused: number }

// types/itinerary.ts
export type SuppliedField = 'segments' | 'segmentTimes' | 'segmentCabins' | 'selfTransfer' | 'baggage' | 'fareConditions' | 'seller' | 'price';
export type Booking = { kind: 'get'; url: string; host: string } | { kind: 'post'; url: string; postData: string; host: string } | { kind: 'none' };
export interface Segment { marketingCarrier: string; operatingCarrier?: string; flightNumber: number; from: Iata; to: Iata; departLocal: string; arriveLocal: string; departUtc?: IsoDateTime; arriveUtc?: IsoDateTime; durationMinutes?: number; aircraft?: string }
export interface Layover { at: Iata; minutes: number; overnight: boolean; airportChange: boolean }
export interface NormalisedItinerary { hash: string; origin: Iata; destination: Iata; departureDate: IsoDate; returnDate: IsoDate; nights: number; outbound: Segment[]; inbound: Segment[]; outboundMinutes?: number; inboundMinutes?: number; stopsOut: number; stopsIn: number; layovers: Layover[]; airportChange?: boolean; marketingCarriers: string[] }
export interface Offer { itineraryHash: string; providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin }
export interface ObservationFields {
  pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo; taxesIncluded: boolean | null; selfTransfer: boolean | null;
  segmentCabins: Cabin[] | null; baggage: BaggageSnapshot | null; fareConditions: FareConditions | null; booking: Booking; bookingExpiresAt: IsoDateTime | null;
}
export interface Observation extends ObservationFields { id: number; offerId: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean }

// ports/provider.ts, ports/budget.ts
export interface Reservation { id: string; finalise(r: { outcome: 'ok' | 'error' | 'unknown'; httpStatus?: number; durationMs: number }): void }
export interface BudgetGuard {
  reserve(p: { providerId: ProviderId; executionId: string | null; tileIndex: number | null; costMicroGbp: number; httpAttempt: number; endpointPath: string }): Reservation;
  recordCacheHit(p: { providerId: ProviderId; executionId: string | null; tileIndex: number | null; endpointPath: string }): void;
  usage(scope: BudgetScope): Usage;
}
export type BudgetScope =
  | { kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost'; executionId: string }
  | { kind: 'provider-month' | 'provider-lifetime'; providerId: ProviderId }
  | { kind: 'global-month' };
export interface Usage { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; cap: number | null; windowStart: IsoDateTime | null; windowEnd: IsoDateTime | null }
// cap is null for a window the plan does not use (a month scope on a lifetime plan, and vice versa) and for a scope with no cap configured.
// Per-execution caps come from the execution's snapshot budget; child executions carry { maxCalls: CHILD_MAX_CALLS, maxFreeCalls: 0, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 } in their snapshot.
export interface ResponseCache { get(key: string): { body: unknown; storedAt: IsoDateTime } | undefined; set(key: string, body: unknown, ttlSeconds: number, storedAt: IsoDateTime): void }

// ports/logger.ts, ports/clock.ts, ports/notifier.ts
export interface Logger { debug(o: object, msg?: string): void; info(o: object, msg?: string): void; warn(o: object, msg?: string): void; error(o: object, msg?: string): void; child(bindings: object): Logger }
export interface Clock { now(): IsoDateTime; todayLondon(): IsoDate }
export const systemClock: Clock;
export function fixedClock(at: IsoDateTime): Clock & { advance(ms: number): void; set(at: IsoDateTime): void };
export type OperatorMessageKind = 'paused_budget' | 'paused_error' | 'skipped_budget' | 'skipped_confirm' | 'skipped_window' | 'skipped_missed' | 'interrupted' | 'provider_disabled' | 'provider_capped' | 'parity_break' | 'alerts_calendar_only' | 'crash_loop';

// types/alerts.ts
export type AlertRuleType = 'absolute' | 'dropPct' | 'dropAbs';
export interface AlertRule { id: string; definitionId: string; type: AlertRuleType; thresholdPence: number | null; pct: number | null; basis: ResultBasis; requireAllVerified: boolean | null; cooldownHours: number; enabled: boolean }
export type AlertRuleInput = Omit<AlertRule, 'id'>;
export interface AlertEvent { id: number; ruleId: string; identityKey: string; observationId: number | null; calendarPriceId: number | null; dedupKey: string; firedAt: IsoDateTime; status: 'sent' | 'failed' | 'suppressed'; reason: string | null; payload: unknown }
export interface PriorFiring { identityKey: string; dedupKey: string; firedAt: IsoDateTime; pricePence: number }

// errors.ts, config, api-contract
// new BudgetExceeded({ scope, cap, current, requested, providerId? }); stripUrlsInText(text): string
export interface ProviderLimits { monthlyCallCap: number; window: 'month' | 'lifetime'; billingDay: number; ratePerMinute: number; ratePerHour: number; concurrency: number; cacheTtlHours: number; costPerCallMicroGbp: number; planFixedMicroGbp: number; excludedFromSchedule: boolean }
// Config (plan "Config" block): spend: { monthlyCapMicroGbp; globalBillingDay; softConfirmMicroGbp; executionMaxCalls; executionMaxFreeCalls; executionMaxCostMicroGbp; childMaxCalls };
//   providers: { travelpayouts: { token; limits } | null; searchapi: { key; plan; trialUsed; limits } | null; fixture: { costMicroGbp: number } | null }
export function parseConfig(env: Record<string, string | undefined>, opts: ParseConfigOptions): ConfigResult;
export function syntheticAllowed(c: Pick<Config, 'nodeEnv' | 'databasePath' | 'allowFixtureProvider' | 'inContainer'>): boolean;
export type Estimate = z.infer<typeof estimateSchema>;
export type HistoryPoint = z.infer<typeof historyPointSchema>;          // api-contract (pinned, decision 14): { at: IsoDateTime; pricePence; provenance: Provenance }
export type RouteSeriesPoint = z.infer<typeof routeSeriesPointSchema>;  // api-contract (pinned): { date: IsoDate; pricePence; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerId; sourceKind }

// @gmt/db client.ts, migrate.ts (WP0)
export interface Db { raw: BetterSqlite3.Database; drizzle: BetterSQLite3Database; transaction<T>(fn: () => T): T; close(): void }
export function openDatabase(path: string): Db;
export function pendingMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): string[];
export function applyMigrations(db: Db, migrationsDir: string): { applied: string[] };
```

## 5. Interfaces produced

The plan's "Database" block, verbatim (WP3 owns everything after the WP0 lines):

```ts
export interface RepoOptions { allowSynthetic: boolean }
export interface Repos { airports: AirportsRepo; fx: FxRepo; definitions: DefinitionsRepo; executions: ExecutionsRepo; cells: CellsRepo; calendarPrices: CalendarPricesRepo; itineraries: ItinerariesRepo; ledger: LedgerRepo; alerts: AlertsRepo; notifications: NotificationsRepo; routeHistory: RoutePriceHistoryRepo }
export function createRepos(db: Db, clock: Clock, opts: RepoOptions): Repos;
export interface Airport { iata: Iata; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; datasetVersion: string }
export interface AirportsDataset { version: string; airports: Array<Omit<Airport, 'source' | 'datasetVersion'>>; metros: Record<string, string[]> }
export class AirportsRepo { exists(iata): boolean; get(iata): Airport | null; search(q: string, limit: number): Airport[]; metroOf(iata): string | null; airportsOfMetro(code: string): Iata[]; sharedMetro(iatas: Iata[]): string | null; count(): number; upsertAll(dataset: AirportsDataset): void; datasetVersion(): string | null; tzOf(iata): string | null }
export class FxRepo { rateNano(date: IsoDate, currency: string): number | null; latest(currency: string, maxAgeDays: number, today: IsoDate): { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate; source: string } | null; upsert(rows: Array<{ date: IsoDate; currency: string; rateNano: number; source: string }>): void }
export class DefinitionsRepo { create(input: DefinitionInput): SearchDefinition; update(id, patch: Partial<DefinitionInput>): SearchDefinition; get(id): SearchDefinition | null; list(opts?: { includeDeleted?: boolean }): SearchDefinition[]; softDelete(id): void; setConfirmedCost(id, pence: number | null): void }
export type ExecutionCreateInput = { definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; estimate: Estimate | null; trigger: Execution['trigger']; status: 'pending' | 'running' | ExecutionStatus & `skipped_${string}` }
export type ExecutionPatch = Partial<Pick<Execution, 'stage' | 'resumeAfter' | 'errorText' | 'startedAt' | 'finishedAt' | 'cancelRequested' | 'estimate' | 'snapshot'>>
export class ExecutionsRepo { create(input: ExecutionCreateInput): Execution; get(id): Execution | null; listForDefinition(definitionId, opts: { limit: number; includeChildren: boolean }): Execution[]; listByStatus(statuses: ExecutionStatus[]): Execution[]; activeParent(definitionId): Execution | null; activeChild(definitionId, cell: CellKey): Execution | null; latestCompleted(definitionId): Execution | null; claimResume(id): boolean; setStatus(id, status: ExecutionStatus, patch?: ExecutionPatch): void; patch(id, patch: ExecutionPatch): void; requestCancel(id): void; bootSweep(bootAt: IsoDateTime): { interrupted: string[] }; incrementAutoResume(id): number; resetAutoResume(id): void; lastScheduledFiring(definitionId): IsoDateTime | null }
export interface TileWrite { tileIndex: number; cells: CellKey[]; prices: CalendarPriceInsert[]; outcomes: Array<Pick<CellOutcome, 'origin' | 'destination' | 'departureDate' | 'returnDate' | 'status' | 'reason' | 'lastError' | 'fetchedAt' | 'minPence' | 'cabin' | 'pointOfSale' | 'sourceKind'>>; discards: number }
export interface CalendarPriceInsert { providerId; sourceKind; cabin; cell: CellKey; pricePence; priceNativeMinor; currencyNative; fx: FxInfo; pointOfSale; fetchedAt; providerObservedAt: IsoDateTime | null; maxAgeHours: number | null; vendorLink: string | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null }
export interface CalendarPriceRow extends CalendarPriceInsert { id: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance }
export interface VerifiedCellWrite { cell: CellKey; outcome: TileWrite['outcomes'][number]; itineraries: Array<{ itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; offers: Array<{ offer: Offer; observation: ObservationFields & { provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean } }> }>; bestObservationSelector: 'cheapest-verified-or-partial' }
export interface ReusedCopy { cell: CellKey; outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }
export class CellsRepo { seed(executionId, cells: Cell[], discoveryProviders: ProviderId[]): void; seedVerification(executionId, selected: Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>, allCells: CellKey[], verificationProviders: ProviderId[]): void; outcomes(executionId, filter?: { stage?: Stage; providerId?: ProviderId; cells?: CellKey[] }): CellOutcome[]; resumable(executionId, stage, providerId, manual: boolean): CellKey[]; markDeparted(executionId, today: IsoDate): number; markNoData(executionId, stage, providerId, reason: NoDataReason): number; writeTile(executionId, providerId, w: TileWrite): void; writeTileError(executionId, providerId, cells: CellKey[], reason: string, lastError: string | null): void; writeVerifiedCell(executionId, providerId, w: VerifiedCellWrite): void; setOutcome(executionId, cell, stage, providerId, patch: Partial<CellOutcome> & { incrementAttempts?: boolean }): void; setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, stage, providerId, from: CellOutcomeStatus[], to: { status; reason }): number; copyReused(executionId, providerId, copies: ReusedCopy[]): void; rollUps(executionId): Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }>; buckets(executionId, stage, providerId): Buckets; bucketsPerDay(executionId): Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage; providerId; buckets: Buckets; total }>>; selected(executionId): Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>; hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId): boolean }
export class CalendarPricesRepo { insert(executionId, rows: CalendarPriceInsert[]): number[]; latestPerCell(definitionId, cabin, space: ResolvedSpace | null): CalendarPriceRow[]; fetchedBy(executionId): CalendarPriceRow[] /* reused excluded */; previousFor(definitionId, providerId, cabin, cell, before: IsoDateTime, notOlderThan: IsoDateTime): CalendarPriceRow | null; reuseCandidates(definitionId, providerId, cabin, pointOfSale, sourceKind, cells: CellKey[], newerThan: IsoDateTime): ReusedCopy[]; supersedeReused(executionId, providerId, cell): void; history(origin, destination, cabin): Array<{ providerId; points: HistoryPoint[] }>; belowVerified(definitionId, cabin, space, thresholdPence): CalendarPriceRow[] }
export interface ObservationRow extends Observation { offer: Offer & { id: number }; itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; cell: Cell; seller: string }
export class ItinerariesRepo { upsertItinerary(it: NormalisedItinerary): void; upsertOffer(offer: Offer): number; insertObservation(obs: Omit<Observation, 'id'>): number; get(hash): (NormalisedItinerary & { firstSeenAt }) | null; observationsForDefinition(definitionId, opts: { executionIds: string[] | 'all'; space: ResolvedSpace | null; cabin: Cabin; includeChildren: boolean }): ObservationRow[]; writtenBy(executionId): ObservationRow[]; previousObservation(offerId: number, before: IsoDateTime): Observation | null; offerHistory(hash): Array<{ providerId; seller; cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }>; latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId, cell): CellOutcome | null; bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell): number | null }
export class LedgerRepo { countsFor(executionId): { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number; perProvider: Array<{ providerId; stage: Stage | null; calls; costMicroGbp; tiles; cacheHits }> }; firstAttemptTiles(executionId, providerId): number; sweepPending(olderThan: IsoDateTime): number; pruneCacheHits(olderThan: IsoDateTime, providerIds: ProviderId[]): number }
export class AlertsRepo { rules(definitionId?: string): AlertRule[]; get(id): AlertRule | null; create(input: AlertRuleInput): AlertRule; update(id, patch: Partial<AlertRuleInput>): AlertRule; delete(id): void; events(filter: { ruleId?: string; definitionId?: string; limit: number }): AlertEvent[]; priorFirings(ruleId, identityKeys: string[], since: IsoDateTime): PriorFiring[]; insertEvent(e: Omit<AlertEvent, 'id'>): number }
export class NotificationsRepo { wasSent(kind: OperatorMessageKind, executionId: string | null, definitionId: string): boolean; record(kind, executionId, definitionId, status: 'sent' | 'failed'): void }
export class RoutePriceHistoryRepo { upsert(rows: Array<{ origin; destination; cabin: Cabin | 'unknown'; providerId; date: IsoDate; lowestPricePence: number; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime }>): void; series(origin, destination, providerId): RouteSeriesPoint[] }
export interface BudgetConfig { providers: Partial<Record<ProviderId, ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number }>>; spend: Config['spend']; fixtureCostMicroGbp: number }   // trialUsed seeds a lifetime window: effective cap = monthlyCallCap − trialUsed
export function budgetConfigFromConfig(c: Config): BudgetConfig;
export class SqliteBudgetGuard implements BudgetGuard { constructor(db: Db, config: BudgetConfig, clock: Clock) }
export class SqliteResponseCache implements ResponseCache { constructor(db: Db, clock: Clock) }
export class AppLock { constructor(db: Db, holder: 'app' | 'tool', clock: Clock, log: Logger); acquire(opts: { waitMs: number }): 'acquired' | 'refused'; heartbeat(): void; release(): void; static inspect(db: Db): { holder; heartbeatAt; acquiredAt } | null }
export function seedAirports(db: Db, dataset: AirportsDataset): { inserted: number; datasetVersion: string };
export function sanityCheckAirports(db: Db, referenced: Iata[]): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string };
export function runMaintenance(db: Db, clock: Clock, log: Logger, opts: { lifetimeProviders: ProviderId[] }): { cacheRowsDeleted: number; ledgerRowsPruned: number; dbBytes: number };
export function loadAirportsDataset(dir: string): AirportsDataset;
export function backupDatabase(dbPath: string, backupsDir: string, opts: { keep: number; clock: Clock }): { file: string };   // src/backup.ts: VACUUM INTO a timestamped file, PRAGMA integrity_check on the output, refuse an existing target, prune to `keep`; throws on any failure
```

Every repository is constructed as `new XRepo(db, clock, opts)` with `opts: RepoOptions`; `createRepos` is the only place production code constructs them. Additional exports (all additive; nothing in the block is changed):

```ts
export class RowRejected extends Error { readonly rule: string }          // internal/validate.ts: every repository rejection
export const MIGRATIONS_DIR: string;                                       // paths.ts: absolute path of packages/db/drizzle
export const AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR: string;                                    // paths.ts: absolute path of packages/db/data (the loadAirportsDataset argument)
export const AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS = 5000;
export const CLAIM_RESUME_SQL: string;                                     // the one compare-and-set statement (spec §8.1)
export function monthWindow(db: Db, now: IsoDateTime, billingDay: number): { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime };
export const HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_MS = 30_000; export const LOCK_STALE_AFTER_MS = 90_000;
export * as schema from './schema/index.js';
// AppLock takes an optional 5th constructor argument { sleep?(ms): void; pollMs?: number } so tests drive the wait with a fake clock; production omits it.
```

## 6. Library notes

Verified on 2026-10-14. Versions are the plan's Task 0.1 pins.

- **better-sqlite3 13.0.3** (context7 `/wiselibs/better-sqlite3`, `docs/api.md`; the indexed docs are v12.6.2 and these APIs are unchanged in 13). `db.transaction(fn)` returns a function that runs `BEGIN`, commits on return and rolls back on throw; variants `.deferred()`, `.immediate()`, `.exclusive()`; WP3 uses `db.raw.transaction(fn).immediate()` in `reserve` and `AppLock` (spec §7 "Acquisition and `reserve()` use `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`"). Nested transaction functions become savepoints. "Transaction functions do not work with async functions." SQLite may roll back on `SQLITE_FULL`/`SQLITE_BUSY`, so errors are rethrown, never swallowed. `db.pragma(name, { simple: true })` returns the first column of the first row. `SqliteError` (`Database.SqliteError`) subclasses `Error` and carries `code` (extended result code, e.g. `SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE`, `SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK`, `SQLITE_BUSY`). `stmt.run/get/all`; named parameters `@name` bound from an object; extra keys are ignored, a missing or `undefined` value throws; booleans are not bindable (bind 0/1). `RETURNING` works with `.all()`/`.get()`. Works inside `node:worker_threads`.
- **drizzle-orm 0.45.4** `drizzle-orm/sqlite-core` (context7 `/drizzle-team/drizzle-orm-docs`, `sqlite/indexes-constraints.mdx`): `sqliteTable(name, columns, (t) => [ ... ])`; `integer`, `text`, `real`; `primaryKey({ columns })`; `foreignKey({ columns, foreignColumns })`; `.references(() => t.id, { onDelete: 'cascade' })`; `index(...).on(...)`, `uniqueIndex(...).on(...)`, **partial indexes** `.on(...).where(sql\`...\`)` (documented for SQLite); `check(name, sql\`...\`)`. CHECK and WHERE bodies are written with `sql.raw` and unqualified column names. Repositories use `db.raw.prepare` with explicit SQL, not the query builder. `// why:` the queries are window functions, CTEs, `json_each`, correlated roll-up updates and compare-and-set statements whose exact SQL reviewers must read; Drizzle owns schema and migration. If drizzle-kit emits a partial unique index without its `WHERE` (asserted in Task 3.8), remove the two `uniqueIndex(...)` entries from `schema/searchExecutions.ts`, keep their SQL in `packages/db/drizzle.custom.sql`, and append it to `drizzle/0000_initial.sql` after each generate, each statement preceded by `--> statement-breakpoint`.
- **drizzle-kit 0.31.11** (context7, `drizzle-kit-generate.mdx`): `drizzle-kit generate --name=<tag>` writes `drizzle/0000_<tag>.sql` and `drizzle/meta/{_journal.json,0000_snapshot.json}` (the 0.x layout WP0's `pendingMigrations` reads); with no schema change it writes nothing. Command throughout: `pnpm --filter @gmt/db db:generate` (= `drizzle-kit generate --name=initial`). If drizzle-kit cannot resolve the schema's `./x.js` imports, add `tsx@4.23.15` as a db devDependency and run `pnpm --filter @gmt/db exec tsx node_modules/drizzle-kit/bin.cjs generate --name=initial`.
- **vitest 5.0.4**: `describe`, `it`, `it.each`, `expect(...).toThrow(Class | RegExp | asymmetric matcher)`, `vi.fn`, `vi.stubGlobal`, `vi.unstubAllGlobals`. Per-task runs use `pnpm --filter @gmt/db exec vitest run <file>` (decision 8: no coverage threshold on a partial run); thresholds (85/70/85/85) apply to the full `pnpm --filter @gmt/db test`.
- **node:worker_threads** (Node ≥ 22): `Worker`, `SharedArrayBuffer`, `Atomics` as a start barrier, in one race test.
- **mwgg/Airports** (https://github.com/mwgg/Airports, MIT, read 2026-10-14): `airports.json` keyed by ICAO with `icao, iata, name, city, state, country, elevation, lat, lon, tz`; no metro field. At commit `a89e4e0dda9d9878337e2da190ebe1ef1d6af705` (2026-10-01): 29,308 entries, 7,917 with a three-letter IATA code and a `tz`, no duplicate IATA codes; LHR → `Europe/London`, EWR → `America/New_York`.
- **Wikipedia, "IATA airport code", section "Cities with multiple commercial airports"** (CC BY-SA 4.0), revision `1378312633`, raw wikitext at `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=IATA_airport_code&oldid=1378312633&action=raw&section=8`. Each bullet reads `City (MET)&nbsp;– Airport (AAA), …`; parsing `\(([A-Z]{3})\)&nbsp;–` for the metro and `\(([A-Z]{3})[,)]` for members yields 48 metros on that revision, including `LON: LCY LGW LHR LTN SEN STN` and `NYC: EWR IDL JFK LGA`. The list is illustrative, not exhaustive: an airport absent from it has `metro = NULL`, which the spec handles as per-airport Travelpayouts queries.

## 8. Tasks

Conventions for every task: run commands from the repository root; tests import from `../src/...js`; when a step says "Add to" a file, its `import` lines go to the top of that file and merge with existing ones; every commit message ends with the repository's attribution line `MrReasonable <4990954+MrReasonable@users.noreply.github.com>`. "Regenerate the migration" means `rm -rf packages/db/drizzle && pnpm --filter @gmt/db db:generate` (plus the section 6 fallback append, if it was needed).

### Task 3.1: Ledger tables, the global-month cap in SQL, and the first migration (first failing test)

**Files:**
- Create: `src/paths.ts`, `src/types.ts`, `src/internal/validate.ts`, `src/schema/checks.ts`, `src/schema/searchDefinitions.ts`, `src/schema/searchExecutions.ts`, `src/schema/providerRequests.ts`, `src/budget/window.ts`, `src/budgetConfig.ts` (types), `src/budgetGuard.ts`, `test/helpers/db.ts`, `test/helpers/factories.ts`, `test/budgetGuard.cap.test.ts`, `test/migrations.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/schema/index.ts`, `package.json`
- Generate: `drizzle/0000_initial.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `openDatabase`, `pendingMigrations`, `applyMigrations`, `BudgetGuard`, `Reservation`, `BudgetExceeded`, `ProviderLimits`, `fixedClock`, `isoDateTimeSchema`, `stripUrlsInText`.
- Produces: `SqliteBudgetGuard` (provider and global-month predicates), `BudgetConfig`, `monthWindow`, `RowRejected`, `MIGRATIONS_DIR`, `AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR`, every type in `src/types.ts`, the `provider_requests`, `search_definitions`, `search_executions` tables.

**Arithmetic pinned by this task.** The cap is 300p = 300 × 10 000 = **3,000,000 µGBP**; each call costs **3,200 µGBP** (0.32p, spec §6.3). After *k* billed calls the global usage is 3,200·*k*. A reserve is refused when `current + this > cap`, i.e. when 3,200·*k* + 3,200 > 3,000,000, i.e. *k* + 1 > 937.5. So reserves 1 to **937** succeed (937 × 3,200 = **2,998,400 µGBP**), and reserve 938 throws `BudgetExceeded` with scope `global-month`, `current` 2,998,400, `requested` 3,200, `cap` 3,000,000 (2,998,400 + 3,200 = 3,001,600 > 3,000,000). Spec §9 item 3's literal form: 1,000 ledger rows × 3,200 µGBP = **3,200,000 µGBP = 320p**, already over the 300p cap, so the next 3,200 µGBP reserve throws with `current` 3,200,000.

```ts
import { mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import type { IsoDateTime, Logger } from '@gmt/core';
import { type Db, openDatabase } from '../../src/client.js';
import { applyMigrations } from '../../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../../src/paths.js';

/** The instant every test starts from unless it says otherwise: 2026-10-14T12:00Z. */
export const T0 = '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime;

/** A migrated database. `:memory:` by default; pass a path for multi-handle tests. */
export function freshDb(path = ':memory:'): Db {
  const db = openDatabase(path);
  applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
  return db;
}

/** A unique file path ending in `.test.db` (the synthetic gate's test suffix, spec §5.5). */
export function tmpDbPath(name = 'wp3.test.db'): string {
  return join(mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'gmt-db-')), name);
}

/** A logger that records nothing; tests that assert on logs spread it and replace one method with vi.fn(). */
export const silentLog: Logger = { debug: () => undefined, info: () => undefined, warn: () => undefined, error: () => undefined, child: () => silentLog };

export const count = (db: Db, table: string): number => (db.raw.prepare(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM ${table}`).get() as { n: number }).n;
```

```ts
import type { ProviderLimits } from '@gmt/core';
import type { BudgetConfig } from '../../src/budgetConfig.js';

/** One provider's guard entry. Defaults: the 40usd subscription shape with no fixed fee. */
export function limits(over: Partial<ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number }> = {}): ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number } {
  return {
    monthlyCallCap: 10_000, window: 'month', billingDay: 1, ratePerMinute: 10, ratePerHour: 2000, concurrency: 2, cacheTtlHours: 1,
    costPerCallMicroGbp: 0, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, excludedFromSchedule: false, billable: true, trialUsed: 0, ...over,
  };
}

/** A guard configuration; `spend` overrides the config defaults (spec §12). */
export function budgetConfig(providers: BudgetConfig['providers'], spend: Partial<BudgetConfig['spend']> = {}): BudgetConfig {
  return {
    providers,
    spend: { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 0, globalBillingDay: 1, softConfirmMicroGbp: 1_000_000, executionMaxCalls: 2000, executionMaxFreeCalls: 2000, executionMaxCostMicroGbp: 0, childMaxCalls: 6, ...spend },
    fixtureCostMicroGbp: 0,
  };
}
```

`packages/db/test/budgetGuard.cap.test.ts`:

```ts
import { BudgetExceeded, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits } from './helpers/factories.js';

const COST = 3_200; // µGBP per call: 0.32p (spec §6.3)
const CAP = 300 * 10_000; // 300p in µGBP = 3,000,000
// A per-call SearchApi plan (no fixed fee) so the global cap is driven by per-call cost alone.
const perCall = budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: COST }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: CAP });
const call = { providerId: 'searchapi' as const, executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: COST, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' };

describe('SqliteBudgetGuard global-month cap in SQL (spec §5.3, §9 item 3)', () => {
  it('937 calls of 3,200 µGBP pass a 3,000,000 µGBP cap and the 938th throws global-month', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, perCall, fixedClock(T0));
    for (let k = 1; k <= 937; k++) guard.reserve(call).finalise({ outcome: 'ok', httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 10 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' }).costMicroGbp).toBe(2_998_400);
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      guard.reserve(call);
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(BudgetExceeded);
    expect(thrown).toMatchObject({ scope: 'global-month', cap: 3_000_000, current: 2_998_400, requested: 3_200 });
    // the refused reserve wrote no ledger row: predicates and insert share one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(937);
  });

  it('1,000 ledger rows at 3,200 µGBP (320p) trip a 300p cap in SQL', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const insert = db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, finished_at, duration_ms, http_status, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
       VALUES ('searchapi', NULL, '/api/v1/search', NULL, @at, @at, 5, 200, 'ok', 1, 3200, 1)`,
    );
    db.transaction(() => {
      for (let i = 0; i < 1000; i++) insert.run({ at: T0 });
    });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, perCall, fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 1000, costMicroGbp: 3_200_000, cap: 3_000_000 });
    expect(() => guard.reserve(call)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'global-month', current: 3_200_000, requested: 3_200 }));
  });
});
```

`packages/db/test/migrations.test.ts`:

```ts
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { openDatabase } from '../src/client.js';
import { applyMigrations, pendingMigrations } from '../src/migrate.js';
import { MIGRATIONS_DIR } from '../src/paths.js';
import { count, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';

const schemaOf = (db: ReturnType<typeof openDatabase>) =>
  db.raw.prepare(`SELECT type, name, sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name NOT LIKE 'sqlite_%' ORDER BY type, name`).all();
const insertLedgerRow = (db: ReturnType<typeof openDatabase>) =>
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, outcome, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt) VALUES ('travelpayouts', '/v3', '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 'pending', 0, 1)`).run();

describe('migrations (spec §7 "Migrations", §9 item 3)', () => {
  it('apply twice: the second run applies nothing and changes nothing', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(tmpDbPath());
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual(['0000_initial']);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: ['0000_initial'] });
    const before = schemaOf(db);
    expect(applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual({ applied: [] });
    expect(schemaOf(db)).toEqual(before);
    expect(pendingMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR)).toEqual([]);
    expect(count(db, '__drizzle_migrations')).toBe(1);
    db.close();
  });

  it('a missing migrations directory fails naming the directory', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(':memory:');
    expect(() => applyMigrations(db, '/nonexistent/drizzle')).toThrow(/\/nonexistent\/drizzle/);
    db.close();
  });
});

describe('transactions (spec §7 "Transactions")', () => {
  it('an async callback is rejected and its synchronous writes are rolled back', () => {
    const db = openDatabase(':memory:');
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    // @ts-expect-error repository transaction callbacks are typed to return non-Promise values
    expect(() => db.transaction(async () => insertLedgerRow(db))).toThrow(/synchronous/);
    // why: better-sqlite3 runs the callback up to its first await, so the insert happened; the rollback must undo it.
    expect(count(db, 'provider_requests')).toBe(0);
    db.close();
  });
});

describe('SQLite settings on a file database (spec §7, plan Review Focus 5)', () => {
  it('busy_timeout=5000, WAL and foreign keys hold on a real file and on a second handle', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openDatabase(path);
    expect(db.raw.pragma('busy_timeout', { simple: true })).toBe(5000);
    expect(db.raw.pragma('journal_mode', { simple: true })).toBe('wal');
    expect(db.raw.pragma('foreign_keys', { simple: true })).toBe(1);
    const second = openDatabase(path); // the tool bootstrap opens its own handle
    expect(second.raw.pragma('busy_timeout', { simple: true })).toBe(5000);
    second.close();
    db.close();
  });

  it('a write blocked by another connection surfaces SqliteError SQLITE_BUSY, not a hang or a crash', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = openDatabase(path);
    applyMigrations(db, MIGRATIONS_DIR);
    const other = openDatabase(path);
    other.raw.pragma('busy_timeout = 50'); // why: keep the test fast; production uses 5000 (asserted above)
    db.raw.prepare('BEGIN IMMEDIATE').run();
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      insertLedgerRow(other);
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    db.raw.prepare('ROLLBACK').run();
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    expect((thrown as InstanceType<typeof Database.SqliteError>).code).toBe('SQLITE_BUSY');
    other.close();
    db.close();
  });
});
```

`packages/db/src/internal/validate.ts`:

```ts
import { type FxInfo, type ProviderId, type SourceKind, isoDateSchema, isoDateTimeSchema, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';

/**
 * A row a repository refuses to write. Spec §5.5 (provider ⇔ kind, synthetic gate), §6.3 (integers,
 * timestamp form), §7 (FX rules). A rejection is always an error, never a silent fix.
 */
export class RowRejected extends Error {
  readonly rule: string;
  constructor(rule: string, message: string) {
    // why: rejection messages can quote values; spec §5.3 forbids any sink holding a URL query.
    super(stripUrlsInText(`${rule}: ${message}`));
    this.name = 'RowRejected';
    this.rule = rule;
  }
}

/** Integers only for money, counts and rates (spec §6.3 "No float ever holds money"). */
export function assertInt(field: string, v: number, min = 0): void {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(v) || v < min) throw new RowRejected('integer', `${field} must be an integer ≥ ${min}, got ${String(v)}`);
}

export function assertNullableInt(field: string, v: number | null, min = 0): void {
  if (v !== null) assertInt(field, v, min);
}

/** `Date.prototype.toISOString()` form only (spec §6.3). */
export function assertIsoDateTime(field: string, v: string): void {
  if (!isoDateTimeSchema.safeParse(v).success) throw new RowRejected('timestamp', `${field} must be in toISOString() form, got ${v}`);
}

export function assertNullableIsoDateTime(field: string, v: string | null): void {
  if (v !== null) assertIsoDateTime(field, v);
}

export function assertIsoDate(field: string, v: string): void {
  if (!isoDateSchema.safeParse(v).success) throw new RowRejected('date', `${field} must be YYYY-MM-DD, got ${v}`);
}

/** Spec §5.5: `provider_id = 'fixture'` ⇔ `source_kind = 'synthetic'`, and no synthetic row unless allowed. */
export function assertProviderKind(providerId: ProviderId, sourceKind: SourceKind, allowSynthetic: boolean): void {
  if ((providerId === 'fixture') !== (sourceKind === 'synthetic')) {
    throw new RowRejected('provider-kind', `provider ${providerId} with source kind ${sourceKind}: fixture ⇔ synthetic must hold`);
  }
  if (sourceKind === 'synthetic' && !allowSynthetic) throw new RowRejected('synthetic-gate', 'synthetic rows are refused outside syntheticAllowed');
}

/**
 * Spec §7 price-table money rules, enforced before SQL so the error names the rule: integers; ISO
 * 4217 currency; converted ⇔ currency ≠ GBP; converted rows carry rate, date and source;
 * unconverted rows have `price_pence = price_native_minor`.
 */
export function assertPriceMoney(p: { pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo }): void {
  assertInt('price_pence', p.pricePence);
  assertInt('price_native_minor', p.priceNativeMinor);
  if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(p.currencyNative)) throw new RowRejected('currency', `currency_native must be ISO 4217, got ${p.currencyNative}`);
  if (!p.fx.converted) {
    if (p.currencyNative !== 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('fx', `unconverted price in ${p.currencyNative}`);
    if (p.pricePence !== p.priceNativeMinor) throw new RowRejected('fx', `price_pence ${p.pricePence} must equal price_native_minor ${p.priceNativeMinor} when unconverted`);
    return;
  }
  if (p.currencyNative === 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('fx', 'a GBP price cannot be FX-converted');
  assertInt('fx_rate_nano', p.fx.rateNano, 1);
  assertIsoDate('fx_rate_date', p.fx.rateDate);
  if (p.fx.source.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('fx', 'fx_source must be named');
}

/** Point of sale is an ISO alpha-2 code or 'unknown' (spec §3, revision 10). */
export function assertPointOfSale(v: string): void {
  if (v !== 'unknown' && !/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(v)) throw new RowRejected('point-of-sale', `point_of_sale must be ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 or 'unknown', got ${v}`);
}
```

`packages/db/src/schema/checks.ts`:

```ts
import { type SQL, sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

// why: CHECK and partial-index bodies use unqualified column names via sql.raw so the emitted DDL is
// exactly what SQLite accepts in a CHECK or an index WHERE clause. Values are schema constants, never input.

export function isInteger(col: string): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`typeof(${col}) = 'integer' AND ${col} >= 0`);
}
export function isNullOrInteger(col: string): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`${col} IS NULL OR (typeof(${col}) = 'integer' AND ${col} >= 0)`);
}
const ISO_GLOB = "'[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]T[0-9][0-9]:[0-9][0-9]:[0-9][0-9].[0-9][0-9][0-9]Z'";
/** `toISOString()` form, checked in SQL behind the repositories (spec §6.3). */
export function isIsoTimestamp(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} GLOB ${ISO_GLOB}` : `${col} GLOB ${ISO_GLOB}`);
}
export function isOneOf(col: string, values: readonly string[], nullable = false): SQL {
  const list = values.map((v) => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} IN (${list})` : `${col} IN (${list})`);
}
export function isBool(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ${col} IN (0, 1)` : `${col} IN (0, 1)`);
}
export function isPointOfSale(col: string, nullable = false): SQL {
  return sql.raw(`${nullable ? `${col} IS NULL OR ` : ''}${col} = 'unknown' OR ${col} GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z]'`);
}

export const ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL = "'pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'";
export const EXECUTION_STATUSES = ['pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted', 'cancelled', 'completed', 'skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed'] as const;
export const PROVIDER_IDS = ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi', 'fixture'] as const;
export const SOURCE_KINDS = ['authorised-cached', 'scraped-aggregate', 'scraped-search', 'synthetic'] as const;
export const CABINS = ['economy', 'premium_economy', 'business', 'first'] as const;
export const OUTCOME_STATUSES = ['not_run', 'priced', 'empty', 'no_data', 'error', 'skipped'] as const;

/** The CHECKs both price tables carry (spec §7): integer money, the two FX constraints verbatim, timestamp form, explicit max-age. */
export function priceTableChecks(table: string) {
  return [
    check(`${table}_price_ck`, isInteger('price_pence')),
    check(`${table}_native_ck`, isInteger('price_native_minor')),
    check(`${table}_fx_converted_ck`, sql.raw("(fx_converted = 1) = (currency_native <> 'GBP')")),
    check(`${table}_fx_complete_ck`, sql.raw('fx_converted = 0 OR (fx_rate_nano IS NOT NULL AND fx_rate_date IS NOT NULL AND fx_source IS NOT NULL)')),
    check(`${table}_fx_rate_ck`, isNullOrInteger('fx_rate_nano')),
    check(`${table}_fx_bool_ck`, isBool('fx_converted')),
    check(`${table}_currency_ck`, sql.raw("currency_native GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check(`${table}_fetched_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at')),
    check(`${table}_observed_ck`, isIsoTimestamp('provider_observed_at', true)),
    check(`${table}_max_age_ck`, isNullOrInteger('max_age_hours')),
    check(`${table}_cabin_ck`, isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check(`${table}_pos_ck`, isPointOfSale('point_of_sale')),
    check(`${table}_kind_ck`, isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS)),
  ];
}
```

`packages/db/src/schema/searchExecutions.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL, EXECUTION_STATUSES, isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchDefinitions } from './searchDefinitions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_executions` (`trigger`, `created_at`). `created_at` backs `Execution.createdAt` and
 * the boot sweep's "pending older than the boot"; `started_at` is NULL until the run starts.
 * One active parent per definition and one active child per (definition, cell) are partial unique indexes.
 */
export const searchExecutions = sqliteTable(
  'search_executions',
  {
    id: text('id').primaryKey(),
    definitionId: text('definition_id').notNull().references(() => searchDefinitions.id),
    isChild: integer('is_child').notNull().default(0),
    childOrigin: text('child_origin'),
    childDestination: text('child_destination'),
    childDepartureDate: text('child_departure_date'),
    childReturnDate: text('child_return_date'),
    definitionSnapshotJson: text('definition_snapshot_json').notNull(),
    trigger: text('trigger').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    stage: text('stage'),
    estimateJson: text('estimate_json'),
    cancelRequested: integer('cancel_requested').notNull().default(0),
    resumeAfter: text('resume_after'),
    autoResumeCount: integer('auto_resume_count').notNull().default(0),
    createdAt: text('created_at').notNull(),
    startedAt: text('started_at'),
    finishedAt: text('finished_at'),
    errorText: text('error_text'),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('search_executions_status_ck', isOneOf('status', EXECUTION_STATUSES)),
    check('search_executions_trigger_ck', isOneOf('trigger', ['manual', 'schedule', 'tool'])),
    check('search_executions_stage_ck', isOneOf('stage', ['discovery', 'verification'], true)),
    check('search_executions_is_child_ck', isBool('is_child')),
    check('search_executions_cancel_ck', isBool('cancel_requested')),
    check('search_executions_resume_count_ck', isInteger('auto_resume_count')),
    check('search_executions_created_ck', isIsoTimestamp('created_at')),
    check('search_executions_started_ck', isIsoTimestamp('started_at', true)),
    check('search_executions_finished_ck', isIsoTimestamp('finished_at', true)),
    check('search_executions_resume_after_ck', isIsoTimestamp('resume_after', true)),
    // why: a parent never carries a cell; a child always carries all four cell columns.
    check(
      'search_executions_child_cell_ck',
      sql.raw(
        '(is_child = 0 AND child_origin IS NULL AND child_destination IS NULL AND child_departure_date IS NULL AND child_return_date IS NULL) OR ' +
          '(is_child = 1 AND child_origin IS NOT NULL AND child_destination IS NOT NULL AND child_departure_date IS NOT NULL AND child_return_date IS NOT NULL)',
      ),
    ),
    uniqueIndex('search_executions_active_parent_uq').on(t.definitionId).where(sql.raw(`is_child = 0 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`)),
    uniqueIndex('search_executions_active_child_uq')
      .on(t.definitionId, t.childOrigin, t.childDestination, t.childDepartureDate, t.childReturnDate)
      .where(sql.raw(`is_child = 1 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`)),
    index('search_executions_definition_started_idx').on(t.definitionId, t.startedAt),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { PROVIDER_IDS, isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `provider_requests`: the ledger. One row per HTTP attempt (inserted `pending` at reserve) and
 * per cache hit. `endpoint_path` is a path only, never a query (spec §5.3). FX is never ledgered (revision 10).
 */
export const providerRequests = sqliteTable(
  'provider_requests',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    executionId: text('execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    endpointPath: text('endpoint_path').notNull(),
    tileIndex: integer('tile_index'),
    startedAt: text('started_at').notNull(),
    finishedAt: text('finished_at'),
    durationMs: integer('duration_ms'),
    httpStatus: integer('http_status'),
    outcome: text('outcome').notNull(),
    billable: integer('billable'),
    costMicroGbp: integer('cost_micro_gbp').notNull(),
    httpAttempt: integer('http_attempt').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('provider_requests_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('provider_requests_outcome_ck', isOneOf('outcome', ['pending', 'ok', 'error', 'unknown', 'cache_hit'])),
    check('provider_requests_billable_ck', isBool('billable', true)),
    check('provider_requests_cost_ck', isInteger('cost_micro_gbp')),
    check('provider_requests_attempt_ck', isInteger('http_attempt')),
    check('provider_requests_tile_ck', isNullOrInteger('tile_index')),
    check('provider_requests_started_ck', isIsoTimestamp('started_at')),
    check('provider_requests_finished_ck', isIsoTimestamp('finished_at', true)),
    check('provider_requests_path_ck', sql.raw("endpoint_path NOT LIKE '%?%' AND endpoint_path LIKE '/%'")),
    index('provider_requests_provider_idx').on(t.providerId, t.startedAt, t.outcome, t.billable),
    index('provider_requests_execution_idx').on(t.executionId),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/budget/window.ts`:

```ts
import type { IsoDateTime } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { assertInt } from '../internal/validate.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 "Budget windows": a monthly window starts at 00:00 UTC on the billing day (1–28) on or
 * before `now` and ends one month later. Computed in SQL so the bounds the cap predicates use and
 * the bounds `/budget` reports are one expression.
 */
export const MONTH_WINDOW_SQL = `
WITH w AS (
  SELECT CASE WHEN CAST(strftime('%d', @now) AS INTEGER) >= @billingDay
              THEN strftime('%Y-%m-', @now)
              ELSE strftime('%Y-%m-', @now, 'start of month', '-1 month') END
         || printf('%02d', @billingDay) || 'T00:00:00.000Z' AS ws
)
SELECT ws AS windowStart, strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ', ws, '+1 month') AS windowEnd FROM w`;

export function monthWindow(db: Db, now: IsoDateTime, billingDay: number): { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime } {
  assertInt('billingDay', billingDay, 1);
  if (billingDay > 28) throw new RangeError(`billingDay must be 1–28, got ${billingDay}`);
  const row = db.raw.prepare<{ now: string; billingDay: number }, { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime }>(MONTH_WINDOW_SQL).get({ now, billingDay });
  if (row === undefined) throw new Error('month window query returned no row');
  return row;
}
```

`packages/db/src/budgetConfig.ts` (the type in this task; Task 3.3 adds `budgetConfigFromConfig`):

```ts
import type { Config, ProviderId, ProviderLimits } from '@gmt/core';

/**
 * The guard's view of the config (plan "Database" contract). One entry per configured provider:
 * its limits, `trialUsed`, and whether it is billable (spec §5.3 Billable rule: searchapi and fixture iff HTTP 200,
 * travelpayouts never). `spend` carries the global cap and billing day. A provider with no entry
 * cannot be reserved for (fail closed).
 */
export interface BudgetConfig { providers: Partial<Record<ProviderId, ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number }>>; spend: Config['spend']; fixtureCostMicroGbp: number }

/** Spec §12 `SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED` (decision 14): credits spent before this install count against a lifetime window. Monthly windows ignore it. */
export function effectiveCallCap(b: ProviderLimits & { trialUsed: number }): number {
  return b.window === 'lifetime' ? Math.max(0, b.monthlyCallCap - b.trialUsed) : b.monthlyCallCap;
}
```

`packages/db/src/budgetGuard.ts` (Task 3.1 version: provider and global-month predicates; Task 3.2 replaces `checkCaps` and `executionUsage`):

```ts
import { type BudgetGuard, BudgetExceeded, type BudgetScope, type Clock, type IsoDateTime, type ProviderId, type ProviderLimits, type Reservation, type Usage } from '@gmt/core';
import { monthWindow } from './budget/window.js';
import { type BudgetConfig, effectiveCallCap } from './budgetConfig.js';
import type { Db } from './client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt } from './internal/validate.js';

type ProviderBudget = ProviderLimits & { billable: boolean; trialUsed: number };
type ReserveParams = Parameters<BudgetGuard['reserve']>[0];
type CacheHitParams = Parameters<BudgetGuard['recordCacheHit']>[0];
interface CallsAndCost { calls: number; cost: number }
interface Window { windowStart: IsoDateTime | null; windowEnd: IsoDateTime | null }

/** Spec §5.3 cap predicate for one provider: billed vendors count pending/unknown or billable rows; free vendors count every non-cache row. */
export const PROVIDER_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE provider_id = @providerId
  AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (@billed = 0 OR outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)
  AND (@windowStart IS NULL OR (started_at >= @windowStart AND started_at < @windowEnd))`;

/** Spec §5.3 global-month: Σ cost over billed rows of billed providers in the global window (fixed fees added in TS). */
export const GLOBAL_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@billedProviders))
  AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)
  AND started_at >= @windowStart AND started_at < @windowEnd`;

const INSERT_PENDING_SQL = `
INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
VALUES (@providerId, @executionId, @endpointPath, @tileIndex, @startedAt, 'pending', NULL, @cost, @httpAttempt)`;

const FINALISE_SQL = `
UPDATE provider_requests
SET outcome = @outcome, http_status = @httpStatus, duration_ms = @durationMs, finished_at = @finishedAt, billable = @billable,
    cost_micro_gbp = CASE WHEN @zeroCost = 1 THEN 0 ELSE cost_micro_gbp END
WHERE id = @id AND outcome = 'pending'`;

const INSERT_CACHE_HIT_SQL = `
INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, execution_id, endpoint_path, tile_index, started_at, finished_at, duration_ms, http_status, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt)
VALUES (@providerId, @executionId, @endpointPath, @tileIndex, @at, @at, 0, NULL, 'cache_hit', 0, 0, 0)`;

/**
 * The budget guard, enforced in SQL (spec §5.3). `reserve` runs every cap predicate and inserts the
 * `pending` ledger row inside one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction, so two writers can never both pass
 * the same headroom. The billable decision happens at `finalise`.
 */
export class SqliteBudgetGuard implements BudgetGuard {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly config: BudgetConfig,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
  ) {}

  reserve(p: ReserveParams): Reservation {
    const budget = this.budgetFor(p.providerId);
    assertInt('costMicroGbp', p.costMicroGbp);
    assertInt('httpAttempt', p.httpAttempt, 1);
    if (p.tileIndex !== null) assertInt('tileIndex', p.tileIndex);
    if (!budget.billable && p.costMicroGbp !== 0) throw new RowRejected('cost', `${p.providerId} is never billable; cost must be 0`);
    if (!p.endpointPath.startsWith('/') || p.endpointPath.includes('?')) throw new RowRejected('endpoint-path', 'endpoint_path is a path only, without a query');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const run = this.db.raw.transaction(() => {
      this.checkCaps(p, budget, now);
      return this.db.raw
        .prepare(INSERT_PENDING_SQL)
        .run({ providerId: p.providerId, executionId: p.executionId, endpointPath: p.endpointPath, tileIndex: p.tileIndex, startedAt: now, cost: p.costMicroGbp, httpAttempt: p.httpAttempt })
        .lastInsertRowid;
    });
    // why: BEGIN IMMEDIATE takes the write lock before the predicates read (spec §7).
    return this.reservation(Number(run.immediate()), budget);
  }

  recordCacheHit(p: CacheHitParams): void {
    this.budgetFor(p.providerId);
    if (p.tileIndex !== null) assertInt('tileIndex', p.tileIndex);
    this.db.raw.prepare(INSERT_CACHE_HIT_SQL).run({ providerId: p.providerId, executionId: p.executionId, endpointPath: p.endpointPath, tileIndex: p.tileIndex, at: this.clock.now() });
  }

  usage(scope: BudgetScope): Usage {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    if (scope.kind === 'global-month') {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      return { calls: g.calls, costMicroGbp: g.cost, cap: this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp, windowStart: g.windowStart, windowEnd: g.windowEnd };
    }
    if (scope.kind === 'provider-month' || scope.kind === 'provider-lifetime') {
      const budget = this.budgetFor(scope.providerId);
      const asked = scope.kind === 'provider-lifetime' ? 'lifetime' : 'month';
      const u = this.providerUsage(scope.providerId, budget, now, asked);
      // why: plan Budget port "cap is null for a window the plan does not use".
      return { calls: u.calls, costMicroGbp: u.cost, cap: asked === budget.window ? effectiveCallCap(budget) : null, windowStart: u.windowStart, windowEnd: u.windowEnd };
    }
    return this.executionUsage(scope.kind, scope.executionId);
  }

  // ---- predicates ------------------------------------------------------------------------

  protected checkCaps(p: ReserveParams, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime): void {
    const prov = this.providerUsage(p.providerId, budget, now, budget.window);
    const callCap = effectiveCallCap(budget);
    if (prov.calls + 1 > callCap) {
      throw this.exceeded(budget.window === 'lifetime' ? 'provider-lifetime' : 'provider-month', callCap, prov.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
    if (budget.billable) {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      const cap = this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp;
      // why: spec §5.3 "A cap of 0 refuses when current + this > 0; zero-cost calls pass."
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && g.cost + p.costMicroGbp > cap) throw this.exceeded('global-month', cap, g.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    }
  }

  protected providerUsage(providerId: ProviderId, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime, window: 'month' | 'lifetime'): CallsAndCost & Window {
    const w: Window = window === 'lifetime' ? { windowStart: null, windowEnd: null } : monthWindow(this.db, now, budget.billingDay);
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(PROVIDER_USAGE_SQL).get({ providerId, billed: budget.billable ? 1 : 0, ...w });
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: row?.cost ?? 0, ...w };
  }

  protected globalUsage(now: IsoDateTime): CallsAndCost & { windowStart: IsoDateTime; windowEnd: IsoDateTime } {
    const w = monthWindow(this.db, now, this.config.spend.globalBillingDay);
    const billed = this.providersWhere(true);
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(GLOBAL_USAGE_SQL).get({ billedProviders: JSON.stringify(billed), ...w });
    // why: spec §5.3 the subscription fee is counted once per enabled subscription provider, not per row.
    const fixed = billed.reduce((sum, id) => sum + (this.config.providers[id]?.planFixedMicroGbp ?? 0), 0);
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: (row?.cost ?? 0) + fixed, ...w };
  }

  protected executionUsage(kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost', executionId: string): Usage {
    // why: Task 3.1 enforces provider and global scopes only; Task 3.2 replaces this method.
    throw new RangeError(`scope ${kind} is not supported (execution ${executionId})`);
  }

  protected providersWhere(billable: boolean): ProviderId[] {
    return (Object.keys(this.config.providers) as ProviderId[]).filter((id) => this.config.providers[id]?.billable === billable).sort();
  }

  protected budgetFor(providerId: ProviderId): ProviderBudget {
    const b = this.config.providers[providerId];
    // why: fail closed; a provider with no budget entry never reaches the network.
    if (b === undefined) throw new Error(`provider ${providerId} has no budget configuration`);
    return b;
  }

  protected exceeded(scope: BudgetExceeded['scope'], cap: number, current: number, requested: number, providerId: ProviderId): BudgetExceeded {
    return new BudgetExceeded({ scope, cap, current, requested, providerId });
  }

  private reservation(id: number, budget: ProviderBudget): Reservation {
    let done = false;
    return {
      id: String(id),
      finalise: (r) => {
        if (done) throw new Error(`reservation ${id} already finalised`);
        assertInt('durationMs', r.durationMs);
        // spec §5.3 Billable rule: billable providers iff upstream HTTP 200; free providers never.
        const billable = budget.billable && r.outcome === 'ok' && r.httpStatus === 200;
        // why: an `unknown` attempt may have been billed by the vendor, so it keeps its cost and stays counted.
        const zeroCost = !billable && r.outcome !== 'unknown';
        const changes = this.db.raw.prepare(FINALISE_SQL).run({
          id, outcome: r.outcome, httpStatus: r.httpStatus ?? null, durationMs: r.durationMs, finishedAt: this.clock.now(), billable: billable ? 1 : 0, zeroCost: zeroCost ? 1 : 0,
        }).changes;
        if (changes !== 1) throw new Error(`reservation ${id} is no longer pending`);
        done = true;
      },
    };
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/types.ts` (complete in this task; every name is the plan's except where a comment says otherwise):

```ts
import type {
  Cabin, Cell, CellKey, CellOutcome, DefinitionSnapshot, Estimate, Execution, ExecutionStatus, FxInfo, Iata, IsoDateTime, NormalisedItinerary,
  Observation, ObservationFields, Offer, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, SourceKind, SuppliedField,
} from '@gmt/core';

/** Spec §7 `airports` row. */
export interface Airport { iata: Iata; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; datasetVersion: string }
/** The vendored airports dataset plus the attributed metro-code list (decision 11). */
export interface AirportsDataset { version: string; airports: Array<Omit<Airport, 'source' | 'datasetVersion'>>; metros: Record<string, string[]> }

/** Repositories refuse synthetic rows unless this is true (`syntheticAllowed(config)`, spec §5.5). */
export interface RepoOptions { allowSynthetic: boolean }

export type ExecutionCreateInput = { definitionId: string; isChild: boolean; cell: CellKey | null; snapshot: DefinitionSnapshot; estimate: Estimate | null; trigger: Execution['trigger']; status: 'pending' | 'running' | (ExecutionStatus & `skipped_${string}`) };
export type ExecutionPatch = Partial<Pick<Execution, 'stage' | 'resumeAfter' | 'errorText' | 'startedAt' | 'finishedAt' | 'cancelRequested' | 'estimate' | 'snapshot'>>;

/** One discovery tile's write (spec §7 atomic unit). `cells` are the tile's covered cells; `discards` is reported by the engine and not stored. */
export interface TileWrite {
  tileIndex: number; cells: CellKey[]; prices: CalendarPriceInsert[];
  outcomes: Array<Pick<CellOutcome, 'origin' | 'destination' | 'departureDate' | 'returnDate' | 'status' | 'reason' | 'lastError' | 'fetchedAt' | 'minPence' | 'cabin' | 'pointOfSale' | 'sourceKind'>>;
  discards: number;
}
/** A calendar price to write; `maxAgeHours: null` means unknown and is written explicitly (spec §7). */
export interface CalendarPriceInsert {
  providerId: ProviderId; sourceKind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; cell: CellKey; pricePence: number; priceNativeMinor: number; currencyNative: string; fx: FxInfo;
  pointOfSale: PointOfSale; fetchedAt: IsoDateTime; providerObservedAt: IsoDateTime | null; maxAgeHours: number | null; vendorLink: string | null; reusedFromExecutionId: string | null;
}
export interface CalendarPriceRow extends CalendarPriceInsert { id: number; executionId: string; provenance: Provenance }
/** One verified cell's write (spec §7 atomic unit). */
export interface VerifiedCellWrite {
  cell: CellKey; outcome: TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
  itineraries: Array<{ itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; offers: Array<{ offer: Offer; observation: ObservationFields & { provenance: Provenance; suppliedFields: SuppliedField[]; partialExpansion: boolean } }> }>;
  bestObservationSelector: 'cheapest-verified-or-partial';
}
/** A reuse source for one cell (spec §8.1 reuse). `price` is null for empty and no_data outcomes. */
export interface ReusedCopy { cell: CellKey; outcome: CellOutcome; price: CalendarPriceRow | null }
export interface ObservationRow extends Observation { offer: Offer & { id: number }; itinerary: NormalisedItinerary; cell: Cell; seller: string }
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { type CellKey, type ExecutionBudget, asIata, asIsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../../src/client.js';
import { T0 } from './db.js';

// The worked example only (brief rules): London → New York.
export const LHR = asIata('LHR');
export const LGW = asIata('LGW');
export const JFK = asIata('JFK');
export const EWR = asIata('EWR');

export function cell(origin = LHR, destination = JFK, departureDate = '2027-03-14', returnDate = '2027-03-28'): CellKey {
  return { origin, destination, departureDate: asIsoDate(departureDate), returnDate: asIsoDate(returnDate) };
}

/**
 * Inserts a definition and an execution with raw SQL, the snapshot holding only the budget the guard
 * reads (plan Budget port: "Per-execution caps come from the execution's snapshot budget").
 */
export function rawExecution(db: Db, budget: ExecutionBudget, opts: { isChild?: boolean } = {}): { definitionId: string; executionId: string } {
  const definitionId = randomUUID();
  const executionId = randomUUID();
  db.raw
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_definitions (id, name, origins_json, destinations_json, window_json, min_nights, max_nights, cabin, constraints_json,
        verification_policy_json, budget_json, providers_json, schedule_tz, enabled, created_at, updated_at)
       VALUES (@id, 'London to New York', '["LHR","LGW"]', '["JFK","EWR"]', '{"kind":"relative","startOffsetDays":7,"lengthDays":182}', 10, 21, 'economy', '{}',
        '{"maxCells":10,"topN":10,"alwaysInclude":[],"expandTop":2,"bookingLinks":true}', @budget, '{"discovery":[],"verification":[],"discoveryRefreshHours":{}}', 'Europe/London', 1, @at, @at)`,
    )
    .run({ id: definitionId, budget: JSON.stringify(budget), at: T0 });
  const c = cell();
  db.raw
    .prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, is_child, child_origin, child_destination, child_departure_date, child_return_date,
        definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, created_at, started_at)
       VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @isChild, @o, @d, @dep, @ret, @snapshot, 'manual', 'running', @at, @at)`,
    )
    .run({
      id: executionId, definitionId, isChild: opts.isChild ? 1 : 0,
      o: opts.isChild ? c.origin : null, d: opts.isChild ? c.destination : null, dep: opts.isChild ? c.departureDate : null, ret: opts.isChild ? c.returnDate : null,
      snapshot: JSON.stringify({ budget }), at: T0,
    });
  return { definitionId, executionId };
}
```
