`packages/db/test/budgetGuard.predicates.test.ts`:

```ts
import { BudgetExceeded, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits, rawExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const sub40 = limits({ planFixedMicroGbp: 32_000_000 });
const perCall = limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 });
const tp = limits({ billable: false, monthlyCallCap: 50_000 });
const trial = limits({ window: 'lifetime', monthlyCallCap: 100, trialUsed: 9 }); // effective lifetime cap 91
const req = (providerId: 'searchapi' | 'travelpayouts', executionId: string | null, costMicroGbp = 0) => ({ providerId, executionId, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/api/v1/search' });
const ok = { outcome: 'ok' as const, httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 };

describe('cap predicates (spec §5.3)', () => {
  it('pending and unknown rows count against a billed provider; error rows do not; ok HTTP 200 is billable', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ monthlyCallCap: 3 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)); // left pending (crash between reserve and finalise)
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'unknown', durationMs: 60_000 });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise({ outcome: 'error', httpStatus: 500, durationMs: 5 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' }).calls).toBe(2);
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' }).calls).toBe(3);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 3200))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'provider-month', cap: 3, current: 3 }));
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp AS cost FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([
      { outcome: 'pending', billable: null, cost: 3200 },
      { outcome: 'unknown', billable: 0, cost: 3200 },
      { outcome: 'error', billable: 0, cost: 0 },
      { outcome: 'ok', billable: 1, cost: 3200 },
    ]);
  });

  it('free vendors count every non-cache row, including errors, and never carry cost', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ travelpayouts: limits({ billable: false, monthlyCallCap: 2 }) }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null)).finalise({ outcome: 'error', httpStatus: 500, durationMs: 1 });
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 0, endpointPath: '/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates' });
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'provider-month', current: 2 }));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null, 1))).toThrow(/never billable/);
  });

  it('billed vs free execution scopes are counted separately, from the snapshot budget', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 2, maxFreeCalls: 3, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000, executionMaxCalls: 2000, executionMaxFreeCalls: 2000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', executionId)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', executionId))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-free-calls', cap: 3, current: 3 }));
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i++) guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise(ok); // free calls did not consume the billed allowance
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-calls', cap: 2, current: 2 }));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-calls', executionId })).toMatchObject({ calls: 2, cap: 2, windowStart: null });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-free-calls', executionId })).toMatchObject({ calls: 3, cap: 3 });
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'execution-cost', executionId })).toMatchObject({ costMicroGbp: 0, cap: 0 });
  });

  it('a child execution is capped by its own snapshot budget (the engine writes CHILD_MAX_CALLS there)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 6, maxFreeCalls: 0, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 }, { isChild: true });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', executionId))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-calls', cap: 6 }));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', executionId))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-free-calls', cap: 0 }));
  });

  it('execution-cost: zero-cost calls pass a 0 cap; a 1 µGBP call does not; 6,400 allows two 3,200 calls', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const zero = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 }).executionId;
    const some = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 6_400 }).executionId;
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: perCall }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', zero, 0)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', zero, 1))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-cost', cap: 0, current: 0, requested: 1 }));
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', some, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', some, 3200)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', some, 3200))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'execution-cost', current: 6_400 }));
  });

  it('global cap includes the subscription fee once; zero-cost calls pass even when the fee alone exceeds the cap', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40, travelpayouts: tp }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 3_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 0, costMicroGbp: 32_000_000, cap: 3_000_000 });
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 0)).finalise(ok);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'global-month' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 5, costMicroGbp: 32_000_000 });
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null, 1))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'global-month', current: 32_000_000, requested: 1 }));
  });

  it('a call cap of 0 refuses the first call', () => {
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(freshDb(), budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits({ monthlyCallCap: 0 }) }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null))).toThrow(BudgetExceeded);
  });

  it('lifetime window: every row ever counts; provider-lifetime throws; the month scope reports cap null', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const insert = db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO provider_requests (provider_id, endpoint_path, started_at, finished_at, outcome, billable, cost_micro_gbp, http_attempt, http_status, duration_ms)
       VALUES ('searchapi', '/api/v1/search', @at, @at, 'ok', 1, 0, 1, 200, 1)`,
    );
    for (const month of ['2026-08', '2026-09', '2026-10']) for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) insert.run({ at: `${month}-02T00:00:00.000Z` });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ searchapi: trial }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-lifetime', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toEqual({ calls: 90, costMicroGbp: 0, cap: 91, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null });
    guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null)).finalise(ok);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ scope: 'provider-lifetime', cap: 91, current: 91 }));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 31, cap: null, windowStart: '2026-10-01T00:00:00.000Z' });
  });

  it('a monthly provider reports cap null for the lifetime scope', () => {
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(freshDb(), budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-lifetime', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toEqual({ calls: 0, costMicroGbp: 0, cap: null, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null });
  });

  it('fails closed: unconfigured provider, unknown execution, double finalise, query in the path', () => {
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(freshDb(), budgetConfig({ searchapi: sub40 }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 35_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null))).toThrow(/no budget configuration/);
    expect(() => guard.reserve(req('searchapi', 'no-such-execution'))).toThrow(/no-such-execution/);
    expect(() => guard.reserve({ ...req('searchapi', null), endpointPath: '/x?api_key=SECRET' })).toThrow(/path only/);
    const r = guard.reserve(req('searchapi', null));
    r.finalise(ok);
    expect(() => r.finalise(ok)).toThrow(/already finalised/);
  });

  it('every ledger row carries tile_index as given, NULL for verification (spec §5.3)', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ travelpayouts: tp }), fixedClock(T0));
    guard.reserve({ ...req('travelpayouts', null), tileIndex: 4 }).finalise(ok);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 5, endpointPath: '/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates' });
    guard.reserve(req('travelpayouts', null)).finalise(ok);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT tile_index AS t FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ t: 4 }, { t: 5 }, { t: null }]);
  });
});
```

In `src/budgetGuard.ts`, add the SQL and replace `checkCaps` and `executionUsage`:

```ts
/** Spec §5.3 execution scopes: rows of this execution for the given providers, billed or free rule. */
export const EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL = `
SELECT COUNT(*) AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost
FROM provider_requests
WHERE execution_id = @executionId
  AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@providers))
  AND outcome <> 'cache_hit'
  AND (@billed = 0 OR outcome IN ('pending', 'unknown') OR billable = 1)`;

/** Plan Budget port: per-execution caps come from the execution's snapshot budget, never from config at reserve time. */
const EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL = `
SELECT json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCalls') AS maxCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxFreeCalls') AS maxFreeCalls,
       json_extract(definition_snapshot_json, '$.budget.maxCostMicroGbp') AS maxCostMicroGbp
FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?`;
```

```ts
  // why: check order is provider (the vendor's hard stop), then global (total spend), then the execution's
  // own caps; one breach is reported and the engine's error table (spec §8.1) acts on its scope.
  protected checkCaps(p: ReserveParams, budget: ProviderBudget, now: IsoDateTime): void {
    const prov = this.providerUsage(p.providerId, budget, now, budget.window);
    const callCap = effectiveCallCap(budget);
    if (prov.calls + 1 > callCap) {
      throw this.exceeded(budget.window === 'lifetime' ? 'provider-lifetime' : 'provider-month', callCap, prov.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
    if (budget.billable) {
      const g = this.globalUsage(now);
      const cap = this.config.spend.monthlyCapMicroGbp;
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && g.cost + p.costMicroGbp > cap) throw this.exceeded('global-month', cap, g.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    }
    if (p.executionId === null) return;
    const caps = this.executionCaps(p.executionId);
    if (budget.billable) {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.providersWhere(true), true);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-calls', caps.maxCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
      if (p.costMicroGbp > 0 && u.cost + p.costMicroGbp > caps.maxCostMicroGbp) throw this.exceeded('execution-cost', caps.maxCostMicroGbp, u.cost, p.costMicroGbp, p.providerId);
    } else {
      const u = this.executionRows(p.executionId, this.providersWhere(false), false);
      if (u.calls + 1 > caps.maxFreeCalls) throw this.exceeded('execution-free-calls', caps.maxFreeCalls, u.calls, 1, p.providerId);
    }
  }

  protected executionUsage(kind: 'execution-calls' | 'execution-free-calls' | 'execution-cost', executionId: string): Usage {
    const caps = this.executionCaps(executionId);
    const billed = kind !== 'execution-free-calls';
    const u = this.executionRows(executionId, this.providersWhere(billed), billed);
    const cap = kind === 'execution-calls' ? caps.maxCalls : kind === 'execution-free-calls' ? caps.maxFreeCalls : caps.maxCostMicroGbp;
    return { calls: u.calls, costMicroGbp: u.cost, cap, windowStart: null, windowEnd: null };
  }

  private executionRows(executionId: string, providers: ProviderId[], billed: boolean): CallsAndCost {
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CallsAndCost>(EXECUTION_USAGE_SQL).get({ executionId, providers: JSON.stringify(providers), billed: billed ? 1 : 0 });
    return { calls: row?.calls ?? 0, cost: row?.cost ?? 0 };
  }

  private executionCaps(executionId: string): ExecutionBudget {
    const row = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { maxCalls: number | null; maxFreeCalls: number | null; maxCostMicroGbp: number | null }>(EXECUTION_CAPS_SQL).get(executionId);
    if (row === undefined) throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} does not exist; refusing to reserve`);
    if (row.maxCalls === null || row.maxFreeCalls === null || row.maxCostMicroGbp === null) throw new Error(`execution ${executionId} snapshot has no budget; refusing to reserve`);
    return { maxCalls: row.maxCalls, maxFreeCalls: row.maxFreeCalls, maxCostMicroGbp: row.maxCostMicroGbp };
  }
```

```ts
    ['2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 15, '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z'],
    ['2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z', 15, '2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-11-15T00:00:00.000Z'],
    ['2026-10-14T23:59:59.999Z', 15, '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z', '2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z'],
    ['2027-01-03T08:00:00.000Z', 28, '2026-12-28T00:00:00.000Z', '2027-01-28T00:00:00.000Z'],
    ['2028-02-29T10:00:00.000Z', 28, '2028-02-28T00:00:00.000Z', '2028-03-28T00:00:00.000Z'],
    ['2027-03-01T00:00:00.000Z', 28, '2027-02-28T00:00:00.000Z', '2027-03-28T00:00:00.000Z'],
  ])('now %s, billing day %i → [%s, %s)', (now, day, start, end) => {

    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toEqual({ calls: 2, costMicroGbp: 0, cap: 10, windowStart: '2026-09-15T00:00:00.000Z', windowEnd: '2026-10-15T00:00:00.000Z' });

  it('trial: lifetime window carrying SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED; the effective cap is the allowance left', () => {
    const b = budgetConfigFromConfig(parse({ ...base, SEARCHAPI_KEY: 'test-searchapi-key-0123456789abcdef', SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED: '9' }));
    expect(b.providers.searchapi).toMatchObject({ window: 'lifetime', monthlyCallCap: 100, trialUsed: 9, billable: true });
    const over = budgetConfigFromConfig(parse({ ...base, SEARCHAPI_KEY: 'test-searchapi-key-0123456789abcdef', SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED: '150' })).providers.searchapi;
    if (over === undefined) throw new Error('fixture');
    expect(effectiveCallCap(over)).toBe(0);
    expect(effectiveCallCap(limits({ window: 'month', monthlyCallCap: 10, trialUsed: 9 }))).toBe(10); // monthly windows ignore it
  });

  it('fixture appears only under syntheticAllowed, billable at the configured test cost', () => {
    const b = budgetConfigFromConfig(parse({ ...base, FIXTURE_COST_MICRO_GBP: '3200' }));
    expect(b.providers.fixture).toMatchObject({ billable: true, costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 });
    expect(b.fixtureCostMicroGbp).toBe(3200);
    const prod = budgetConfigFromConfig(parse({ ...base, NODE_ENV: 'production', DATABASE_PATH: '/data/get-me-there.db' }));
    expect(prod.providers.fixture).toBeUndefined();
    expect(prod.fixtureCostMicroGbp).toBe(0);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Append to `packages/db/src/budgetConfig.ts` (import `syntheticAllowed` as a value from `@gmt/core`; `effectiveCallCap` is already there from Task 3.1):

```ts
/** The fixture provider's guard entry: billable at the configured test cost, a large but finite call cap (tests only). */
const FIXTURE_LIMITS: ProviderLimits = {
  monthlyCallCap: 100_000, window: 'month', billingDay: 1, ratePerMinute: 6000, ratePerHour: 100_000, concurrency: 4, cacheTtlHours: 1,
  costPerCallMicroGbp: 0, planFixedMicroGbp: 0, excludedFromSchedule: false,
};

/**
 * Builds the guard's configuration from the parsed config (spec §12). Only configured providers
 * appear. `trialUsed` carries `SEARCHAPI_TRIAL_USED` for a lifetime (trial) plan; the guard's
 * effective lifetime cap is `monthlyCallCap − trialUsed` (decision 14).
 */
export function budgetConfigFromConfig(c: Config): BudgetConfig {
  const providers: BudgetConfig['providers'] = {};
  const tp = c.providers.travelpayouts;
  if (tp !== null) providers.travelpayouts = { ...tp.limits, billable: false, trialUsed: 0 };
  const sa = c.providers.searchapi;
  if (sa !== null) providers.searchapi = { ...sa.limits, billable: true, trialUsed: sa.limits.window === 'lifetime' ? sa.trialUsed : 0 };
  const fixture = c.providers.fixture;
  if (fixture !== null && syntheticAllowed(c)) providers.fixture = { ...FIXTURE_LIMITS, costPerCallMicroGbp: fixture.costMicroGbp, billable: true, trialUsed: 0 };
  return { providers, spend: c.spend, fixtureCostMicroGbp: fixture?.costMicroGbp ?? 0 };
}
```

**Interfaces:**

`// why:` the ledger has no stage column; a row's stage is derived from `tile_index` (spec §5.3 "tile_index (NULL for verification)"): non-NULL → `discovery`, NULL → `verification`. `stage: null` is reserved for rows with no execution, which `countsFor` never returns.

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { SqliteBudgetGuard } from '../src/budgetGuard.js';
import { LedgerRepo } from '../src/repositories/ledger.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { budgetConfig, limits, rawExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const cfg = budgetConfig({ searchapi: limits(), travelpayouts: limits({ billable: false, monthlyCallCap: 50_000 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 });
const ok = { outcome: 'ok' as const, httpStatus: 200, durationMs: 1 };
const repo = (db: ReturnType<typeof freshDb>, clock = fixedClock(T0)) => new LedgerRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });

describe('LedgerRepo (spec §5.3, §7, §8.1 parity)', () => {
  it('sweepPending: pending rows older than the cutoff become unknown and stay counted', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:00, left pending
    clock.advance(9 * 60_000);
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }); // 12:09
    clock.advance(2 * 60_000); // boot at 12:11
    expect(repo(db, clock).sweepPending('2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime)).toBe(1); // boot − 10 min
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT outcome FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ outcome: 'unknown' }, { outcome: 'pending' }]);
    expect(guard.usage({ kind: 'provider-month', providerId: 'searchapi' })).toMatchObject({ calls: 2, costMicroGbp: 6400 });
  });

  it('countsFor: totals and per (provider, stage); tiles = distinct tile_index over first attempts and cache hits', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const { executionId } = rawExecution(db, { maxCalls: 100, maxFreeCalls: 100, maxCostMicroGbp: 100_000 });
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, budgetConfig({ ...cfg.providers, searchapi: limits({ costPerCallMicroGbp: 3200 }) }, { monthlyCapMicroGbp: 1_000_000_000 }), fixedClock(T0));
    const tp = (tileIndex: number, httpAttempt: number) => guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, tileIndex, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise(ok);
    tp(0, 1); tp(0, 1); // tile 0, two pages
    tp(1, 1); tp(1, 2); // tile 1, one retry
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId, tileIndex: 2, endpointPath: '/p' });
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId, tileIndex: null, costMicroGbp: 3200, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/s' }).finalise(ok);
    const c = repo(db).countsFor(executionId);
    expect({ ...c, perProvider: undefined }).toEqual({ calls: 5, costMicroGbp: 3200, tiles: 3, cacheHits: 1, perProvider: undefined });
    expect(c.perProvider).toEqual([
      { providerId: 'searchapi', stage: 'verification', calls: 1, costMicroGbp: 3200, tiles: 0, cacheHits: 0 },
      { providerId: 'travelpayouts', stage: 'discovery', calls: 4, costMicroGbp: 0, tiles: 3, cacheHits: 1 },
    ]);
    expect(repo(db).firstAttemptTiles(executionId, 'travelpayouts')).toBe(3);
    expect(repo(db).firstAttemptTiles(executionId, 'searchapi')).toBe(0);
    expect(repo(db).countsFor('none')).toEqual({ calls: 0, costMicroGbp: 0, tiles: 0, cacheHits: 0, perProvider: [] });
  });

  it('pruneCacheHits removes only cache_hit rows older than the cutoff for the named providers', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock('2026-06-01T00:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime);
    const guard = new SqliteBudgetGuard(db, cfg, clock);
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 0, endpointPath: '/p' });
    guard.recordCacheHit({ providerId: 'searchapi', executionId: null, tileIndex: null, endpointPath: '/s' });
    guard.reserve({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', executionId: null, tileIndex: 0, costMicroGbp: 0, httpAttempt: 1, endpointPath: '/p' }).finalise(ok);
    expect(repo(db, clock).pruneCacheHits(T0, ['travelpayouts'])).toBe(1);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT provider_id AS p, outcome AS o FROM provider_requests ORDER BY id').all()).toEqual([{ p: 'searchapi', o: 'cache_hit' }, { p: 'travelpayouts', o: 'ok' }]);
    expect(repo(db, clock).pruneCacheHits(T0, [])).toBe(0);
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDateTime, ProviderId, Stage } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { assertIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface PerProviderDbRow { providerId: ProviderId; stage: Stage; calls: number; cost: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }

/** Distinct tile requests: first-attempt rows and cache hits with a tile index (spec §8.1 parity). */
const TILE_EXPR = `COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN tile_index IS NOT NULL AND (http_attempt = 1 OR outcome = 'cache_hit') THEN provider_id || ':' || tile_index END)`;

/**
 * Reads and housekeeping over `provider_requests` (spec §7). Inserts and finalisation belong to
 * `SqliteBudgetGuard`; nothing else writes the ledger except the boot sweep and retention here.
 */
export class LedgerRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: the uniform repository constructor (plan contract); cutoffs are passed in by the caller.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Actuals for one execution, total and per (provider, stage) (spec §8.1 "Actuals come from the ledger by execution_id"). */
  countsFor(executionId: string): { calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number; perProvider: Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; stage: Stage | null; calls: number; costMicroGbp: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number }> } {
    const total = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { calls: number | null; cost: number; tiles: number; cacheHits: number | null }>(
        `SELECT SUM(outcome <> 'cache_hit') AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost, ${TILE_EXPR} AS tiles, SUM(outcome = 'cache_hit') AS cacheHits
         FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ?`,
      )
      .get(executionId);
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], PerProviderDbRow>(
        `SELECT provider_id AS providerId, CASE WHEN tile_index IS NULL THEN 'verification' ELSE 'discovery' END AS stage,
                SUM(outcome <> 'cache_hit') AS calls, COALESCE(SUM(cost_micro_gbp), 0) AS cost, ${TILE_EXPR} AS tiles, SUM(outcome = 'cache_hit') AS cacheHits
         FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ? GROUP BY providerId, stage ORDER BY providerId, stage`,
      )
      .all(executionId);
    return {
      calls: total?.calls ?? 0, costMicroGbp: total?.cost ?? 0, tiles: total?.tiles ?? 0, cacheHits: total?.cacheHits ?? 0,
      perProvider: rows.map((r) => ({ providerId: r.providerId, stage: r.stage, calls: r.calls, costMicroGbp: r.cost, tiles: r.tiles, cacheHits: r.cacheHits })),
    };
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 parity numerator for one provider: distinct tile requests in this execution. */
  firstAttemptTiles(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId): number {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string, string], { n: number }>(`SELECT ${TILE_EXPR} AS n FROM provider_requests WHERE execution_id = ? AND provider_id = ?`).get(executionId, providerId)?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** Spec §5.3 "boot sweep marks pending older than 10 minutes unknown (still counted)". The caller passes boot − 10 min. */
  sweepPending(olderThan: IsoDateTime): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    return this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE provider_requests SET outcome = 'unknown' WHERE outcome = 'pending' AND started_at < ?`).run(olderThan).changes;
  }

  /** Spec §7 retention: prune `cache_hit` rows older than the cutoff for the named (monthly-window) providers. */
  pruneCacheHits(olderThan: IsoDateTime, providerIds: ProviderId[]): number {
    assertIsoDateTime('olderThan', olderThan);
    if (providerIds.length === 0) return 0;
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare(`DELETE FROM provider_requests WHERE outcome = 'cache_hit' AND started_at < @olderThan AND provider_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@ids))`)
      .run({ olderThan, ids: JSON.stringify(providerIds) }).changes;
  }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `AirportsRepo`, `seedAirports`, `loadAirportsDataset`, `sanityCheckAirports`, `AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS`, `Airport`, `AirportsDataset`.

Data layout (decision 11): `airports.<version>.json` holds `{ version, source, licence, airports: [{ iata, icao, name, city, country, tz, lat, lon }] }` built from mwgg/Airports; `metros.json` holds `{ source, revision, licence, retrieved, metros: { "LON": ["LCY", …], … } }` built from the Wikipedia revision in section 6. `loadAirportsDataset(dir)` reads both, sets each airport's `metro` from the list, and reports the dataset version as `<airports version>+metros-<revision>` so a change to either file re-seeds.

```ts
import { existsSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { asIata, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR } from '../src/paths.js';
import { AirportsRepo } from '../src/repositories/airports.js';

import type { AirportsDataset } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, JFK, LGW, LHR } from './helpers/factories.js';

type Row = AirportsDataset['airports'][number];
const A = 'A'.charCodeAt(0);
/** Synthetic IATA-shaped codes AAA, AAB, …; their names say they are synthetic. */
const syntheticCode = (i: number) => String.fromCharCode(A + Math.floor(i / 676), A + (Math.floor(i / 26) % 26), A + (i % 26));
const row = (iata: string, over: Partial<Row> = {}): Row => ({ iata: asIata(iata), icao: null, name: `Synthetic ${iata}`, city: 'Synthetic City', country: 'ZZ', tz: 'UTC', lat: 0, lon: 0, metro: null, ...over });
const dataset = (airports: Row[], version = 'synthetic-1', metros: Record<string, string[]> = {}): AirportsDataset => ({ version, airports, metros });
const many = (n: number, version?: string) => dataset(Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => row(syntheticCode(i))), version);
const repoOf = (db: ReturnType<typeof freshDb>) => new AirportsRepo(db, fixedClock(T0), { allowSynthetic: false });

afterEach(() => vi.unstubAllGlobals());

describe('airports seed and sanity check (spec §7 airports)', () => {
  it(`passes with ${AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS} rows carrying IATA and tz and fails with one fewer`, () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    seedAirports(db, many(AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS - 1));
    expect(sanityCheckAirports(db, [])).toMatchObject({ ok: false, reason: expect.stringContaining('5000') });
    seedAirports(db, many(AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS, 'synthetic-2'));
    expect(sanityCheckAirports(db, [asIata('AAA')])).toEqual({ ok: true });
  });

  it('fails naming every referenced airport that is missing', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    seedAirports(db, many(AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS));
    expect(sanityCheckAirports(db, [asIata('AAA'), asIata('ZZZ'), asIata('ZZY'), asIata('ZZZ')])).toEqual({ ok: false, reason: 'airports referenced by enabled definitions are missing: ZZY, ZZZ' });
  });

  it('re-seeding the same version inserts nothing; a new version upserts by IATA and applies its metro list', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const rows = [row('AAA'), row('AAB'), row('AAC')];
    expect(seedAirports(db, dataset(rows, 'v1'))).toEqual({ inserted: 3, datasetVersion: 'v1' });
    expect(seedAirports(db, dataset(rows, 'v1'))).toEqual({ inserted: 0, datasetVersion: 'v1' });
    expect(seedAirports(db, dataset(rows, 'v2', { AAX: ['AAA', 'AAB', 'QQQ'] }))).toEqual({ inserted: 3, datasetVersion: 'v2' });
    const repo = repoOf(db);
    expect(repo.get('AAA')).toMatchObject({ datasetVersion: 'v2', metro: 'AAX' });
    expect(repo.get('AAC')?.metro).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.datasetVersion()).toBe('v2');
    expect(repo.count()).toBe(3);
  });

  it('the vendored data loads without network, carries the worked example and its metro codes, and passes the sanity check', () => {
    vi.stubGlobal('fetch', () => {
      throw new Error('no network at seed (spec §7)');
    });
    const ds = loadAirportsDataset(AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR);
    expect(ds.version).toBe('2026-10-01-a89e4e0+metros-1378312633');
    expect(ds.airports.filter((a) => /^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(a.iata) && a.tz.length > 0).length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS);
    expect(ds.metros.LON).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['LHR', 'LGW']));
    const db = freshDb();
    seedAirports(db, ds);
    expect(sanityCheckAirports(db, [LHR, LGW, JFK, EWR])).toEqual({ ok: true });
    const repo = repoOf(db);
    expect(repo.get('LHR')).toMatchObject({ icao: 'EGLL', country: 'GB', tz: 'Europe/London', metro: 'LON', source: expect.stringContaining('mwgg/Airports') });
    expect(repo.tzOf('EWR')).toBe('America/New_York');
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([LHR, LGW])).toBe('LON');
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([JFK, EWR])).toBe('NYC');
  });

  it('the licence and attribution files sit beside the data', () => {
    expect(readFileSync(join(AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR, 'airports.LICENSE'), 'utf8')).toMatch(/MIT License[\s\S]*mwgg/);
    const attribution = readFileSync(join(AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR, 'metros.ATTRIBUTION'), 'utf8');
    expect(attribution).toContain('CC BY-SA 4.0');
    expect(attribution).toContain('1378312633');
    expect(existsSync(join(AIRPORTS_DATA_DIR, 'metros.json'))).toBe(true);
  });

  it('a metro list that puts one airport in two metros is refused', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    expect(() => repoOf(db).upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA')], 'v', { AAX: ['AAA'], AAY: ['AAA'] }))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'metro' }));
  });
});

describe('AirportsRepo', () => {
  const seeded = (rows: Row[], metros: Record<string, string[]> = {}) => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const repo = repoOf(db);
    repo.upsertAll(dataset(rows, 'test', metros));
    return repo;
  };

  it('exists, metroOf, airportsOfMetro, sharedMetro, tzOf', () => {
    const repo = seeded([row('AAA'), row('AAB'), row('BBB'), row('CCC', { tz: 'Europe/London' })], { AAX: ['AAA', 'AAB'], BBX: ['BBB'] });
    expect(repo.exists('AAA')).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.exists('ZZZ')).toBe(false);
    expect(repo.metroOf('AAA')).toBe('AAX');
    expect(repo.metroOf('CCC')).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.airportsOfMetro('AAX')).toEqual(['AAA', 'AAB']);
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('AAB')])).toBe('AAX');
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('BBB')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('CCC')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([asIata('AAA'), asIata('ZZZ')])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.sharedMetro([])).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.tzOf('CCC')).toBe('Europe/London');
    expect(repo.tzOf('ZZZ')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('search ranks an exact code, then a code prefix, then name or city matches; wildcards are literal', () => {
    const repo = seeded([row('AAB', { name: 'Alpha Field' }), row('AAA', { name: 'Beta Field' }), row('BAA', { name: 'Field AA' }), row('CCC', { name: '100% Synthetic' })]);
    expect(repo.search('aaa', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA']);
    expect(repo.search('AA', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAA', 'AAB', 'BAA']);
    expect(repo.search('alpha', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['AAB']);
    expect(repo.search('%', 10).map((a) => a.iata)).toEqual(['CCC']);
    expect(repo.search('  ', 10)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.search('field', 1)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('rejects malformed IATA codes and unknown time zones, writing nothing', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const repo = repoOf(db);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('aa1')]))).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA', { tz: 'Mars/Olympus_Mons' })]))).toThrow(/tz/);
    expect(() => repo.upsertAll(dataset([row('AAA')], 'v', { x1: ['AAA'] }))).toThrow(/metro/);
    expect(repo.count()).toBe(0);
    expect(repo.datasetVersion()).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`packages/db/src/schema/airports.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, real, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/**
 * Spec §7 `airports`, seeded from vendored mwgg/Airports (MIT) with `metro` from the separately
 * vendored, attributed list of IATA metropolitan codes (revision 10). `tz` is the IANA zone every
 * local time is derived in (spec §5.6); `metro` drives Travelpayouts metro queries (spec §5.2).
 */
export const airports = sqliteTable(
  'airports',
  {
    iata: text('iata').primaryKey(),
    icao: text('icao'),
    name: text('name').notNull(),
    city: text('city').notNull(),
    country: text('country').notNull(),
    tz: text('tz').notNull(),
    lat: real('lat').notNull(),
    lon: real('lon').notNull(),
    metro: text('metro'),
    source: text('source').notNull(),
    datasetVersion: text('dataset_version').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    check('airports_iata_ck', sql.raw("iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_metro_ck', sql.raw("metro IS NULL OR metro GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]'")),
    check('airports_tz_ck', sql.raw('length(tz) > 0')),
    index('airports_metro_idx').on(t.metro),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/airports.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, Iata } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { Airport, AirportsDataset, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface AirportDbRow { iata: string; icao: string | null; name: string; city: string; country: string; tz: string; lat: number; lon: number; metro: string | null; source: string; dataset_version: string }

const toAirport = (r: AirportDbRow): Airport => ({
  iata: r.iata as Iata, icao: r.icao, name: r.name, city: r.city, country: r.country, tz: r.tz, lat: r.lat, lon: r.lon, metro: r.metro, source: r.source, datasetVersion: r.dataset_version,
});
const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;

function isKnownTimeZone(tz: string): boolean {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

/** Metro code per airport from the list; an airport in two metros is a data error, never a guess. */
export function metroIndex(metros: Record<string, string[]>): Map<string, string> {
  const out = new Map<string, string>();
  for (const [code, members] of Object.entries(metros)) {
    if (!IATA.test(code)) throw new RowRejected('metro', `metro code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${code}`);
    for (const m of members) {
      const prior = out.get(m);
      if (prior !== undefined && prior !== code) throw new RowRejected('metro', `${m} is listed under both ${prior} and ${code}`);
      out.set(m, code);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

/** Airport lookups for validation, tiling and normalisation (spec §5.2, §5.6, §6.1). No airport is named in code. */
export class AirportsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); airports carry no timestamps and no synthetic rows.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  exists(iata: string): boolean {
    return this.db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata) !== undefined;
  }

  get(iata: string): Airport | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], AirportDbRow>('SELECT * FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata);
    return r === undefined ? null : toAirport(r);
  }

  tzOf(iata: string): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { tz: string }>('SELECT tz FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata)?.tz ?? null;
  }

  /** Autocomplete (spec §11, `GET /api/airports`): exact code, then code prefix, then name/city substring. */
  search(q: string, limit: number): Airport[] {
    const needle = q.trim();
    if (needle.length === 0) return [];
    // why: the operator's text is matched literally; % and _ are escaped for LIKE.
    const e = needle.replace(/[\\%_]/g, (c) => `\\${c}`);
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, AirportDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM airports
         WHERE iata = upper(@q) OR iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
            OR name LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\' OR city LIKE '%' || @e || '%' ESCAPE '\\'
         ORDER BY CASE WHEN iata = upper(@q) THEN 0 WHEN iata LIKE upper(@e) || '%' ESCAPE '\\' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END, iata
         LIMIT @limit`,
      )
      .all({ q: needle, e, limit: Math.max(1, Math.trunc(limit)) })
      .map(toAirport);
  }

  metroOf(iata: string): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { metro: string | null }>('SELECT metro FROM airports WHERE iata = ?').get(iata)?.metro ?? null;
  }

  /** Every airport the table maps to a metro code (WP1 names them, plan Review Focus 1). */
  airportsOfMetro(code: string): Iata[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { iata: string }>('SELECT iata FROM airports WHERE metro = ? ORDER BY iata').all(code).map((r) => r.iata as Iata);
  }

  /** The one metro code every given airport shares, else null (spec §5.2 Travelpayouts metro queries). */
  sharedMetro(iatas: Iata[]): string | null {
    const unique = [...new Set(iatas)];
    if (unique.length === 0) return null;
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { n: number; nulls: number; distinctMetros: number; metro: string | null }>(
        `SELECT COUNT(*) AS n, SUM(metro IS NULL) AS nulls, COUNT(DISTINCT metro) AS distinctMetros, MIN(metro) AS metro
         FROM airports WHERE iata IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?))`,
      )
      .get(JSON.stringify(unique));
    if (r === undefined || r.n !== unique.length || r.nulls > 0 || r.distinctMetros !== 1) return null;
    return r.metro;
  }

  count(): number {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports').get()?.n ?? 0;
  }

  /** The version most rows carry, or null for an empty table (WP8 compares it with the vendored version). */
  datasetVersion(): string | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[], { v: string }>('SELECT dataset_version AS v FROM airports GROUP BY dataset_version ORDER BY COUNT(*) DESC, v DESC LIMIT 1').get()?.v ?? null;
  }

  /** Upsert by IATA in one transaction; every row is validated first so a bad dataset writes nothing. `metro` comes from `dataset.metros`. */
  upsertAll(dataset: AirportsDataset): void {
    const metros = metroIndex(dataset.metros);
    for (const a of dataset.airports) {
      if (!IATA.test(a.iata)) throw new RowRejected('iata', `airport code must match ^[A-Z]{3}$, got ${a.iata}`);
      if (!isKnownTimeZone(a.tz)) throw new RowRejected('tz', `unknown time zone ${a.tz} for ${a.iata}`);
    }
    const source = `mwgg/Airports ${dataset.version}`;
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO airports (iata, icao, name, city, country, tz, lat, lon, metro, source, dataset_version)
       VALUES (@iata, @icao, @name, @city, @country, @tz, @lat, @lon, @metro, @source, @datasetVersion)
       ON CONFLICT(iata) DO UPDATE SET icao = excluded.icao, name = excluded.name, city = excluded.city, country = excluded.country, tz = excluded.tz,
         lat = excluded.lat, lon = excluded.lon, metro = excluded.metro, source = excluded.source, dataset_version = excluded.dataset_version`,
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const a of dataset.airports) stmt.run({ ...a, metro: metros.get(a.iata) ?? null, source, datasetVersion: dataset.version });
    });
  }
}
```

`packages/db/src/seed/airports.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { type Iata, systemClock } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { AirportsRepo, metroIndex } from '../repositories/airports.js';
import type { AirportsDataset } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7: boot fails unless at least this many airports carry an IATA code and a tz. */
export const AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS = 5000;

/**
 * Reads the vendored `airports.<version>.json` and `metros.json` from `dir` (decision 11). No network
 * is involved anywhere in seeding (spec §7). The dataset version names both inputs.
 */
export function loadAirportsDataset(dir: string): AirportsDataset {
  const files = readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => /^airports\..+\.json$/.test(f));
  const only = files[0];
  if (files.length !== 1 || only === undefined) throw new Error(`expected exactly one airports.<version>.json in ${dir}, found ${files.length}`);
  const a = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, only), 'utf8')) as { version?: unknown; airports?: unknown };
  const m = JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(dir, 'metros.json'), 'utf8')) as { revision?: unknown; metros?: unknown };
  if (typeof a.version !== 'string' || !Array.isArray(a.airports)) throw new Error(`${only} lacks version or airports`);
  if ((typeof m.revision !== 'string' && typeof m.revision !== 'number') || typeof m.metros !== 'object' || m.metros === null) throw new Error('metros.json lacks revision or metros');
  const metros = m.metros as Record<string, string[]>;
  const index = metroIndex(metros);
  const airports = (a.airports as Array<Omit<AirportsDataset['airports'][number], 'metro'>>).map((x) => ({ ...x, metro: index.get(x.iata) ?? null }));
  return { version: `${a.version}+metros-${String(m.revision)}`, airports, metros };
}

/** Seeds or re-seeds `airports` by upsert when the dataset version changes (spec §7); skips when every row is present at this version. */
export function seedAirports(db: Db, dataset: AirportsDataset): { inserted: number; datasetVersion: string } {
  const atVersion = db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE dataset_version = ?').get(dataset.version)?.n ?? 0;
  if (atVersion > 0 && atVersion >= dataset.airports.length) return { inserted: 0, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
  // why: airports carry no timestamps and no synthetic rows; the system clock and the default gate are never consulted.
  new AirportsRepo(db, systemClock, { allowSynthetic: false }).upsertAll(dataset);
  return { inserted: dataset.airports.length, datasetVersion: dataset.version };
}

/** Spec §7 sanity check, run at boot after seeding; a failure fails boot (WP8). */
export function sanityCheckAirports(db: Db, referenced: Iata[]): { ok: true } | { ok: false; reason: string } {
  const n = db.raw.prepare<[], { n: number }>(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM airports WHERE iata GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND length(tz) > 0`).get()?.n ?? 0;
  if (n < AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS) return { ok: false, reason: `airports table holds ${n} rows with an IATA code and tz; at least ${AIRPORTS_MIN_ROWS} are required` };
  const exists = db.raw.prepare('SELECT 1 AS present FROM airports WHERE iata = ?');
  const missing = [...new Set(referenced)].filter((iata) => exists.get(iata) === undefined).sort();
  if (missing.length > 0) return { ok: false, reason: `airports referenced by enabled definitions are missing: ${missing.join(', ')}` };
  return { ok: true };
}
```

`packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs`:

```js
#!/usr/bin/env node
// Builds the vendored airports data (spec §7, decision 11). Run by a person when updating the data,
// never by the app or CI; the app never downloads anything at seed.
//
//   node packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs --input <mwgg airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD>
//   node packages/db/scripts/build-airports.mjs --wikitext <section wikitext> --revision <Wikipedia revision id>
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { parseArgs } from 'node:util';

const MIN_ROWS = 5000; // spec §7 sanity threshold; the build refuses to write a smaller file
const IATA = /^[A-Z]{3}$/;
const out = (name) => fileURLToPath(new URL(`../data/${name}`, import.meta.url));
const { values } = parseArgs({ options: { input: { type: 'string' }, commit: { type: 'string' }, date: { type: 'string' }, wikitext: { type: 'string' }, revision: { type: 'string' } } });

if (values.wikitext) {
  if (!/^\d+$/.test(values.revision ?? '')) throw new Error('--revision <numeric revision id> is required with --wikitext');
  // Each bullet: "* City (MET)&nbsp;– Airport (AAA), Airport (BBB, formerly CCC) …"
  const metros = {};
  for (const line of readFileSync(values.wikitext, 'utf8').split('\n')) {
    if (!line.startsWith('*')) continue;
    const head = line.match(/\(([A-Z]{3})\)&nbsp;–/);
    if (!head) continue;
    const rest = line.slice(line.indexOf(head[0]) + head[0].length);
    metros[head[1]] = [...new Set([...rest.matchAll(/\(([A-Z]{3})[,)]/g)].map((m) => m[1]))].sort();
  }
  const source = `https://en.wikipedia.org/w/index.php?title=IATA_airport_code&oldid=${values.revision}`;
  writeFileSync(out('metros.json'), `${JSON.stringify({ source, revision: values.revision, licence: 'CC BY-SA 4.0', retrieved: new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10), metros }, null, 1)}\n`);
  console.log(`wrote ${Object.keys(metros).length} metro codes from revision ${values.revision}`);
  process.exit(0);
}

if (!values.input || !/^[0-9a-f]{40}$/.test(values.commit ?? '') || !/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(values.date ?? '')) {
  console.error('usage: build-airports.mjs --input <airports.json> --commit <40-hex sha> --date <YYYY-MM-DD>');
  process.exit(2);
}
const knownTz = (tz) => {
  try {
    new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en', { timeZone: tz });
    return true;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
};
const seen = new Set();
const airports = [];
let skippedTz = 0;
for (const a of Object.values(JSON.parse(readFileSync(values.input, 'utf8')))) {
  const iata = typeof a.iata === 'string' ? a.iata.trim() : '';
  if (!IATA.test(iata) || typeof a.tz !== 'string' || a.tz.length === 0 || typeof a.lat !== 'number' || typeof a.lon !== 'number') continue;
  if (!knownTz(a.tz)) {
    skippedTz++;
    continue;
  }
  if (seen.has(iata)) throw new Error(`duplicate IATA code ${iata}; refusing to guess which row is right`);
  seen.add(iata);
  airports.push({ iata, icao: a.icao || null, name: String(a.name), city: typeof a.city === 'string' ? a.city : '', country: String(a.country), tz: a.tz, lat: a.lat, lon: a.lon });
}
airports.sort((x, y) => (x.iata < y.iata ? -1 : 1));
if (airports.length < MIN_ROWS) throw new Error(`only ${airports.length} airports with IATA and tz; at least ${MIN_ROWS} required`);
const version = `${values.date}-${values.commit.slice(0, 7)}`;
writeFileSync(out(`airports.${version}.json`), `${JSON.stringify({ version, source: `mwgg/Airports@${values.commit}`, licence: 'MIT; see airports.LICENSE', airports })}\n`);
console.log(`wrote ${airports.length} airports (${skippedTz} unknown time zones skipped), version ${version}`);
```

```ts
import { asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { FxRepo } from '../src/repositories/fx.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';

const d = asIsoDate;
const repoOf = () => new FxRepo(freshDb(), fixedClock(T0), { allowSynthetic: false });

describe('FxRepo (spec §5.6, §6.3, §7 fx_rates)', () => {
  it('stores integer nano rates with their source and answers the newest rate within maxAgeDays', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    repo.upsert([
      { date: d('2026-10-09'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 760_000_000, source: 'frankfurter' },
      { date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 750_000_000, source: 'frankfurter' },
      { date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'JPY', rateNano: 5_100_000, source: 'frankfurter' },
    ]);
    expect(repo.rateNano(d('2026-10-12'), 'USD')).toBe(750_000_000);
    expect(repo.rateNano(d('2026-10-13'), 'USD')).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-14'))).toEqual({ rateNano: 750_000_000, date: '2026-10-12', source: 'frankfurter' });
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-15'))).toEqual({ rateNano: 750_000_000, date: '2026-10-12', source: 'frankfurter' }); // 15 − 3 = 12: in
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-16'))).toBeNull(); // 16 − 3 = 13: too old
    expect(repo.latest('CNY', 3, d('2026-10-14'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('upsert replaces the rate and source for the same date and currency', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    repo.upsert([{ date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 750_000_000, source: 'a' }]);
    repo.upsert([{ date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 751_000_000, source: 'b' }]);
    expect(repo.latest('USD', 3, d('2026-10-12'))).toEqual({ rateNano: 751_000_000, date: '2026-10-12', source: 'b' });
  });

  it('rejects non-integer rates, lower-case currencies, GBP itself and an empty source', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    const row = { date: d('2026-10-12'), currency: 'USD', rateNano: 750_000_000, source: 'frankfurter' };
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, rateNano: 0.75 }])).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, currency: 'usd' }])).toThrow(/currency/);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, currency: 'GBP' }])).toThrow(/GBP/);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row, source: '' }])).toThrow(/source/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';

/** Spec §7 `fx_rates`: GBP per 1 native unit × 1e9 (spec §6.3). `source` is stamped on converted prices as `fx_source`. */
export const fxRates = sqliteTable(
  'fx_rates',
  {
    date: text('date').notNull(),
    currency: text('currency').notNull(),
    rateNano: integer('rate_nano').notNull(),
    source: text('source').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.date, t.currency] }),
    check('fx_rates_rate_ck', sql.raw("typeof(rate_nano) = 'integer' AND rate_nano > 0")),
    check('fx_rates_currency_ck', sql.raw("currency GLOB '[A-Z][A-Z][A-Z]' AND currency <> 'GBP'")),
    check('fx_rates_date_ck', sql.raw("date GLOB '[0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]'")),
    check('fx_rates_source_ck', sql.raw('length(source) > 0')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/fx.ts`:

```ts
import type { Clock, IsoDate } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

/** FX rates for normalisation (spec §5.6: `FX_MAX_RATE_AGE_DAYS`, ECB-covered currencies only). */
export class FxRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    // why: uniform repository constructor (plan contract); `today` is passed explicitly by callers.
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  rateNano(date: IsoDate, currency: string): number | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<[string, string], { r: number }>('SELECT rate_nano AS r FROM fx_rates WHERE date = ? AND currency = ?').get(date, currency)?.r ?? null;
  }

  /** The newest rate dated within [today − maxAgeDays, today]; null means `fx-unavailable` (spec §5.6). */
  latest(currency: string, maxAgeDays: number, today: IsoDate): { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate; source: string } | null {
    assertInt('maxAgeDays', maxAgeDays);
    return (
      this.db.raw
        .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { rateNano: number; date: IsoDate; source: string }>(
          `SELECT rate_nano AS rateNano, date, source FROM fx_rates
           WHERE currency = @currency AND date <= @today AND date >= date(@today, '-' || @maxAgeDays || ' days')
           ORDER BY date DESC LIMIT 1`,
        )
        .get({ currency, today, maxAgeDays }) ?? null
    );
  }

  upsert(rows: Array<{ date: IsoDate; currency: string; rateNano: number; source: string }>): void {
    for (const r of rows) {
      assertIsoDate('date', r.date);
      if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(r.currency)) throw new RowRejected('currency', `currency must be ISO 4217 upper-case, got ${r.currency}`);
      if (r.currency === 'GBP') throw new RowRejected('currency', 'GBP is the base currency and has no rate row');
      assertInt('rate_nano', r.rateNano, 1);
      if (r.source.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('source', 'an FX rate must name its source');
    }
    const stmt = this.db.raw.prepare(
      'INSERT INTO fx_rates (date, currency, rate_nano, source) VALUES (@date, @currency, @rateNano, @source) ON CONFLICT(date, currency) DO UPDATE SET rate_nano = excluded.rate_nano, source = excluded.source',
    );
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const r of rows) stmt.run(r);
    });
  }
}
```

```ts
import { fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { JFK, exampleDefinitionInput } from './helpers/factories.js';

const repoOf = (db = freshDb(), clock = fixedClock(T0)) => new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });

describe('DefinitionsRepo (spec §7 search_definitions)', () => {
  it('round-trips every field; confirmed cost starts null', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    const input = exampleDefinitionInput({ destinationNotes: { [JFK]: 'onward leg not included' }, onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 4500 } });
    const created = repo.create(input);
    expect(created).toEqual({ ...input, id: created.id, confirmedCostPence: null, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: T0, deletedAt: null });
    expect(repo.get(created.id)).toEqual(created);
    expect(repo.get('missing')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('update merges a patch and moves updated_at', () => {
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(freshDb(), clock);
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    clock.advance(60_000);
    expect(repo.update(d.id, { maxNights: 14, enabled: false })).toMatchObject({ maxNights: 14, enabled: false, minNights: 10, createdAt: T0, updatedAt: '2026-10-14T12:01:00.000Z' });
  });

  it('soft delete: hidden from list unless asked, kept for history, refuses updates, keeps executions', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const keep = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'kept' }));
    const gone = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ name: 'gone' }));
    db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, created_at) VALUES ('e1', ?, '{}', 'manual', 'completed', ?)`).run(gone.id, T0);
    clock.advance(1000);
    repo.softDelete(gone.id);
    expect(repo.list().map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(['kept']);
    expect(repo.list({ includeDeleted: true }).map((d) => d.name)).toEqual(['kept', 'gone']);
    expect(repo.get(gone.id)).toMatchObject({ deletedAt: '2026-10-14T12:00:01.000Z', enabled: false });
    expect(() => repo.update(gone.id, { name: 'x' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.softDelete('missing')).toThrow(/missing/);
    expect(count(db, 'search_executions')).toBe(1);
    expect(repo.get(keep.id)?.deletedAt).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setConfirmedCost stores integer pence only (spec §7: only from an explicit confirmation)', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    const d = repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput());
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 412);
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBe(412);
    repo.update(d.id, { name: 'renamed' });
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBe(412); // update never touches it
    repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, null);
    expect(repo.get(d.id)?.confirmedCostPence).toBeNull();
    expect(() => repo.setConfirmedCost(d.id, 4.5)).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('rejects non-integer nights, budgets and onward costs', () => {
    const repo = repoOf();
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ minNights: 10.5 }))).toThrow(/min_nights/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ budget: { maxCalls: 1, maxFreeCalls: 1, maxCostMicroGbp: 0.5 } }))).toThrow(/maxCostMicroGbp/);
    expect(() => repo.create(exampleDefinitionInput({ onwardCostPence: { [JFK]: 12.3 } }))).toThrow(/onward/);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import type { Clock, DefinitionInput, IsoDateTime, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertNullableInt } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface DefinitionDbRow {
  id: string; name: string; origins_json: string; destinations_json: string; window_json: string; min_nights: number; max_nights: number;
  cabin: SearchDefinition['cabin']; constraints_json: string; verification_policy_json: string; budget_json: string; providers_json: string;
  destination_notes_json: string | null; onward_cost_json: string | null; schedule_cron: string | null; schedule_tz: string;
  confirmed_cost_pence: number | null; enabled: number; deleted_at: string | null; created_at: string; updated_at: string;
}

function toDefinition(r: DefinitionDbRow): SearchDefinition {
  return {
    id: r.id, name: r.name, origins: JSON.parse(r.origins_json), destinations: JSON.parse(r.destinations_json), window: JSON.parse(r.window_json),
    minNights: r.min_nights, maxNights: r.max_nights, cabin: r.cabin, constraints: JSON.parse(r.constraints_json),
    verification: JSON.parse(r.verification_policy_json), budget: JSON.parse(r.budget_json), providers: JSON.parse(r.providers_json),
    destinationNotes: r.destination_notes_json === null ? {} : JSON.parse(r.destination_notes_json),
    onwardCostPence: r.onward_cost_json === null ? {} : JSON.parse(r.onward_cost_json),
    scheduleCron: r.schedule_cron, scheduleTz: r.schedule_tz, confirmedCostPence: r.confirmed_cost_pence, enabled: r.enabled === 1,
    createdAt: r.created_at as IsoDateTime, updatedAt: r.updated_at as IsoDateTime, deletedAt: r.deleted_at as IsoDateTime | null,
  };
}

/** Integer rules the repository owns (spec §6.3); shape and range rules are zod's in `api-contract`. */
function validate(d: DefinitionInput): void {
  assertInt('min_nights', d.minNights);
  assertInt('max_nights', d.maxNights);
  assertInt('budget.maxCalls', d.budget.maxCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxFreeCalls', d.budget.maxFreeCalls);
  assertInt('budget.maxCostMicroGbp', d.budget.maxCostMicroGbp);
  for (const [dest, pence] of Object.entries(d.onwardCostPence)) if (pence !== undefined) assertInt(`onward cost for ${dest}`, pence);
  if (d.scheduleTz.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('schedule-tz', 'schedule_tz is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
}

const params = (d: DefinitionInput, updatedAt: IsoDateTime): Record<string, unknown> => ({
  name: d.name, origins: JSON.stringify(d.origins), destinations: JSON.stringify(d.destinations), window: JSON.stringify(d.window),
  minNights: d.minNights, maxNights: d.maxNights, cabin: d.cabin, constraints: JSON.stringify(d.constraints), verification: JSON.stringify(d.verification),
  budget: JSON.stringify(d.budget), providers: JSON.stringify(d.providers), destinationNotes: JSON.stringify(d.destinationNotes), onwardCost: JSON.stringify(d.onwardCostPence),
  scheduleCron: d.scheduleCron, scheduleTz: d.scheduleTz, enabled: d.enabled ? 1 : 0, updatedAt,
});

/** Spec §7 `search_definitions`, soft-deleted. `confirmed_cost_pence` changes only through `setConfirmedCost`. */
export class DefinitionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  create(input: DefinitionInput): SearchDefinition {
    validate(input);
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_definitions (id, created_at, name, origins_json, destinations_json, window_json, min_nights, max_nights, cabin, constraints_json,
          verification_policy_json, budget_json, providers_json, destination_notes_json, onward_cost_json, schedule_cron, schedule_tz, confirmed_cost_pence, enabled, updated_at)
         VALUES (@id, @createdAt, @name, @origins, @destinations, @window, @minNights, @maxNights, @cabin, @constraints, @verification, @budget, @providers,
          @destinationNotes, @onwardCost, @scheduleCron, @scheduleTz, NULL, @enabled, @updatedAt)`,
      )
      .run({ id, createdAt: now, ...params(input, now) });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  update(id: string, patch: Partial<DefinitionInput>): SearchDefinition {
    const { id: _id, confirmedCostPence: _c, createdAt: _ca, updatedAt: _u, deletedAt, ...current } = this.mustGet(id);
    if (deletedAt !== null) throw new RowRejected('deleted', `definition ${id} is deleted`);
    const merged: DefinitionInput = { ...current, ...patch };
    validate(merged);
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `UPDATE search_definitions SET name = @name, origins_json = @origins, destinations_json = @destinations, window_json = @window, min_nights = @minNights,
           max_nights = @maxNights, cabin = @cabin, constraints_json = @constraints, verification_policy_json = @verification, budget_json = @budget,
           providers_json = @providers, destination_notes_json = @destinationNotes, onward_cost_json = @onwardCost, schedule_cron = @scheduleCron,
           schedule_tz = @scheduleTz, enabled = @enabled, updated_at = @updatedAt
         WHERE id = @id`,
      )
      .run({ id, ...params(merged, this.clock.now()) });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  /** Returns soft-deleted definitions too (history needs them). */
  get(id: string): SearchDefinition | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : toDefinition(r);
  }

  list(opts: { includeDeleted?: boolean } = {}): SearchDefinition[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[number], DefinitionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_definitions WHERE (? = 1 OR deleted_at IS NULL) ORDER BY created_at, rowid')
      .all(opts.includeDeleted ? 1 : 0)
      .map(toDefinition);
  }

  /** Spec §7 "Soft-deleted": executions, prices and history keep their foreign keys; the definition is disabled and never scheduled or resumed (plan engine rules). */
  softDelete(id: string): void {
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const changes = this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET deleted_at = @now, enabled = 0, updated_at = @now WHERE id = @id AND deleted_at IS NULL').run({ id, now }).changes;
    if (changes === 0 && this.get(id) === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /** Spec §7: set only from an explicit operator confirmation, never recomputed automatically. */
  setConfirmedCost(id: string, pence: number | null): void {
    assertNullableInt('confirmed_cost_pence', pence);
    const changes = this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_definitions SET confirmed_cost_pence = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?').run(pence, this.clock.now(), id).changes;
    if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): SearchDefinition {
    const d = this.get(id);
    if (d === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `definition ${id} does not exist`);
    return d;
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { Clock, DefinitionSnapshot, Execution, ExecutionStatus, SearchDefinition } from '@gmt/core';
import { DefinitionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/definitions.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { ExecutionCreateInput } from '../../src/types.js';

/** A frozen snapshot of a definition resolved at T0 (spec §3 "snapshot"). */
export function snapshotOf(d: SearchDefinition): DefinitionSnapshot {
  return {
    ...d,
    resolved: { origins: d.origins, destinations: d.destinations, departureFrom: asIsoDate('2026-10-21'), departureTo: asIsoDate('2027-04-20'), minNights: d.minNights, maxNights: d.maxNights },
    resolvedAt: T0, windowClamped: false, droppedAlwaysInclude: [],
  };
}

const CREATABLE: ReadonlySet<ExecutionStatus> = new Set(['pending', 'running', 'skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed']);

/**
 * Creates (or reuses) a definition and one execution through the repositories. A status the
 * repository cannot create (e.g. `completed`) is reached by creating `running` then `setStatus`,
 * the path the engine takes.
 */
export function newExecution(
  db: Db,
  clock: Clock,
  opts: { definition?: SearchDefinition; status?: ExecutionStatus; cell?: CellKey | null; trigger?: Execution['trigger'] } = {},
): { definition: SearchDefinition; execution: Execution } {
  const definition = opts.definition ?? new DefinitionsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false }).create(exampleDefinitionInput());
  const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
  const wanted = opts.status ?? 'running';
  const createAs = (CREATABLE.has(wanted) ? wanted : 'running') as ExecutionCreateInput['status'];
  const cell = opts.cell ?? null;
  const created = executions.create({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: cell !== null, cell, snapshot: snapshotOf(definition), estimate: null, trigger: opts.trigger ?? 'manual', status: createAs });
  if (createAs === wanted) return { definition, execution: created };
  executions.setStatus(created.id, wanted, { finishedAt: clock.now() });
  const moved = executions.get(created.id);
  if (moved === null) throw new Error('execution vanished');
  return { definition, execution: moved };
}
```

`packages/db/test/helpers/claim-worker.mjs`:

```js
// One contender in the compare-and-set race (spec §8.1 "Resume is a compare-and-set"): its own
// connection, a shared start barrier, then the repository's own claim statement; reports `changes`.
import { parentPort, workerData } from 'node:worker_threads';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';

const { path, sql, id, barrier } = workerData;
const db = new Database(path);
db.pragma('busy_timeout = 5000');
const gate = new Int32Array(barrier);
Atomics.add(gate, 0, 1);
Atomics.notify(gate, 0);
while (Atomics.load(gate, 0) < 2) Atomics.wait(gate, 0, 1, 50);
const { changes } = db.prepare(sql).run(id);
db.close();
parentPort.postMessage(changes);
```

`packages/db/test/executions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Worker } from 'node:worker_threads';
import { ACTIVE_STATUSES, type IsoDateTime, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { openDatabase } from '../src/client.js';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../src/schema/checks.js';
import { T0, freshDb, tmpDbPath } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution, snapshotOf } from './helpers/factories.js';

const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;
const isUniqueViolation = (e: unknown) => e instanceof Database.SqliteError && e.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE';
const repoOf = (db: ReturnType<typeof freshDb>, clock = fixedClock(T0)) => new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
const throwsUnique = (fn: () => unknown) => {
  try {
    fn();
    return false;
  } catch (e) {
    return isUniqueViolation(e);
  }
};

describe('ExecutionsRepo (spec §7 search_executions, §8.1)', () => {
  it('the schema active set matches core ACTIVE_STATUSES', () => {
    expect(ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL).toBe(ACTIVE_STATUSES.map((s) => `'${s}'`).join(', '));
  });

  it('round-trips parent and child; pending has no startedAt, running does, skipped is created finished', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending', trigger: 'schedule' });
    expect(execution).toMatchObject({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: false, cell: null, status: 'pending', stage: null, trigger: 'schedule', createdAt: T0, startedAt: null, finishedAt: null, autoResumeCount: 0, cancelRequested: false, errorText: null });
    expect(execution.snapshot).toEqual(snapshotOf(definition));
    expect(newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }).execution).toMatchObject({ isChild: true, cell: cell(), startedAt: T0 });
    expect(newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'skipped_window' }).execution).toMatchObject({ status: 'skipped_window', startedAt: null, finishedAt: T0 });
    expect(() => repoOf(db).create({ definitionId: definition.id, isChild: true, cell: null, snapshot: snapshotOf(definition), estimate: null, trigger: 'manual', status: 'running' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('partial unique indexes: one active parent per definition, one active child per cell; parent and child coexist', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'pending' });
    expect(throwsUnique(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition }))).toBe(true);
    newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() });
    expect(throwsUnique(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }))).toBe(true);
    expect(throwsUnique(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }))).toBe(false);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed', { finishedAt: T0 });
    expect(throwsUnique(() => newExecution(db, clock, { definition }))).toBe(false);
    const sqlOf = (name: string) => (db.raw.prepare(`SELECT sql FROM sqlite_master WHERE name = ?`).get(name) as { sql: string }).sql;
    expect(sqlOf('search_executions_active_parent_uq')).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 0 AND status IN \('pending', 'running', 'paused_budget', 'paused_error', 'interrupted'\)/);
    expect(sqlOf('search_executions_active_child_uq')).toMatch(/WHERE is_child = 1 AND status IN/);
  });

  it('reads: activeParent, activeChild, latestCompleted, listForDefinition, listByStatus, lastScheduledFiring', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: first } = newExecution(db, clock, { trigger: 'schedule' });
    repo.setStatus(first.id, 'completed');
    clock.advance(1000);
    const missed = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'skipped_missed', trigger: 'schedule' }).execution;
    clock.advance(1000);
    const second = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution;
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell() }).execution;
    expect(repo.activeParent(definition.id)?.id).toBe(second.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell())?.id).toBe(child.id);
    expect(repo.activeChild(definition.id, cell(undefined, EWR))).toBeNull();
    expect(repo.latestCompleted(definition.id)?.id).toBe(first.id);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: false }).map((e) => e.id)).toEqual([second.id, missed.id, first.id]);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 10, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(repo.listForDefinition(definition.id, { limit: 1, includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.listByStatus(['running']).map((e) => e.id).sort()).toEqual([second.id, child.id].sort());
    expect(repo.lastScheduledFiring(definition.id)).toBe('2026-10-14T12:00:01.000Z'); // the skipped_missed row counts as a firing
    expect(repo.lastScheduledFiring('none')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('setStatus and patch write only the keys given; error_text is URL-stripped; requestCancel and resetAutoResume', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.patch(execution.id, { stage: 'discovery' });
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'paused_error', {
      resumeAfter: at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'),
      errorText: 'SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://user:pw@api.example.com/v1/x?api_key=SECRET',
    });
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_error', stage: 'discovery', resumeAfter: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', errorText: 'SQLITE_FULL: database or disk is full while fetching https://api.example.com/v1/x' });
    const refreshed = { ...snapshotOf(definition), budget: { maxCalls: 5, maxFreeCalls: 5, maxCostMicroGbp: 0 } };
    repo.patch(execution.id, { snapshot: refreshed, startedAt: at('2026-10-14T12:30:00.000Z') });
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_error', startedAt: '2026-10-14T12:30:00.000Z', snapshot: { budget: { maxCalls: 5 } } });
    repo.requestCancel(execution.id);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.cancelRequested).toBe(true);
    repo.incrementAutoResume(execution.id);
    repo.resetAutoResume(execution.id);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(0);
    expect(() => repo.setStatus('missing', 'running')).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.patch(execution.id, { resumeAfter: at('tomorrow') })).toThrow(/toISOString/);
  });

  it('claimResume is a compare-and-set: resumable → running once; non-resumable and conflicting claims lose', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'paused_budget', { resumeAfter: at('2026-11-01T00:00:00.000Z') });
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'running', resumeAfter: null });
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false);
    repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    const other = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution;
    expect(repo.claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false); // another parent is active: the partial index refuses, the claim loses
    expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('completed');
    expect(repo.claimResume(other.id)).toBe(false);
    expect(repo.claimResume('missing')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('two repositories on two connections to one file: the second claim loses', () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const a = freshDb(path);
    const b = openDatabase(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const { execution } = newExecution(a, clock);
    repoOf(a, clock).setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
    expect(repoOf(b, clock).get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('interrupted');
    expect(repoOf(a, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(repoOf(b, clock).claimResume(execution.id)).toBe(false);
    b.close();
    a.close();
  });

  it('two concurrent claimers in two threads: exactly one wins, every round', async () => {
    const path = tmpDbPath();
    const db = freshDb(path);
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'skipped_window' });
    const contender = (id: string, barrier: SharedArrayBuffer) =>
      new Promise<number>((resolve, reject) => {
        const w = new Worker(new URL('./helpers/claim-worker.mjs', import.meta.url), { workerData: { path, sql: CLAIM_RESUME_SQL, id, barrier } });
        w.once('message', resolve);
        w.once('error', reject);
      });
    for (let round = 0; round < 10; round++) {
      const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock, { definition });
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'interrupted');
      const barrier = new SharedArrayBuffer(4);
      const results = await Promise.all([contender(execution.id, barrier), contender(execution.id, barrier)]);
      expect(results.sort()).toEqual([0, 1]);
      expect(repo.get(execution.id)?.status).toBe('running');
      repo.setStatus(execution.id, 'completed');
    }
    db.close();
  });

  it('bootSweep: running and pending-created-before-boot become interrupted with auto_resume_count + 1', () => {
    const db = freshDb();
    const clock = fixedClock(T0);
    const repo = repoOf(db, clock);
    const { definition, execution: running } = newExecution(db, clock);
    const oldPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell() }).execution;
    const paused = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    repo.setStatus(paused.id, 'paused_budget');
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const boot = clock.now();
    const freshPending = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'pending', cell: cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29') }).execution;
    expect(repo.bootSweep(boot).interrupted.sort()).toEqual([oldPending.id, running.id].sort());
    expect(repo.get(running.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(oldPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'interrupted', autoResumeCount: 1 });
    expect(repo.get(freshPending.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'pending', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.get(paused.id)).toMatchObject({ status: 'paused_budget', autoResumeCount: 0 });
    expect(repo.incrementAutoResume(running.id)).toBe(2);
    expect(() => repo.incrementAutoResume('missing')).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });
});
```
