`packages/db/src/repositories/executions.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { type CellKey, type Clock, type DefinitionSnapshot, type Estimate, type Execution, type ExecutionStatus, type Iata, type IsoDate, type IsoDateTime, RESUMABLE_STATUSES, type Stage, stripUrlsInText } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableIsoDateTime } from '../internal/validate.js';
import { ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL } from '../schema/checks.js';
import type { ExecutionCreateInput, ExecutionPatch, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface ExecutionDbRow {
  id: string; definition_id: string; is_child: number; child_origin: string | null; child_destination: string | null; child_departure_date: string | null; child_return_date: string | null;
  definition_snapshot_json: string; trigger: Execution['trigger']; status: ExecutionStatus; stage: Stage | null; estimate_json: string | null; cancel_requested: number;
  resume_after: string | null; auto_resume_count: number; created_at: string; started_at: string | null; finished_at: string | null; error_text: string | null;
}

function toExecution(r: ExecutionDbRow): Execution {
  const cell: CellKey | null =
    r.is_child === 1 && r.child_origin !== null && r.child_destination !== null && r.child_departure_date !== null && r.child_return_date !== null
      ? { origin: r.child_origin as Iata, destination: r.child_destination as Iata, departureDate: r.child_departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.child_return_date as IsoDate }
      : null;
  return {
    id: r.id, definitionId: r.definition_id, isChild: r.is_child === 1, cell,
    // why: the snapshot was validated before it was frozen; it is read back exactly as written.
    snapshot: JSON.parse(r.definition_snapshot_json) as DefinitionSnapshot, status: r.status, stage: r.stage,
    estimate: r.estimate_json === null ? null : (JSON.parse(r.estimate_json) as Estimate), cancelRequested: r.cancel_requested === 1,
    resumeAfter: r.resume_after as IsoDateTime | null, autoResumeCount: r.auto_resume_count, startedAt: r.started_at as IsoDateTime | null,
    finishedAt: r.finished_at as IsoDateTime | null, errorText: r.error_text, createdAt: r.created_at as IsoDateTime, trigger: r.trigger,
  };
}

const quoted = (xs: readonly string[]) => xs.map((x) => `'${x}'`).join(', ');

/**
 * Spec §8.1 "Resume is a compare-and-set": the tick, the one-shot job, boot auto-resume and `/resume`
 * all run this statement; exactly one caller sees `changes = 1`. Clearing `resume_after` in the same
 * statement cancels the one-shot schedule in the same step.
 */
export const CLAIM_RESUME_SQL = `UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'running', resume_after = NULL WHERE id = ? AND status IN (${quoted(RESUMABLE_STATUSES)})`;

/** Patch keys → columns; values are encoded by `encode`. Column names never come from input. */
const PATCH_COLUMNS: Record<keyof ExecutionPatch, string> = {
  stage: 'stage', resumeAfter: 'resume_after', errorText: 'error_text', startedAt: 'started_at', finishedAt: 'finished_at',
  cancelRequested: 'cancel_requested', estimate: 'estimate_json', snapshot: 'definition_snapshot_json',
};

function encode(key: keyof ExecutionPatch, patch: ExecutionPatch): unknown {
  switch (key) {
    case 'resumeAfter':
    case 'startedAt':
    case 'finishedAt': {
      const v = patch[key] ?? null;
      assertNullableIsoDateTime(PATCH_COLUMNS[key], v);
      return v;
    }
    case 'errorText':
      return patch.errorText === null || patch.errorText === undefined ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.errorText);
    case 'cancelRequested':
      return patch.cancelRequested ? 1 : 0;
    case 'estimate':
      return patch.estimate === null || patch.estimate === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(patch.estimate);
    case 'snapshot':
      if (patch.snapshot === undefined) throw new RowRejected('snapshot', 'a snapshot patch needs a snapshot');
      return JSON.stringify(patch.snapshot);
    case 'stage':
      return patch.stage ?? null;
  }
}

const SKIPPED: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['skipped_budget', 'skipped_confirm', 'skipped_window', 'skipped_missed']);

/** Spec §7 `search_executions`. Every write is a single statement or one transaction. */
export class ExecutionsRepo {
  constructor(
    private readonly db: Db,
    private readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  create(input: ExecutionCreateInput): Execution {
    if (input.isChild !== (input.cell !== null)) throw new RowRejected('child-cell', 'a child execution carries a cell and a parent does not');
    const id = randomUUID();
    const now = this.clock.now();
    const c = input.cell;
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(
        `INSERT INTO search_executions (id, definition_id, is_child, child_origin, child_destination, child_departure_date, child_return_date,
          definition_snapshot_json, trigger, status, estimate_json, created_at, started_at, finished_at)
         VALUES (@id, @definitionId, @isChild, @o, @d, @dep, @ret, @snapshot, @trigger, @status, @estimate, @now, @startedAt, @finishedAt)`,
      )
      .run({
        id, definitionId: input.definitionId, isChild: input.isChild ? 1 : 0, o: c?.origin ?? null, d: c?.destination ?? null, dep: c?.departureDate ?? null, ret: c?.returnDate ?? null,
        snapshot: JSON.stringify(input.snapshot), trigger: input.trigger, status: input.status, estimate: input.estimate === null ? null : JSON.stringify(input.estimate),
        now, startedAt: input.status === 'running' ? now : null, finishedAt: SKIPPED.has(input.status) ? now : null,
      });
    return this.mustGet(id);
  }

  get(id: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE id = ?').get(id);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  /** Newest first by creation. */
  listForDefinition(definitionId: string, opts: { limit: number; includeChildren: boolean }): Execution[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @definitionId AND (@children = 1 OR is_child = 0) ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT @limit`)
      .all({ definitionId, children: opts.includeChildren ? 1 : 0, limit: opts.limit })
      .map(toExecution);
  }

  /** Executions in any of the statuses, oldest first (boot auto-resume, one-shot re-registration). */
  listByStatus(statuses: ExecutionStatus[]): Execution[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE status IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(?)) ORDER BY created_at, rowid')
      .all(JSON.stringify(statuses))
      .map(toExecution);
  }

  activeParent(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})`).get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  activeChild(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ExecutionDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = @definitionId AND is_child = 1 AND status IN (${ACTIVE_STATUS_SQL})
         AND child_origin = @origin AND child_destination = @destination AND child_departure_date = @departureDate AND child_return_date = @returnDate`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  latestCompleted(definitionId: string): Execution | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], ExecutionDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND status = 'completed' ORDER BY created_at DESC, rowid DESC LIMIT 1`)
      .get(definitionId);
    return r === undefined ? null : toExecution(r);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 compare-and-set. A claim that would make a second active run loses instead of throwing (`/resume` answers 409). */
  claimResume(id: string): boolean {
    try {
      return this.db.raw.prepare(CLAIM_RESUME_SQL).run(id).changes === 1;
    } catch (err) {
      if (err instanceof Database.SqliteError && err.code === 'SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE') return false;
      throw err;
    }
  }

  setStatus(id: string, status: ExecutionStatus, patch: ExecutionPatch = {}): void {
    this.apply(id, status, patch);
  }

  patch(id: string, patch: ExecutionPatch): void {
    this.apply(id, null, patch);
  }

  requestCancel(id: string): void {
    this.apply(id, null, { cancelRequested: true });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "Boot order": `running` and `pending` created before this boot become `interrupted`, counting a crash restart. */
  bootSweep(bootAt: IsoDateTime): { interrupted: string[] } {
    assertIsoDateTime('bootAt', bootAt);
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], { id: string }>(
        `UPDATE search_executions SET status = 'interrupted', auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1
         WHERE status = 'running' OR (status = 'pending' AND created_at < ?) RETURNING id`,
      )
      .all(bootAt);
    return { interrupted: rows.map((r) => r.id) };
  }

  incrementAutoResume(id: string): number {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { n: number }>('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = auto_resume_count + 1 WHERE id = ? RETURNING auto_resume_count AS n').get(id);
    if (r === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return r.n;
  }

  /** Spec §7 "reset when a tile or cell commits"; the tile and cell writes also reset it inside their own transactions. */
  resetAutoResume(id: string): void {
    if (this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(id).changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  /** The newest schedule-triggered parent execution of the definition, including `skipped_*` rows (spec §8.2 missed-firing check). */
  lastScheduledFiring(definitionId: string): IsoDateTime | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], { at: string | null }>(`SELECT MAX(created_at) AS at FROM search_executions WHERE definition_id = ? AND is_child = 0 AND trigger = 'schedule'`).get(definitionId);
    return (r?.at ?? null) as IsoDateTime | null;
  }

  private apply(id: string, status: ExecutionStatus | null, patch: ExecutionPatch): void {
    const sets: string[] = [];
    const params: Record<string, unknown> = { id };
    if (status !== null) {
      sets.push('status = @status');
      params.status = status;
    }
    for (const key of Object.keys(PATCH_COLUMNS) as Array<keyof ExecutionPatch>) {
      if (!(key in patch)) continue;
      sets.push(`${PATCH_COLUMNS[key]} = @${key}`);
      params[key] = encode(key, patch);
    }
    if (sets.length === 0) return;
    if (this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_executions SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE id = @id`).run(params).changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
  }

  private mustGet(id: string): Execution {
    const e = this.get(id);
    if (e === null) throw new RowRejected('missing', `execution ${id} does not exist`);
    return e;
  }
}
```

```ts
import type { Cell } from '@gmt/core';

/** A Cell with its nights computed from the two calendar dates (UTC midnights, so no DST effect). */
export function withNights(c: CellKey): Cell {
  return { ...c, nights: (Date.parse(`${c.returnDate}T00:00:00Z`) - Date.parse(`${c.departureDate}T00:00:00Z`)) / 86_400_000 };
}
```

`packages/db/test/cells.rollup.test.ts`:

```ts
import { type CellKey, type CellOutcomeStatus, type ProviderId, type RollUp, asIsoDate, cellKeyString, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, cell, newExecution, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

type Variant = { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; minPence: number | null };
const VARIANTS: Variant[] = [
  { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 500 },
  { status: 'empty', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'no_data', reason: null, minPence: null },
  { status: 'no_data', reason: 'cabin-not-offered', minPence: null },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', minPence: null },
  { status: 'error', reason: 'auth', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'budget', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'departed', minPence: null },
  { status: 'skipped', reason: 'not_selected', minPence: null },
];

/** Spec §6.6 precedence table, restated independently of the SQL (rows 0–6). */
function reference(outs: Variant[]): RollUp {
  if (outs.length === 0) return { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null }; // row 0
  const has = (s: CellOutcomeStatus) => outs.some((o) => o.status === s);
  if (has('priced')) return { status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: Math.min(...outs.filter((o) => o.status === 'priced').map((o) => o.minPence ?? Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY)) };
  if (has('not_run')) return { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null };
  if (has('error')) return { status: 'error', reason: outs.filter((o) => o.status === 'error').map((o) => o.reason ?? '').sort()[0] ?? null, minPence: null };
  if (has('empty')) return { status: 'empty', reason: null, minPence: null };
  if (has('no_data')) return { status: 'no_data', reason: outs.filter((o) => o.status === 'no_data').map((o) => o.reason).filter((r) => r !== null).sort()[0] ?? null, minPence: null };
  const priority = ['budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed', 'not_selected'];
  return { status: 'skipped', reason: priority.find((r) => outs.some((o) => o.reason === r)) ?? null, minPence: null };
}

const PROVIDERS: ProviderId[] = ['travelpayouts', 'searchapi'];
const dayCell = (j: number): CellKey => cell(undefined, undefined, `2027-03-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`, `2027-04-${String(10 + j).padStart(2, '0')}`);

function setup(cells: CellKey[], providers: ProviderId[] = PROVIDERS) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const repo = new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
  repo.seed(execution.id, cells.map(withNights), providers);
  return { db, clock, repo, executionId: execution.id };
}

describe('CellsRepo seeding and roll-ups (spec §3, §6.6, §7)', () => {
  it('seed writes cells and not_run outcomes per discovery provider; verification has no rows (row 0 → NULL)', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)]);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)).toHaveLength(4);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { providerId: 'searchapi', cells: [cell()] })).toEqual([
      { ...cell(), executionId, stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'searchapi', status: 'not_run', reason: null, attempts: 0, lastError: null, fetchedAt: null, minPence: null, cabin: null, pointOfSale: null, sourceKind: null, reusedFromExecutionId: null },
    ]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))).toEqual({ discovery: { status: 'not_run', reason: null, minPence: null }, verification: { status: null, reason: null, minPence: null } });
    expect(repo.buckets(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 0 });
  });

  it('a stage with no provider configured has no outcome rows and a NULL roll-up', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell()], []);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)).toEqual([]);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: null, reason: null, minPence: null });
    expect(repo.buckets(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 0, reused: 0 });
  });

  it('precedence over every pair of outcomes (121 combinations) matches the independently stated table', () => {
    const cells = VARIANTS.map((_, j) => dayCell(j));
    for (const a of VARIANTS) {
      const { repo, executionId } = setup(cells);
      for (const [j, b] of VARIANTS.entries()) {
        const c = dayCell(j);
        const bOut = { ...b, minPence: b.status === 'priced' ? 400 : null };
        repo.setOutcome(executionId, c, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: a.status, reason: a.reason, minPence: a.minPence });
        repo.setOutcome(executionId, c, 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: bOut.status, reason: bOut.reason, minPence: bOut.minPence });
        expect({ a, b, roll: repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(c))?.discovery }).toEqual({ a, b, roll: reference([a, bOut]) });
      }
    }
  });

  it('setOutcome increments attempts on request, strips URLs from last_error, and rejects unknown rows, bad skip reasons and non-integers', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([cell()], ['searchapi']);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'error', reason: 'network', lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search?api_key=SECRET', incrementAttempts: true });
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId)[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', reason: 'network', attempts: 1, lastError: 'socket hang up https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search' });
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(undefined, EWR), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'priced' })).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'skipped', reason: 'bored' })).toThrow(/skip reason/);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcome(executionId, cell(), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { minPence: 1.5 })).toThrow(/min_pence/);
  });

  it('setOutcomesForProvider and markNoData move one provider\'s outcomes in bulk and recompute roll-ups', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([dayCell(0), dayCell(1), dayCell(2)]);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, dayCell(2), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'priced', minPence: 300 });
    expect(repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['not_run', 'error'], { status: 'skipped', reason: 'provider_disabled' })).toBe(2);
    expect(repo.outcomes(executionId, { providerId: 'searchapi' }).map((o) => [o.status, o.reason])).toEqual([['skipped', 'provider_disabled'], ['skipped', 'provider_disabled'], ['priced', null]]);
    expect(() => repo.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, 'discovery', 'searchapi', ['not_run'], { status: 'skipped', reason: 'whim' })).toThrow(/skip reason/);
    expect(repo.markNoData(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', 'cabin-not-offered')).toBe(3);
    expect(repo.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(dayCell(0)))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'no_data', reason: 'cabin-not-offered', minPence: null });
    expect(repo.markNoData(executionId, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', 'cabin-not-offered')).toBe(0); // only not_run rows move
  });

  it('bucketsPerDay groups the six buckets by departure date, stage and provider', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup([dayCell(0), cell(undefined, EWR, '2027-03-10', '2027-04-10'), dayCell(1)]);
    repo.setOutcome(executionId, dayCell(0), 'discovery', 'searchapi', { status: 'priced', minPence: 100 });
    const m = repo.bucketsPerDay(executionId);
    expect([...m.keys()]).toEqual([asIsoDate('2027-03-10'), asIsoDate('2027-03-11')]);
    expect(m.get(asIsoDate('2027-03-10'))).toEqual([
      { stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'searchapi', buckets: { priced: 1, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 1, reused: 0 }, total: 2 },
      { stage: 'discovery', providerId: 'travelpayouts', buckets: { priced: 0, empty: 0, no_data: 0, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 0 }, total: 2 },
    ]);
  });
});
```

`packages/db/src/schema/searchCells.ts`:

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { OUTCOME_STATUSES, isBool, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_cells` (revision 10 adds the reason and verification minimum) with the stored
 * roll-ups (spec §6.6). NULL status = the stage has no provider or no outcome rows yet.
 * `best_observation_id` gains its foreign key in Task 3.13, when `price_observations` exists.
 */
export const searchCells = sqliteTable(
  'search_cells',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    discoveryStatus: text('discovery_status'),
    discoveryReason: text('discovery_reason'),
    discoveryMinPence: integer('discovery_min_pence'),
    verificationSelected: integer('verification_selected').notNull().default(0),
    selectionReason: text('selection_reason'),
    verificationStatus: text('verification_status'),
    verificationReason: text('verification_reason'),
    verificationMinPence: integer('verification_min_pence'),
    bestObservationId: integer('best_observation_id'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate] }),
    check('search_cells_discovery_status_ck', isOneOf('discovery_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_verification_status_ck', isOneOf('verification_status', OUTCOME_STATUSES, true)),
    check('search_cells_selected_ck', isBool('verification_selected')),
    check('search_cells_selection_reason_ck', isOneOf('selection_reason', ['always_include', 'top_n', 'ceiling', 'stride'], true)),
    check('search_cells_discovery_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('discovery_min_pence')),
    check('search_cells_verification_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('verification_min_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/cellOutcomes.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, foreignKey, index, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, OUTCOME_STATUSES, PROVIDER_IDS, SOURCE_KINDS, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf, isPointOfSale } from './checks.js';
import { searchCells } from './searchCells.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `cell_outcomes`: per (execution, cell, stage, provider). `attempts` counts engine passes; `reason` never holds a vendor message. */
export const cellOutcomes = sqliteTable(
  'cell_outcomes',
  {
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    stage: text('stage').notNull(),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    status: text('status').notNull(),
    reason: text('reason'),
    attempts: integer('attempts').notNull().default(0),
    lastError: text('last_error'),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at'),
    minPence: integer('min_pence'),
    cabin: text('cabin'),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale'),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.stage, t.providerId] }),
    foreignKey({
      columns: [t.executionId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate],
      foreignColumns: [searchCells.executionId, searchCells.origin, searchCells.destination, searchCells.departureDate, searchCells.returnDate],
    }),
    check('cell_outcomes_status_ck', isOneOf('status', OUTCOME_STATUSES)),
    check('cell_outcomes_stage_ck', isOneOf('stage', ['discovery', 'verification'])),
    check('cell_outcomes_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('cell_outcomes_skip_reason_ck', sql.raw("status <> 'skipped' OR reason IN ('not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed')")),
    check('cell_outcomes_attempts_ck', isInteger('attempts')),
    check('cell_outcomes_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('min_pence')),
    check('cell_outcomes_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_pos_ck', isPointOfSale('point_of_sale', true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_kind_ck', isOneOf('source_kind', SOURCE_KINDS, true)),
    check('cell_outcomes_fetched_ck', isIsoTimestamp('fetched_at', true)),
    index('cell_outcomes_stage_provider_status_idx').on(t.executionId, t.stage, t.providerId, t.status),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/internal/outcomeRow.ts`:

```ts
import type { Cabin, CellOutcome, CellOutcomeStatus, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, PointOfSale, ProviderId, SourceKind, Stage } from '@gmt/core';

/** A `cell_outcomes` row as SQLite returns it; shared by every repository that reads outcomes. */
export interface OutcomeDbRow {
  execution_id: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; stage: Stage; provider_id: ProviderId;
  status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null; attempts: number; last_error: string | null; fetched_at: string | null; min_pence: number | null;
  cabin: Cabin | null; point_of_sale: PointOfSale | null; source_kind: SourceKind | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

export function toOutcome(r: OutcomeDbRow): CellOutcome {
  return {
    executionId: r.execution_id, origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate,
    stage: r.stage, providerId: r.provider_id, status: r.status, reason: r.reason, attempts: r.attempts, lastError: r.last_error, fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime | null,
    minPence: r.min_pence, cabin: r.cabin, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, sourceKind: r.source_kind, reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}

/** WHERE fragment for one cell of one execution, with @executionId/@origin/@destination/@departureDate/@returnDate. */
export const CELL_WHERE = 'execution_id = @executionId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate';
```

`packages/db/src/internal/rollUpSql.ts`:

```ts
import type { Stage } from '@gmt/core';

/**
 * Spec §6.6 roll-up precedence as one correlated UPDATE over `search_cells`, run inside every write
 * that changes outcomes (spec §3 "stored, recomputed with every write"):
 *   0 no outcome rows → NULL; 1 any priced; 2 any not_run; 3 any error; 4 any empty; 5 any no_data;
 *   6 all skipped, reason by budget > provider_disabled > departed > not_selected.
 * `min_pence` is the minimum over priced outcomes; error and no_data roll-ups carry the smallest reason.
 */
export function rollUpUpdateSql(stage: Stage, scope: 'execution' | 'cell'): string {
  const from = `FROM cell_outcomes o WHERE o.execution_id = search_cells.execution_id AND o.origin = search_cells.origin
    AND o.destination = search_cells.destination AND o.departure_date = search_cells.departure_date
    AND o.return_date = search_cells.return_date AND o.stage = '${stage}'`;
  return `UPDATE search_cells SET
  ${stage}_status = (SELECT CASE
      WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'priced') > 0 THEN 'priced'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'not_run') > 0 THEN 'not_run'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'error') > 0 THEN 'error'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'empty') > 0 THEN 'empty'
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'no_data') > 0 THEN 'no_data'
      ELSE 'skipped' END ${from}),
  ${stage}_reason = (SELECT CASE
      WHEN COUNT(*) = 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status IN ('priced', 'not_run')) > 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'error') > 0 THEN MIN(CASE WHEN o.status = 'error' THEN o.reason END)
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'empty') > 0 THEN NULL
      WHEN SUM(o.status = 'no_data') > 0 THEN MIN(CASE WHEN o.status = 'no_data' THEN o.reason END)
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'budget') > 0 THEN 'budget'
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'provider_disabled') > 0 THEN 'provider_disabled'
      WHEN SUM(o.reason = 'departed') > 0 THEN 'departed'
      ELSE 'not_selected' END ${from}),
  ${stage}_min_pence = (SELECT MIN(CASE WHEN o.status = 'priced' THEN o.min_pence END) ${from})
WHERE execution_id = @executionId${scope === 'cell' ? ' AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate' : ''}`;
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (Task 3.9 methods; Tasks 3.11–3.14 add the rest of the class):

```ts
import {
  type Buckets, type Cell, type CellKey, type CellOutcome, type CellOutcomeStatus, type Clock, type IsoDate, type NoDataReason,
  type ProviderId, type RollUp, type Stage, cellKeyString, stripUrlsInText,
} from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { CELL_WHERE, type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';
import { rollUpUpdateSql } from '../internal/rollUpSql.js';
import { RowRejected, assertInt, assertNullableInt, assertNullableIsoDateTime, assertPointOfSale } from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface CellDbRow {
  origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string;
  discovery_status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; discovery_reason: string | null; discovery_min_pence: number | null;
  verification_status: CellOutcomeStatus | null; verification_reason: string | null; verification_min_pence: number | null;
}
type BucketRow = { [K in keyof Buckets]: number | null } & { total: number };

const SKIP_REASONS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['not_selected', 'budget', 'provider_disabled', 'departed']);
const BUCKET_COLUMNS = `SUM(status = 'priced') AS priced, SUM(status = 'empty') AS empty, SUM(status = 'no_data') AS no_data, SUM(status = 'error') AS error,
  SUM(status = 'skipped') AS skipped, SUM(status = 'not_run') AS not_run, SUM(reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL) AS reused, COUNT(*) AS total`;
const toBuckets = (r: BucketRow | undefined): Buckets => ({
  priced: r?.priced ?? 0, empty: r?.empty ?? 0, no_data: r?.no_data ?? 0, error: r?.error ?? 0, skipped: r?.skipped ?? 0, not_run: r?.not_run ?? 0, reused: r?.reused ?? 0,
});
const keyParams = (executionId: string, c: CellKey) => ({ executionId, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate });

function assertSkipReason(status: CellOutcomeStatus | undefined, reason: string | null | undefined): void {
  if (status === 'skipped' && (reason === undefined || reason === null || !SKIP_REASONS.has(reason))) {
    throw new RowRejected('skip-reason', `a skipped outcome needs a skip reason (spec §3), got ${String(reason)}`);
  }
}

/**
 * Spec §7 `search_cells` + `cell_outcomes` and the stored roll-ups. Every method that changes an
 * outcome recomputes the affected roll-ups in the same transaction.
 */
export class CellsRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Spec §7 atomic unit "seeding search_cells + discovery cell_outcomes at start". */
  seed(executionId: string, cells: Cell[], discoveryProviders: ProviderId[]): void {
    const insertCell = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO search_cells (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, discovery_status, verification_selected)
       VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @status, 0)`,
    );
    const insertOutcome = this.db.raw.prepare(
      `INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, attempts)
       VALUES (@executionId, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, 'discovery', @providerId, 'not_run', 0)`,
    );
    const status = discoveryProviders.length > 0 ? 'not_run' : null;
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of cells) {
        const key = keyParams(executionId, c);
        insertCell.run({ ...key, status });
        for (const providerId of discoveryProviders) insertOutcome.run({ ...key, providerId });
      }
    });
  }

  outcomes(executionId: string, filter: { stage?: Stage; providerId?: ProviderId; cells?: CellKey[] } = {}): CellOutcome[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId
           AND (@stage IS NULL OR stage = @stage) AND (@providerId IS NULL OR provider_id = @providerId)
           AND (@keys IS NULL OR (origin || '|' || destination || '|' || departure_date || '|' || return_date) IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@keys)))
         ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id`,
      )
      .all({ executionId, stage: filter.stage ?? null, providerId: filter.providerId ?? null, keys: filter.cells === undefined ? null : JSON.stringify(filter.cells.map(cellKeyString)) })
      .map(toOutcome);
  }

  /** Changes one outcome and its cell's roll-up in one transaction. Identity fields in `patch` are ignored. */
  setOutcome(executionId: string, cell: CellKey, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, patch: Partial<CellOutcome> & { incrementAttempts?: boolean }): void {
    assertSkipReason(patch.status, patch.reason);
    if (patch.minPence !== undefined) assertNullableInt('min_pence', patch.minPence);
    if (patch.attempts !== undefined) assertInt('attempts', patch.attempts);
    if (patch.fetchedAt !== undefined) assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', patch.fetchedAt);
    if (patch.pointOfSale !== undefined && patch.pointOfSale !== null) assertPointOfSale(patch.pointOfSale);
    const sets: string[] = [];
    const params: Record<string, unknown> = { ...keyParams(executionId, cell), stage, providerId };
    const put = (column: string, key: string, value: unknown) => {
      sets.push(`${column} = @${key}`);
      params[key] = value;
    };
    if (patch.status !== undefined) put('status', 'status', patch.status);
    if (patch.reason !== undefined) put('reason', 'reason', patch.reason);
    if (patch.lastError !== undefined) put('last_error', 'lastError', patch.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(patch.lastError));
    if (patch.fetchedAt !== undefined) put('fetched_at', 'fetchedAt', patch.fetchedAt);
    if (patch.minPence !== undefined) put('min_pence', 'minPence', patch.minPence);
    if (patch.cabin !== undefined) put('cabin', 'cabin', patch.cabin);
    if (patch.pointOfSale !== undefined) put('point_of_sale', 'pointOfSale', patch.pointOfSale);
    if (patch.sourceKind !== undefined) put('source_kind', 'sourceKind', patch.sourceKind);
    if (patch.reusedFromExecutionId !== undefined) put('reused_from_execution_id', 'reusedFrom', patch.reusedFromExecutionId);
    if (patch.attempts !== undefined) put('attempts', 'attempts', patch.attempts);
    if (patch.incrementAttempts === true) sets.push('attempts = attempts + 1');
    if (sets.length === 0) return;
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`).run(params).changes;
      if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no ${stage} outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${executionId}`);
      this.recompute(executionId, stage, cell);
    });
  }

  /** Moves every outcome of one (stage, provider) in `from` to `to` (provider disabled, budget skip). Returns the count. */
  setOutcomesForProvider(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, from: CellOutcomeStatus[], to: { status: CellOutcomeStatus; reason: string | null }): number {
    assertSkipReason(to.status, to.reason);
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND status IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@from))`)
        .run({ executionId, stage, providerId, from: JSON.stringify(from), status: to.status, reason: to.reason }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) this.recompute(executionId, stage);
      return changes;
    });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 cabin-not-offered (revision 10): the provider is not called; its not_run outcomes become no_data with the reason. */
  markNoData(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, reason: NoDataReason): number {
    return this.setOutcomesForProvider(executionId, stage, providerId, ['not_run'], { status: 'no_data', reason });
  }

  rollUps(executionId: string): Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }> {
    const rows = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], CellDbRow>('SELECT * FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date').all(executionId);
    const out = new Map<string, { discovery: RollUp; verification: RollUp }>();
    for (const r of rows) {
      out.set(`${r.origin}|${r.destination}|${r.departure_date}|${r.return_date}`, {
        discovery: { status: r.discovery_status, reason: r.discovery_reason, minPence: r.discovery_min_pence },
        verification: { status: r.verification_status, reason: r.verification_reason, minPence: r.verification_min_pence },
      });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** The six buckets of spec §3 plus `reused` for one stage and provider. */
  buckets(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId): Buckets {
    return toBuckets(
      this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, BucketRow>(`SELECT ${BUCKET_COLUMNS} FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`).get({ executionId, stage, providerId }),
    );
  }

  /** Per departure date (View B, spec §10 calendar): buckets per (stage, provider), dates ascending. */
  bucketsPerDay(executionId: string): Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; buckets: Buckets; total: number }>> {
    const rows = this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], BucketRow & { day: IsoDate; stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId }>(
        `SELECT departure_date AS day, stage, provider_id AS providerId, ${BUCKET_COLUMNS} FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ?
         GROUP BY departure_date, stage, provider_id ORDER BY departure_date, stage, provider_id`,
      )
      .all(executionId);
    const out = new Map<IsoDate, Array<{ stage: Stage; providerId: ProviderId; buckets: Buckets; total: number }>>();
    for (const r of rows) {
      const list = out.get(r.day) ?? [];
      list.push({ stage: r.stage, providerId: r.providerId, buckets: toBuckets(r), total: r.total });
      out.set(r.day, list);
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Recomputes stored roll-ups for one cell or the whole execution. Callers are inside a transaction. */
  protected recompute(executionId: string, stage: Stage, cell?: CellKey): void {
    if (cell === undefined) this.db.raw.prepare(rollUpUpdateSql(stage, 'execution')).run({ executionId });
    else this.db.raw.prepare(rollUpUpdateSql(stage, 'cell')).run(keyParams(executionId, cell));
  }
}
```

`packages/db/test/calendarPrices.test.ts`:

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { Db } from '../src/client.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { calendarPrice, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const sqliteCode = (fn: () => unknown): string | null => {
  try {
    fn();
    return null;
  } catch (e) {
    return e instanceof Database.SqliteError ? e.code : `not a SqliteError: ${String(e)}`;
  }
};

function setup(allowSynthetic = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, executionId: execution.id, repo: new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }) };
}

/** Raw insert bypassing the repository, to prove the SQL constraints hold on their own. */
function rawInsert(db: Db, executionId: string, over: Record<string, unknown>): void {
  const row = {
    execution_id: executionId, provider_id: 'travelpayouts', source_kind: 'authorised-cached', cabin: 'economy', origin: 'LHR', destination: 'JFK', departure_date: '2027-03-14',
    return_date: '2027-03-28', price_pence: 41_200, price_native_minor: 41_200, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: null, fx_source: null, fx_converted: 0,
    point_of_sale: 'unknown', fetched_at: T0, ...over,
  };
  const cols = Object.keys(row);
  db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO calendar_prices (${cols.join(', ')}) VALUES (${cols.map((c) => `@${c}`).join(', ')})`).run(row);
}

describe('calendar_prices writes (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7)', () => {
  it('stores provenance explicitly and reads it back: max_age_hours NULL ⇔ unknown, FX columns when converted', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup();
    const converted = calendarPrice(cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'), {
      providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', maxAgeHours: null, pointOfSale: 'GB', currencyNative: 'USD', priceNativeMinor: 54_900, pricePence: 41_175,
      fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 750_000_000, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'frankfurter' }, providerObservedAt: '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z' as IsoDateTime,
    });
    const ids = repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell()), converted]);
    expect(ids).toHaveLength(2);
    const rows = repo.fetchedBy(executionId);
    expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({ id: ids[0], executionId, maxAgeHours: 48, provenance: { providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 48, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'unknown', cabin: 'economy' } });
    expect(rows[0]?.provenance).not.toHaveProperty('providerObservedAt');
    expect(rows[1]).toMatchObject({ maxAgeHours: null, currencyNative: 'USD', provenance: { maxAgeHours: 'unknown', providerObservedAt: '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z', fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 750_000_000, rateDate: '2026-10-13', source: 'frankfurter' } } });
  });

  it('provider ⇔ kind, both directions, in the repository and in SQL', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup(true);
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(write({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'fixture', source_kind: 'authorised-cached' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { provider_id: 'searchapi', source_kind: 'synthetic' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(write({ providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })).not.toThrow(); // allowed under the gate
  });

  it('synthetic rows are refused when allowSynthetic is false', () => {
    const { repo, executionId } = setup(false);
    expect(() => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' })])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }));
  });

  it('FX CHECK constraints, both directions, and price_pence = price_native_minor when unconverted', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'GBP', fx_rate_nano: 1, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 0, currency_native: 'USD' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fx_converted: 1, currency_native: 'USD', fx_rate_nano: null, fx_rate_date: '2026-10-13', fx_source: 'x' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ currencyNative: 'USD' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'fx' }));
    expect(write({ priceNativeMinor: 41_201 })).toThrow(/must equal price_native_minor/);
    expect(write({ fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 1, rateDate: asIsoDate('2026-10-13'), source: 'x' } })).toThrow(/GBP price cannot be FX-converted/);
  });

  it('price tables reject non-integers and non-toISOString timestamps (repository and SQL)', () => {
    const { db, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof calendarPrice>[1]) => () => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), over)]);
    expect(write({ pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(write({ fetchedAt: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' as IsoDateTime })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'timestamp' }));
    expect(write({ maxAgeHours: 1.5 })).toThrow(/max_age_hours/);
    expect(write({ pointOfSale: 'gb' })).toThrow(/point_of_sale/);
    expect(write({ reusedFromExecutionId: 'someone-else' })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reused' }));
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { price_pence: 412.5, price_native_minor: 412.5 }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14T12:00:00Z' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(sqliteCode(() => rawInsert(db, executionId, { fetched_at: '2026-10-14 12:00:00.000' }))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_CHECK');
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('UNIQUE (execution, provider, cell): a second row for the same key throws; another provider or execution does not', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell())]);
    expect(sqliteCode(() => repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })]))).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-aggregate', pointOfSale: 'GB' })]);
    repo.insert(newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' }).execution.id, [calendarPrice(cell())]);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(3);
  });
});

describe('plan Review Focus 5: a SqliteError inside a repository transaction', () => {
  it('propagates with its SQLite code and message, holds no URL, and rolls the whole transaction back', () => {
    const { db, clock, repo, executionId } = setup();
    const link = 'https://www.aviasales.com/search/LON1403NYC28031?marker=SECRET-TOKEN-0123456789';
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      db.transaction(() => {
        repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link })]);
        repo.insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { vendorLink: link })]); // same key
      });
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    const err = thrown as InstanceType<typeof Database.SqliteError>;
    expect(err.code).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
    expect(err.message).toMatch(/^UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices\./);
    expect(err.message).not.toMatch(/https?:|SECRET|marker=/);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
    // the engine records it on the execution (WP7); the column never holds a URL query or userinfo
    const executions = new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic: false });
    executions.setStatus(executionId, 'paused_error', { errorText: `${err.message} while writing tile 3 (${link.replace('https://', 'https://user:pw@')})` });
    const text = executions.get(executionId)?.errorText ?? '';
    expect(text).toContain('UNIQUE constraint failed: calendar_prices.');
    expect(text).not.toMatch(/SECRET|marker=|pw@/);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { sql } from 'drizzle-orm';
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { PROVIDER_IDS, isOneOf, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `calendar_prices`: insert-only, one row per (execution, provider, cell); history reads only rows not reused. */
export const calendarPrices = sqliteTable(
  'calendar_prices',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    vendorLink: text('vendor_link'),
    reusedFromExecutionId: text('reused_from_execution_id').references(() => searchExecutions.id),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('calendar_prices_execution_provider_cell_uq').on(t.executionId, t.providerId, t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate),
    index('calendar_prices_cell_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.departureDate, t.returnDate, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_route_fetched_idx').on(t.origin, t.destination, t.fetchedAt),
    index('calendar_prices_execution_idx').on(t.executionId),
    ...priceTableChecks('calendar_prices'),
    check('calendar_prices_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    // why: spec §5.5 "provider_id = 'fixture' ⇔ source_kind = 'synthetic'", held in SQL as well as in the repository.
    check('calendar_prices_provider_kind_ck', sql.raw("(provider_id = 'fixture') = (source_kind = 'synthetic')")),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/internal/priceRow.ts`:

```ts
import type { Cabin, FxInfo, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, SourceKind } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import type { CalendarPriceInsert, CalendarPriceRow } from '../types.js';
import { RowRejected, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt, assertPointOfSale, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind } from './validate.js';

export interface CalendarPriceDbRow {
  id: number; execution_id: string; provider_id: ProviderId; source_kind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string;
  price_pence: number; price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null; fx_converted: number;
  point_of_sale: PointOfSale; fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null; max_age_hours: number | null; vendor_link: string | null; reused_from_execution_id: string | null;
}

/** Every repository rule for a calendar price (spec §5.5, §6.3, §7). `fresh` refuses a reused marker (reuse goes through copyReused). */
export function validateCalendarPrice(row: CalendarPriceInsert, allowSynthetic: boolean, fresh: boolean): void {
  assertProviderKind(row.providerId, row.sourceKind, allowSynthetic);
  assertPriceMoney(row);
  assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', row.fetchedAt);
  if (row.providerObservedAt !== null) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', row.providerObservedAt);
  assertNullableInt('max_age_hours', row.maxAgeHours);
  assertPointOfSale(row.pointOfSale);
  if (!/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(row.cell.origin) || !/^[A-Z]{3}$/.test(row.cell.destination)) throw new RowRejected('iata', `bad cell airports ${row.cell.origin}→${row.cell.destination}`);
  assertIsoDate('departure_date', row.cell.departureDate);
  assertIsoDate('return_date', row.cell.returnDate);
  if (fresh && row.reusedFromExecutionId !== null) throw new RowRejected('reused', 'a fetched price cannot carry reused_from_execution_id; reuse goes through CellsRepo.copyReused');
}

const INSERT_SQL = `
INSERT INTO calendar_prices (execution_id, provider_id, source_kind, cabin, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, price_pence, price_native_minor,
  currency_native, fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, max_age_hours, vendor_link, reused_from_execution_id)
VALUES (@executionId, @providerId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor,
  @currencyNative, @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @maxAgeHours, @vendorLink, @reusedFrom)`;

/** Inserts one validated row; the caller owns the transaction. `max_age_hours` NULL = unknown, written explicitly (spec §7). */
export function insertCalendarPrice(db: Db, executionId: string, row: CalendarPriceInsert): number {
  const fx = row.fx;
  return Number(
    db.raw.prepare(INSERT_SQL).run({
      executionId, providerId: row.providerId, sourceKind: row.sourceKind, cabin: row.cabin, origin: row.cell.origin, destination: row.cell.destination,
      departureDate: row.cell.departureDate, returnDate: row.cell.returnDate, pricePence: row.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: row.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: row.currencyNative,
      fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
      pointOfSale: row.pointOfSale, fetchedAt: row.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: row.providerObservedAt, maxAgeHours: row.maxAgeHours, vendorLink: row.vendorLink, reusedFrom: row.reusedFromExecutionId,
    }).lastInsertRowid,
  );
}

/** Rebuilds the row and its `Provenance` from the stored columns; nothing is defaulted (spec §3). */
export function toCalendarPriceRow(r: CalendarPriceDbRow): CalendarPriceRow {
  let fx: FxInfo = { converted: false };
  if (r.fx_converted === 1) {
    if (r.fx_rate_nano === null || r.fx_rate_date === null || r.fx_source === null) throw new Error(`calendar price ${r.id} is converted without rate data`);
    fx = { converted: true, rateNano: r.fx_rate_nano, rateDate: r.fx_rate_date as IsoDate, source: r.fx_source };
  }
  const fetchedAt = r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime;
  const providerObservedAt = r.provider_observed_at as IsoDateTime | null;
  const provenance: Provenance = {
    providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, fetchedAt, ...(providerObservedAt === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt }),
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin: r.cabin,
  };
  return {
    id: r.id, executionId: r.execution_id, provenance, providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, cabin: r.cabin,
    cell: { origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate },
    pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native, fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, fetchedAt, providerObservedAt,
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours, vendorLink: r.vendor_link, reusedFromExecutionId: r.reused_from_execution_id,
  };
}
```

`packages/db/src/repositories/calendarPrices.ts` (Task 3.10 part):

```ts
import type { Clock } from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { type CalendarPriceDbRow, insertCalendarPrice, toCalendarPriceRow, validateCalendarPrice } from '../internal/priceRow.js';
import type { CalendarPriceInsert, CalendarPriceRow, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

/** Spec §7 `calendar_prices`. Insert-only except the reused-row supersede (Task 3.14). */
export class CalendarPricesRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Validates every row, then inserts all in one transaction and returns the ids in order. A UNIQUE conflict throws SqliteError. */
  insert(executionId: string, rows: CalendarPriceInsert[]): number[] {
    for (const r of rows) validateCalendarPrice(r, this.opts.allowSynthetic, true);
    return this.db.transaction(() => rows.map((r) => insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, r)));
  }

  /** Prices this execution fetched itself (reused copies excluded): the calendar-basis alert candidates (spec §6.8). */
  fetchedBy(executionId: string): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], CalendarPriceDbRow>('SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ? AND reused_from_execution_id IS NULL ORDER BY id')
      .all(executionId)
      .map(toCalendarPriceRow);
  }
}
```

### Task 3.11: `writeTile` and `writeTileError`: one tile, atomically

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/repositories/cells.ts`
- Create: `test/cells.writeTile.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `CellsRepo.writeTile(executionId, providerId, w: TileWrite)`, `CellsRepo.writeTileError(executionId, providerId, cells, reason, lastError)`.

Rules: a tile's outcomes are `priced | empty | no_data` (a failed tile goes through `writeTileError`); every covered cell has exactly one outcome and every outcome is a covered cell (tiles partition cells, spec §5.2); a `priced` outcome has exactly one price for its cell and its `minPence` equals that price; a price's provider is the tile's provider; `discards` is the engine's count and is not stored (no column); committing a tile resets `auto_resume_count` (spec §7). A tile error keeps the reason (`ProviderError.kind`) in `reason`, the URL-stripped vendor message in `last_error`, and increments `attempts`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```ts
import { type CellKey, type IsoDateTime, cellKeyString, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import Database from 'better-sqlite3';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { TileWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, calendarPrice, cell, newExecution, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const A = cell();
const B = cell(undefined, EWR);
const C = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29');
type TileOutcome = TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
const out = (c: CellKey, status: TileOutcome['status'], minPence: number | null = null): TileOutcome => ({
  ...c, status, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, minPence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached',
});
const tile = (cells: CellKey[], outcomes: TileOutcome[], prices: TileWrite['prices'] = []): TileWrite => ({ tileIndex: 0, cells, prices, outcomes, discards: 0 });
const opts = { allowSynthetic: false };

function setup() {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const cells = new CellsRepo(db, clock, opts);
  cells.seed(execution.id, [A, B, C].map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, cells, executions: new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock, opts) };
}

describe('CellsRepo.writeTile (spec §7 atomic unit "each tile\'s prices, outcomes and roll-ups")', () => {
  it('writes prices, outcomes and roll-ups together and resets auto_resume_count', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, executions } = setup();
    executions.incrementAutoResume(executionId);
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A, B, C], [out(A, 'priced', 39_900), out(B, 'empty'), out(C, 'no_data')], [calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 39_900, priceNativeMinor: 39_900 })]));
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
    const r = cells.rollUps(executionId);
    expect(r.get(cellKeyString(A))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 39_900 });
    expect(r.get(cellKeyString(B))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'empty', reason: null, minPence: null });
    expect(r.get(cellKeyString(C))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'no_data', reason: null, minPence: null });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [A] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', minPence: 39_900, fetchedAt: T0, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached', attempts: 0 });
    expect(executions.get(executionId)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(0);
  });

  it('writeTileError marks exactly its cells error with attempts + 1 per pass; reason is the kind, the message goes to last_error URL-free', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = setup();
    const msg = 'HTTP 503 from https://api.travelpayouts.com/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates?token=SECRET';
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A, B], 'upstream', msg);
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A], 'upstream', msg);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [A] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', attempts: 2, lastError: 'HTTP 503 from https://api.travelpayouts.com/aviasales/v3/prices_for_dates' });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [B] })[0]).toMatchObject({ attempts: 1 });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [C] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'not_run', attempts: 0 });
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(A))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', minPence: null });
  });

  it('roll-ups are atomic: a SQL failure inside the write leaves no partial rows', () => {
    const { db, clock, executionId, cells, executions } = setup();
    executions.incrementAutoResume(executionId);
    new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts).insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(B)]); // a fresh price for B already exists
    let thrown: unknown;
    try {
      cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A, B], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200), out(B, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A), calendarPrice(B)]));
    } catch (e) {
      thrown = e;
    }
    expect(thrown).toBeInstanceOf(Database.SqliteError);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId).map((o) => o.status)).toEqual(['not_run', 'not_run', 'not_run']);
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(A))?.discovery.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(executions.get(executionId)?.autoResumeCount).toBe(1);
  });

  it('a rejection inside the transaction (an unseeded cell) rolls back prices already inserted', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = setup();
    const stranger = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-04-01', '2027-04-15');
    expect(() => cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A, stranger], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200), out(stranger, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A), calendarPrice(stranger)]))).toThrow(
      expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'missing' }),
    );
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('rejects malformed tiles before writing', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = setup();
    const w = (t: TileWrite, providerId: 'travelpayouts' | 'searchapi' = 'travelpayouts') => () => cells.writeTile(executionId, providerId, t);
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 1)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'priced-needs-price' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty')], [calendarPrice(A)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'price-needs-priced' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 1)], [calendarPrice(A)]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'min-pence' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A), calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 1, priceNativeMinor: 1 })]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'in-tile-conflict' }));
    expect(w(tile([A, B], [out(A, 'empty')]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'tile-partition' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'empty'), out(B, 'empty')]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'tile-partition' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'error')]))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'tile-status' }));
    expect(w(tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 41_200)], [calendarPrice(A)]), 'searchapi')).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(0);
  });

  it('monotone merge within one provider: priced > empty > no_data > error (safety net, spec §6.6)', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = setup();
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 40_000)], [calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })]));
    cells.writeTileError(executionId, 'travelpayouts', [A, B], 'network', null);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [A] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', minPence: 40_000, attempts: 1, reason: null });
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([B], [out(B, 'no_data')]));
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [B] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'no_data', reason: null, attempts: 1 });
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([B], [out(B, 'empty')]));
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([B], [out(B, 'no_data')]));
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { cells: [B] })[0]?.status).toBe('empty');
  });

  it('a fresh price supersedes a reused row for the same cell in the same transaction', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId, cells } = setup();
    const origin = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    db.raw
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO calendar_prices (execution_id, provider_id, source_kind, cabin, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, price_pence, price_native_minor, currency_native, fx_converted, point_of_sale, fetched_at, max_age_hours, reused_from_execution_id)
                VALUES (?, 'travelpayouts', 'authorised-cached', 'economy', 'LHR', 'JFK', '2027-03-14', '2027-03-28', 50000, 50000, 'GBP', 0, 'unknown', '2026-10-10T05:00:00.000Z', 48, ?)`)
      .run(executionId, origin);
    cells.writeTile(executionId, 'travelpayouts', tile([A], [out(A, 'priced', 38_000)], [calendarPrice(A, { pricePence: 38_000, priceNativeMinor: 38_000 })]));
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT price_pence AS p, reused_from_execution_id AS r, fetched_at AS f FROM calendar_prices').all()).toEqual([{ p: 38_000, r: null, f: T0 as IsoDateTime }]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `insertCalendarPrice`, `validateCalendarPrice` from `../internal/priceRow.js`; `CalendarPriceInsert`, `TileWrite` from `../types.js`; `Cabin`, `PointOfSale`, `SourceKind` from `@gmt/core`):

```ts
type MergeStatus = 'priced' | 'empty' | 'no_data' | 'error';
const MERGE_RANK: Record<MergeStatus, number> = { priced: 4, empty: 3, no_data: 2, error: 1 };
interface Incoming { status: MergeStatus; reason: string | null; lastError: string | null; fetchedAt: string | null; minPence: number | null; cabin: Cabin | null; pointOfSale: PointOfSale | null; sourceKind: SourceKind | null }

/**
 * Spec §6.6 "Within one provider, monotone merge priced > empty > no_data > error, min_pence = min".
 * Tiles partition cells, so a second result for a cell is a safety net; not_run and skipped are always replaced.
 */
function mergeOutcome(existing: OutcomeDbRow, incoming: Incoming, attempt: boolean): Record<string, unknown> {
  const attempts = existing.attempts + (attempt ? 1 : 0);
  const existingRank = (MERGE_RANK as Partial<Record<string, number>>)[existing.status];
  if (existingRank !== undefined && existingRank > MERGE_RANK[incoming.status]) {
    return {
      status: existing.status, reason: existing.reason, lastError: existing.last_error, fetchedAt: existing.fetched_at, minPence: existing.min_pence,
      cabin: existing.cabin, pointOfSale: existing.point_of_sale, sourceKind: existing.source_kind, attempts, reusedFrom: existing.reused_from_execution_id,
    };
  }
  const minPence = incoming.status === 'priced' && existing.status === 'priced' && existing.min_pence !== null && incoming.minPence !== null ? Math.min(existing.min_pence, incoming.minPence) : incoming.minPence;
  return { ...incoming, lastError: incoming.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(incoming.lastError), minPence, attempts, reusedFrom: null };
}

const UPDATE_MERGED_SQL = `
UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, last_error = @lastError, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence,
  cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, attempts = @attempts, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFrom
WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`;
const SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL = `SELECT * FROM cell_outcomes WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`;
const DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL = `DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND provider_id = @providerId AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`;
const TILE_STATUSES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(['priced', 'empty', 'no_data']);
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one tile's prices, outcomes and roll-ups. Validation first (a malformed tile
   * writes nothing); any SQL failure inside rolls the whole tile back; a fresh price deletes a reused
   * row for the same cell in the same transaction; a committed tile resets `auto_resume_count`.
   */
  writeTile(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, w: TileWrite): void {
    assertInt('tileIndex', w.tileIndex);
    assertInt('discards', w.discards);
    const prices = new Map<string, CalendarPriceInsert>();
    for (const p of w.prices) {
      if (p.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('provider', `price from ${p.providerId} in a ${providerId} tile`);
      const key = cellKeyString(p.cell);
      if (prices.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('in-tile-conflict', `two prices for ${key} in one tile; the engine keeps one per cell (spec §5.6)`);
      validateCalendarPrice(p, this.opts.allowSynthetic, true);
      prices.set(key, p);
    }
    const covered = new Set(w.cells.map(cellKeyString));
    const seen = new Set<string>();
    for (const o of w.outcomes) {
      const key = cellKeyString(o);
      if (!TILE_STATUSES.has(o.status)) throw new RowRejected('tile-status', `a tile outcome is priced, empty or no_data; got ${o.status} (errors go through writeTileError)`);
      if (!covered.has(key) || seen.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('tile-partition', `${key} is outside the tile or repeated`);
      seen.add(key);
      assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', o.fetchedAt);
      const price = prices.get(key);
      if (o.status === 'priced' && price === undefined) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-price', `${key} is priced but the tile wrote no price for it (spec §3)`);
      if (o.status !== 'priced' && price !== undefined) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but its outcome is ${o.status}`);
      if (price !== undefined && o.minPence !== price.pricePence) throw new RowRejected('min-pence', `${key} min_pence ${String(o.minPence)} differs from its price ${price.pricePence}`);
    }
    if (seen.size !== covered.size) throw new RowRejected('tile-partition', 'every covered cell needs exactly one outcome');
    for (const key of prices.keys()) if (!seen.has(key)) throw new RowRejected('price-needs-priced', `${key} has a price but no outcome`);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const p of w.prices) {
        this.db.raw.prepare(DELETE_REUSED_PRICE_SQL).run({ ...keyParams(executionId, p.cell), providerId });
        insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, p);
      }
      for (const o of w.outcomes) {
        const key = { ...keyParams(executionId, o), stage: 'discovery', providerId };
        const existing = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL).get(key);
        if (existing === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded discovery outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyString(o)}`);
        this.db.raw.prepare(UPDATE_MERGED_SQL).run({ ...key, ...mergeOutcome(existing, { ...o, status: o.status as MergeStatus }, false) });
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery', o);
      }
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 error table "tile-error marks exactly its cells": error with the kind as reason, attempts + 1, merged monotonically. */
  writeTileError(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cells: CellKey[], reason: string, lastError: string | null): void {
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const c of cells) {
        const key = { ...keyParams(executionId, c), stage: 'discovery', providerId };
        const existing = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL).get(key);
        if (existing === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded discovery outcome for ${providerId} at ${cellKeyString(c)}`);
        const incoming: Incoming = { status: 'error', reason, lastError, fetchedAt: null, minPence: null, cabin: existing.cabin, pointOfSale: existing.point_of_sale, sourceKind: existing.source_kind };
        this.db.raw.prepare(UPDATE_MERGED_SQL).run({ ...key, ...mergeOutcome(existing, incoming, true) });
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery', c);
      }
    });
  }
```

```ts
function setup(cells: CellKey[], discovery: ProviderId[] = ['searchapi']) {

describe('selection persistence (spec §6.7, §7 atomic unit "selection persistence + verification seeding")', () => {
  it('selected cells get not_run, the rest of allCells skipped:not_selected, roll-ups follow, and selection runs once', () => {

    [12, 'skipped', 'budget', 0, true, true],
```
