Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (import `SelectionReason`, `Iata` from `@gmt/core`):

```ts
/** Spec §7 "Resume": error reasons never retried automatically; a manual resume re-admits auth, quota, parse. */
const NON_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse', 'no-return-legs', 'fx-unavailable', 'currency-unsupported', 'unknown-airport-tz'] as const;
const MANUAL_RETRYABLE = ['auth', 'quota', 'parse'] as const;
const list = (xs: readonly string[]) => xs.map((r) => `'${r}'`).join(', ');

/**
 * Spec §7 resume selection for one (stage, provider). `skipped:budget` is returned here; the engine
 * keeps it only when headroom exists (a budget question, WP7). `priced | empty | no_data` never return.
 */
export const RESUMABLE_SQL = `
SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date FROM cell_outcomes
WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId AND (
  status = 'not_run'
  OR (status = 'error' AND attempts < 3 AND (reason IS NULL OR reason NOT IN (${list(NON_RETRYABLE)})))
  OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'budget')
  OR (@manual = 1 AND ((status = 'skipped' AND reason = 'provider_disabled') OR (status = 'error' AND reason IN (${list(MANUAL_RETRYABLE)}))))
)
ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date`;

interface CellKeyDbRow { origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string }
const toCellKey = (r: CellKeyDbRow): CellKey => ({ origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate });
```

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "selection persistence + verification seeding": marks the selection, seeds one
   * verification outcome per cell of `allCells` × provider (selected → not_run, others → skipped:not_selected)
   * and recomputes roll-ups. Selection runs once per execution (spec §6.7); a second call is refused.
   */
  seedVerification(executionId: string, selected: Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }>, allCells: CellKey[], verificationProviders: ProviderId[]): void {
    const all = new Set(allCells.map(cellKeyString));
    for (const s of selected) if (!all.has(cellKeyString(s.cell))) throw new RowRejected('selection-member', `${cellKeyString(s.cell)} is not among allCells`);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      if (this.hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId)) throw new RowRejected('selection-once', `verification outcomes already exist for ${executionId}; selection runs once per execution`);
      this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 0, selection_reason = NULL WHERE execution_id = ?').run(executionId);
      const mark = this.db.raw.prepare(`UPDATE search_cells SET verification_selected = 1, selection_reason = @reason WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`);
      for (const s of selected) {
        if (mark.run({ ...keyParams(executionId, s.cell), reason: s.reason }).changes !== 1) throw new RowRejected('selection-member', `${cellKeyString(s.cell)} is not a cell of ${executionId}`);
      }
      const seed = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO cell_outcomes (execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, stage, provider_id, status, reason, attempts)
         SELECT execution_id, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, 'verification', @providerId,
                CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN 'not_run' ELSE 'skipped' END, CASE WHEN verification_selected = 1 THEN NULL ELSE 'not_selected' END, 0
         FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = @executionId
           AND (origin || '|' || destination || '|' || departure_date || '|' || return_date) IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@keys))`,
      );
      const keys = JSON.stringify([...all]);
      for (const providerId of verificationProviders) seed.run({ executionId, providerId, keys });
      this.recompute(executionId, 'verification');
    });
  }

  selected(executionId: string): Array<{ cell: CellKey; reason: SelectionReason }> {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<[string], CellKeyDbRow & { selection_reason: SelectionReason }>(
        'SELECT origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, selection_reason FROM search_cells WHERE execution_id = ? AND verification_selected = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date',
      )
      .all(executionId)
      .map((r) => ({ cell: toCellKey(r), reason: r.selection_reason }));
  }

  /** Spec §7: resume re-runs selection only when no verification outcomes exist. */
  hasVerificationOutcomes(executionId: string): boolean {
    return this.db.raw.prepare(`SELECT 1 AS present FROM cell_outcomes WHERE execution_id = ? AND stage = 'verification' LIMIT 1`).get(executionId) !== undefined;
  }

  resumable(executionId: string, stage: Stage, providerId: ProviderId, manual: boolean): CellKey[] {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CellKeyDbRow>(RESUMABLE_SQL).all({ executionId, stage, providerId, manual: manual ? 1 : 0 }).map(toCellKey);
  }

  /** Spec §8.1: not_run and skipped outcomes of cells departing on or before `today` become skipped:departed; priced/empty/no_data untouched. */
  markDeparted(executionId: string, today: IsoDate): number {
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = 'skipped', reason = 'departed'
           WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND departure_date <= @today AND (status = 'not_run' OR (status = 'skipped' AND reason <> 'departed'))`,
        )
        .run({ executionId, today }).changes;
      if (changes > 0) {
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery');
        this.recompute(executionId, 'verification');
      }
      return changes;
    });
  }
```

```ts
import type { NormalisedItinerary } from '@gmt/core';

/** A nonstop round trip on the worked example; `hash` stands in for WP2's sha256 hex. */
export function itinerary(hash = 'a'.repeat(64), c: CellKey = cell(), flight = 117): NormalisedItinerary {
  return {
    hash, origin: c.origin, destination: c.destination, departureDate: c.departureDate, returnDate: c.returnDate, nights: withNights(c).nights,
    outbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight, from: c.origin, to: c.destination, departLocal: `${c.departureDate}T08:25`, arriveLocal: `${c.departureDate}T11:20`, durationMinutes: 475 }],
    inbound: [{ marketingCarrier: 'BA', flightNumber: flight - 5, from: c.destination, to: c.origin, departLocal: `${c.returnDate}T18:30`, arriveLocal: `${c.returnDate}T06:35`, durationMinutes: 425 }],
    outboundMinutes: 475, inboundMinutes: 425, stopsOut: 0, stopsIn: 0, layovers: [], airportChange: false, marketingCarriers: ['BA'],
  };
```

```ts
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';

import { ItinerariesRepo } from '../src/repositories/itineraries.js';

const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;
const offerOf = (hash = 'a'.repeat(64), seller = 'British Airways') => ({ itineraryHash: hash, providerId: 'searchapi' as const, seller, cabin: 'economy' as const });

function setup(allowSynthetic = false) {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, repo: new ItinerariesRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }), cells: new CellsRepo(db, clock, { allowSynthetic }) };
}
const insertObs = (repo: ItinerariesRepo, executionId: string, offerId: number, over: Parameters<typeof observationWrite>[0] = {}) =>
  repo.insertObservation({ ...observationWrite(over), offerId, executionId });

describe('itineraries and offers (spec §7)', () => {
  it('itineraries and segments are insert-or-ignore on hash; get rebuilds the whole itinerary', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    repo.upsertItinerary({ ...itinerary(), nights: 99 });
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments')]).toEqual([1, 2]);
    expect(repo.get('a'.repeat(64))).toEqual({ ...itinerary(), firstSeenAt: T0 });
    expect(repo.get('missing')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('UNIQUE (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin): upsertOffer returns the same id; a raw duplicate throws', () => {
    const { db, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const id = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf())).toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer(offerOf(undefined, 'Mytrip'))).not.toBe(id);
    expect(repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), cabin: 'business' })).not.toBe(id);
    let code: string | null = null;
    try {
      db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (?, 'searchapi', 'British Airways', 'economy', ?, ?)`).run('a'.repeat(64), T0, T0);
    } catch (e) {
      code = e instanceof Database.SqliteError ? e.code : null;
    }
    expect(code).toBe('SQLITE_CONSTRAINT_UNIQUE');
  });

  it('latest = max fetched_at, ties broken by id; first, lowest, highest follow; previousObservation looks strictly before', () => {
    const { db, executionId, repo } = setup();
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const obs = (fetchedAt: string, pence: number) => insertObs(repo, executionId, offerId, { pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence, provenance: { ...observationWrite().provenance, fetchedAt: at(fetchedAt) } });
    obs('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', 50_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 45_000);
    obs('2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z', 40_000); // inserted later, fetched earlier
    const read = () => db.raw.prepare('SELECT first_price_pence AS f, lowest_price_pence AS l, highest_price_pence AS h, latest_price_pence AS x, first_seen_at AS fs, last_seen_at AS ls FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(offerId);
    expect(read()).toEqual({ f: 50_000, l: 40_000, h: 50_000, x: 45_000, fs: '2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z', ls: '2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z' });
    obs('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z', 47_000); // same fetched_at as the latest: the higher id wins
    expect(read()).toMatchObject({ x: 47_000 });
    expect(repo.previousObservation(offerId, at('2026-10-14T12:00:00.000Z'))?.pricePence).toBe(40_000);
    expect(repo.previousObservation(offerId, at('2026-10-14T10:00:00.000Z'))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('observations reject provider ⇔ kind, the synthetic gate, an offer mismatch, disagreeing fx, non-integers and bad timestamps', () => {
    const { executionId, repo } = setup(false);
    repo.upsertItinerary(itinerary());
    const offerId = repo.upsertOffer(offerOf());
    const base = observationWrite().provenance;
    const write = (over: Parameters<typeof observationWrite>[0]) => () => insertObs(repo, executionId, offerId, over);
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base, sourceKind: 'synthetic' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'provider-kind' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base, cabin: 'business' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base, providerId: 'travelpayouts', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offer-mismatch' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base, fx: { converted: true, rateNano: 1, rateDate: '2026-10-13' as never, source: 'x' } } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'fx-mismatch' }));
    expect(write({ pricePence: 412.5, priceNativeMinor: 412.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
    expect(write({ provenance: { ...base, fetchedAt: at('2026-10-14T12:00:00+00:00') } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'timestamp' }));
    const fixtureOffer = repo.upsertOffer({ ...offerOf(), providerId: 'fixture' });
    expect(() => insertObs(repo, executionId, fixtureOffer, { provenance: { ...base, providerId: 'fixture', sourceKind: 'synthetic' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }));
  });
});

describe('CellsRepo.writeVerifiedCell (spec §7 atomic unit "each verified cell")', () => {
  function seeded() {
    const s = setup();
    const all = [cell(), cell(undefined, EWR)];
    s.cells.seed(s.executionId, all.map(withNights), []);
    s.cells.seedVerification(s.executionId, [{ cell: cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], all, ['searchapi']);
    return s;
  }
  const write = (c: CellKey, offers: Array<{ hash: string; seller: string; pence: number }>, status: 'priced' | 'empty' = 'priced'): VerifiedCellWrite => {
    const pences = offers.map((o) => o.pence);
    const hashes = [...new Set(offers.map((o) => o.hash))];
    return {
      cell: c,
      outcome: { ...c, status, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, minPence: pences.length > 0 ? Math.min(...pences) : null, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
      itineraries: hashes.map((h) => ({ itinerary: itinerary(h, c), offers: offers.filter((o) => o.hash === h).map((o) => ({ offer: offerOf(h, o.seller), observation: observationWrite({ pricePence: o.pence, priceNativeMinor: o.pence }) })) })),
      bestObservationSelector: 'cheapest-verified-or-partial',
    };
  };

  it('writes itinerary, segments, offers, observations, outcome, best_observation_id and roll-up together', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells, repo } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]));
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments'), count(db, 'fare_offers'), count(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([1, 2, 2, 2]);
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 57_300 });
    const best = repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell());
    expect(best).not.toBeNull();
    expect(repo.writtenBy(executionId).find((o) => o.id === best)?.pricePence).toBe(57_300);
    expect(repo.bestObservationForCell(executionId, cell(undefined, EWR))).toBeNull();
  });

  it('a failure on the second offer leaves no itinerary, offer, observation or outcome change', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    const w = write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'British Airways', pence: 60_300 }, { hash: 'b'.repeat(64), seller: 'Mytrip', pence: 57_300 }]);
    const second = w.itineraries[1]?.offers[0];
    if (second === undefined) throw new Error('fixture has two itineraries');
    second.observation = { ...second.observation, pricePence: 573.5, priceNativeMinor: 573.5 };
    w.outcome = { ...w.outcome, minPence: 573.5 }; // passes the pre-check, so the integer rule fires inside the transaction, after the first itinerary was written
    expect(() => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', w)).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect([count(db, 'itineraries'), count(db, 'segments'), count(db, 'fare_offers'), count(db, 'price_observations')]).toEqual([0, 0, 0, 0]);
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
  });

  it('rejects priced without offers, offers on a non-priced outcome, an itinerary for another cell, and a wrong min_pence', () => {
    const { executionId, cells } = seeded();
    const w = (v: VerifiedCellWrite) => () => cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', v);
    expect(w(write(cell(), []))).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'priced-needs-offer' }));
    expect(w({ ...write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 1 }]), outcome: { ...write(cell(), []).outcome, status: 'empty' } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'offers-need-priced' }));
    expect(w({ ...write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 1 }]), cell: cell(undefined, EWR) })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'cell-mismatch' }));
    const bad = write(cell(), [{ hash: 'a'.repeat(64), seller: 'x', pence: 100 }]);
    expect(w({ ...bad, outcome: { ...bad.outcome, minPence: 99 } })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'min-pence' }));
  });

  it('an empty verified cell records the outcome with no rows; an error outcome counts an attempt', () => {
    const { db, executionId, cells } = seeded();
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', write(cell(), [], 'empty'));
    expect(cells.rollUps(executionId).get(cellKeyString(cell()))?.verification.status).toBe('empty');
    expect(count(db, 'price_observations')).toBe(0);
    const e = write(cell(), [], 'empty');
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', { ...e, outcome: { ...e.outcome, status: 'error', reason: 'upstream', lastError: 'no-return-legs https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search?api_key=SECRET' } });
    expect(cells.outcomes(executionId, { stage: 'verification', cells: [cell()] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'error', attempts: 1, lastError: 'no-return-legs https://www.searchapi.io/api/v1/search' });
  });
});

describe('ItinerariesRepo reads', () => {
  it('observationsForDefinition: execution set, current space, cabin, children; writtenBy; offerHistory; latest verification outcome', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId, cells, repo } = setup();
    cells.seed(executionId, [withNights(cell())], []);
    cells.seedVerification(executionId, [{ cell: cell(), reason: 'top_n' }], [cell()], ['searchapi']);
    const w = (c: CellKey, hash: string, pence: number): VerifiedCellWrite => ({
      cell: c, outcome: { ...c, status: 'priced', reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt: T0, minPence: pence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'GB', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
      itineraries: [{ itinerary: itinerary(hash, c), offers: [{ offer: offerOf(hash), observation: observationWrite({ pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence }) }] }],
      bestObservationSelector: 'cheapest-verified-or-partial',
    });
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(executionId, 'searchapi', w(cell(), 'a'.repeat(64), 41_200));
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: cell(undefined, EWR) }).execution;
    cells.seed(child.id, [withNights(cell(undefined, EWR))], []);
    cells.seedVerification(child.id, [{ cell: cell(undefined, EWR), reason: 'always_include' }], [cell(undefined, EWR)], ['searchapi']);
    cells.writeVerifiedCell(child.id, 'searchapi', w(cell(undefined, EWR), 'c'.repeat(64), 39_000));
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    const all = repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true });
    expect(all.map((r) => [r.executionId, r.pricePence, r.cell.destination, r.cell.nights, r.seller])).toEqual([[child.id, 39_000, 'EWR', 14, 'British Airways'], [executionId, 41_200, 'JFK', 14, 'British Airways']]);
    expect(all[0]?.provenance).toEqual({ providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search', fetchedAt: T0, maxAgeHours: 0, fx: { converted: false }, pointOfSale: 'GB', cabin: 'economy' });
    expect(all[0]?.itinerary).toEqual(itinerary('c'.repeat(64), cell(undefined, EWR)));
    expect(all[0]?.offer).toMatchObject({ itineraryHash: 'c'.repeat(64), providerId: 'searchapi', seller: 'British Airways', cabin: 'economy', id: expect.any(Number) });
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: false })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: [executionId], space: null, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space, cabin: 'business', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(repo.observationsForDefinition(definition.id, { executionIds: 'all', space: { ...space, maxNights: 13 }, cabin: 'economy', includeChildren: true })).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(repo.writtenBy(child.id).map((o) => o.pricePence)).toEqual([39_000]);
    expect(repo.offerHistory('a'.repeat(64))).toEqual([{ providerId: 'searchapi', seller: 'British Airways', cabin: 'economy', points: [{ at: T0, pricePence: 41_200, provenance: all[1]?.provenance }] }]);
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell())).toMatchObject({ executionId, status: 'priced' });
    expect(repo.latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definition.id, cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-01-01', '2027-01-15'))).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { check, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, isInteger, isNullOrInteger } from './checks.js';

/** Spec §7 `itineraries`: cabin-independent flights, identity by hash (spec §6.4). Insert-or-ignore. */
export const itineraries = sqliteTable(
  'itineraries',
  {
    hash: text('hash').primaryKey(),
    kind: text('kind').notNull().default('flight'),
    origin: text('origin').notNull(),
    destination: text('destination').notNull(),
    departureDate: text('departure_date').notNull(),
    returnDate: text('return_date').notNull(),
    nights: integer('nights').notNull(),
    outboundMinutes: integer('outbound_minutes'),
    inboundMinutes: integer('inbound_minutes'),
    stopsOut: integer('stops_out').notNull(),
    stopsIn: integer('stops_in').notNull(),
    airportChange: integer('airport_change'),
    carriersJson: text('carriers_json').notNull(),
    layoversJson: text('layovers_json').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
  },
  () => [
    check('itineraries_nights_ck', isInteger('nights')),
    check('itineraries_stops_out_ck', isInteger('stops_out')),
    check('itineraries_stops_in_ck', isInteger('stops_in')),
    check('itineraries_out_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('outbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_in_min_ck', isNullOrInteger('inbound_minutes')),
    check('itineraries_airport_change_ck', isBool('airport_change', true)),
    check('itineraries_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
  ],
);
```

```ts
import { check, integer, primaryKey, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isInteger, isIsoTimestamp, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `segments`: no cabin (segments belong to the cabin-independent itinerary). */
export const segments = sqliteTable(
  'segments',
  {
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    direction: text('direction').notNull(),
    position: integer('position').notNull(),
    mode: text('mode').notNull().default('air'),
    marketingCarrier: text('marketing_carrier').notNull(),
    operatingCarrier: text('operating_carrier'),
    flightNumber: integer('flight_number').notNull(),
    fromIata: text('from_iata').notNull(),
    toIata: text('to_iata').notNull(),
    departLocal: text('depart_local').notNull(),
    arriveLocal: text('arrive_local').notNull(),
    departUtc: text('depart_utc'),
    arriveUtc: text('arrive_utc'),
    durationMinutes: integer('duration_minutes'),
    aircraft: text('aircraft'),
  },
  (t) => [
    primaryKey({ columns: [t.itineraryHash, t.direction, t.position] }),
    check('segments_direction_ck', isOneOf('direction', ['outbound', 'inbound'])),
    check('segments_position_ck', isInteger('position')),
    check('segments_flight_number_ck', isInteger('flight_number')),
    check('segments_duration_ck', isNullOrInteger('duration_minutes')),
    check('segments_depart_utc_ck', isIsoTimestamp('depart_utc', true)),
    check('segments_arrive_utc_ck', isIsoTimestamp('arrive_utc', true)),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/fareOffers.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text, uniqueIndex } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { CABINS, PROVIDER_IDS, isIsoTimestamp, isNullOrInteger, isOneOf } from './checks.js';
import { itineraries } from './itineraries.js';

/** Spec §7 `fare_offers`: (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin). Price aggregates are recomputed from observations. */
export const fareOffers = sqliteTable(
  'fare_offers',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    itineraryHash: text('itinerary_hash').notNull().references(() => itineraries.hash),
    providerId: text('provider_id').notNull(),
    seller: text('seller').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    firstSeenAt: text('first_seen_at').notNull(),
    lastSeenAt: text('last_seen_at').notNull(),
    firstPricePence: integer('first_price_pence'),
    lowestPricePence: integer('lowest_price_pence'),
    highestPricePence: integer('highest_price_pence'),
    latestPricePence: integer('latest_price_pence'),
  },
  (t) => [
    uniqueIndex('fare_offers_identity_uq').on(t.itineraryHash, t.providerId, t.seller, t.cabin),
    index('fare_offers_itinerary_idx').on(t.itineraryHash),
    check('fare_offers_provider_ck', isOneOf('provider_id', PROVIDER_IDS)),
    check('fare_offers_cabin_ck', isOneOf('cabin', CABINS)),
    check('fare_offers_first_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('first_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_last_seen_ck', isIsoTimestamp('last_seen_at')),
    check('fare_offers_first_ck', isNullOrInteger('first_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_lowest_ck', isNullOrInteger('lowest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_highest_ck', isNullOrInteger('highest_price_pence')),
    check('fare_offers_latest_ck', isNullOrInteger('latest_price_pence')),
  ],
);
```

`packages/db/src/schema/priceObservations.ts`:

```ts
import { check, index, integer, sqliteTable, text } from 'drizzle-orm/sqlite-core';
import { isBool, isIsoTimestamp, priceTableChecks } from './checks.js';
import { fareOffers } from './fareOffers.js';
import { searchExecutions } from './searchExecutions.js';

/** Spec §7 `price_observations`: append-only; verdicts are recomputed at read time from `supplied_fields_json`. */
export const priceObservations = sqliteTable(
  'price_observations',
  {
    id: integer('id').primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),
    offerId: integer('offer_id').notNull().references(() => fareOffers.id),
    executionId: text('execution_id').notNull().references(() => searchExecutions.id),
    sourceKind: text('source_kind').notNull(),
    cabin: text('cabin').notNull(),
    fetchedAt: text('fetched_at').notNull(),
    providerObservedAt: text('provider_observed_at'),
    pricePence: integer('price_pence').notNull(),
    priceNativeMinor: integer('price_native_minor').notNull(),
    currencyNative: text('currency_native').notNull(),
    fxRateNano: integer('fx_rate_nano'),
    fxRateDate: text('fx_rate_date'),
    fxSource: text('fx_source'),
    fxConverted: integer('fx_converted').notNull(),
    pointOfSale: text('point_of_sale').notNull(),
    maxAgeHours: integer('max_age_hours'),
    taxesIncluded: integer('taxes_included'),
    selfTransfer: integer('self_transfer'),
    segmentCabinsJson: text('segment_cabins_json'),
    baggageJson: text('baggage_json'),
    fareConditionsJson: text('fare_conditions_json'),
    bookingJson: text('booking_json'),
    bookingExpiresAt: text('booking_expires_at'),
    suppliedFieldsJson: text('supplied_fields_json').notNull(),
    partialExpansion: integer('partial_expansion').notNull(),
  },
  (t) => [
    index('price_observations_execution_price_idx').on(t.executionId, t.pricePence),
    index('price_observations_offer_fetched_idx').on(t.offerId, t.fetchedAt),
    ...priceTableChecks('price_observations'),
    check('price_observations_taxes_ck', isBool('taxes_included', true)),
    check('price_observations_self_transfer_ck', isBool('self_transfer', true)),
    check('price_observations_partial_ck', isBool('partial_expansion')),
    check('price_observations_expires_ck', isIsoTimestamp('booking_expires_at', true)),
  ],
);
```

In `src/schema/searchCells.ts` change the column to `bestObservationId: integer('best_observation_id').references(() => priceObservations.id),` with `import { priceObservations } from './priceObservations.js';`, and add the four tables to `schema/index.ts`.

`packages/db/src/repositories/itineraries.ts`:

```ts
import type {
  BaggageSnapshot, Booking, Cabin, CellKey, CellOutcome, Clock, FareConditions, FxInfo, HistoryPoint, Iata, IsoDate, IsoDateTime, Layover,
  NormalisedItinerary, Observation, Offer, PointOfSale, ProviderId, Provenance, ResolvedSpace, Segment, SourceKind, SuppliedField,
} from '@gmt/core';
import type { Db } from '../client.js';
import { CELL_WHERE, type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome } from '../internal/outcomeRow.js';
import {
  RowRejected, assertInt, assertIsoDate, assertIsoDateTime, assertNullableInt, assertNullableIsoDateTime, assertPointOfSale, assertPriceMoney, assertProviderKind,
} from '../internal/validate.js';
import type { ObservationRow, RepoOptions } from '../types.js';

interface ObservationDbRow {
  id: number; offer_id: number; execution_id: string; source_kind: SourceKind; cabin: Cabin; fetched_at: string; provider_observed_at: string | null; price_pence: number;
  price_native_minor: number; currency_native: string; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null; fx_converted: number;
  point_of_sale: PointOfSale; max_age_hours: number | null; taxes_included: number | null; self_transfer: number | null; segment_cabins_json: string | null;
  baggage_json: string | null; fare_conditions_json: string | null; booking_json: string | null; booking_expires_at: string | null; supplied_fields_json: string;
  partial_expansion: number; itinerary_hash: string; provider_id: ProviderId; seller: string;
}
interface ItineraryDbRow {
  hash: string; origin: string; destination: string; departure_date: string; return_date: string; nights: number; outbound_minutes: number | null; inbound_minutes: number | null;
  stops_out: number; stops_in: number; airport_change: number | null; carriers_json: string; layovers_json: string; first_seen_at: string;
}
interface SegmentDbRow {
  direction: 'outbound' | 'inbound'; marketing_carrier: string; operating_carrier: string | null; flight_number: number; from_iata: string; to_iata: string;
  depart_local: string; arrive_local: string; depart_utc: string | null; arrive_utc: string | null; duration_minutes: number | null; aircraft: string | null;
}

const nullableBool = (v: number | null): boolean | null => (v === null ? null : v === 1);
const parseOr = <T>(json: string | null, fallback: T): T => (json === null ? fallback : (JSON.parse(json) as T));
const boolOrNull = (v: boolean | null) => (v === null ? null : v ? 1 : 0);

function fxOf(r: { id: number; fx_converted: number; fx_rate_nano: number | null; fx_rate_date: string | null; fx_source: string | null }): FxInfo {
  if (r.fx_converted !== 1) return { converted: false };
  if (r.fx_rate_nano === null || r.fx_rate_date === null || r.fx_source === null) throw new Error(`observation ${r.id} is converted without rate data`);
  return { converted: true, rateNano: r.fx_rate_nano, rateDate: r.fx_rate_date as IsoDate, source: r.fx_source };
}

function toObservation(r: ObservationDbRow): Observation {
  const fx = fxOf(r);
  const providerObservedAt = r.provider_observed_at as IsoDateTime | null;
  const provenance: Provenance = {
    providerId: r.provider_id, sourceKind: r.source_kind, fetchedAt: r.fetched_at as IsoDateTime, ...(providerObservedAt === null ? {} : { providerObservedAt }),
    maxAgeHours: r.max_age_hours ?? 'unknown', fx, pointOfSale: r.point_of_sale, cabin: r.cabin,
  };
  return {
    id: r.id, offerId: r.offer_id, executionId: r.execution_id, provenance, pricePence: r.price_pence, priceNativeMinor: r.price_native_minor, currencyNative: r.currency_native, fx,
    taxesIncluded: nullableBool(r.taxes_included), selfTransfer: nullableBool(r.self_transfer), segmentCabins: parseOr<Cabin[] | null>(r.segment_cabins_json, null),
    baggage: parseOr<BaggageSnapshot | null>(r.baggage_json, null), fareConditions: parseOr<FareConditions | null>(r.fare_conditions_json, null),
    booking: parseOr<Booking>(r.booking_json, { kind: 'none' }), bookingExpiresAt: r.booking_expires_at as IsoDateTime | null,
    suppliedFields: JSON.parse(r.supplied_fields_json) as SuppliedField[], partialExpansion: r.partial_expansion === 1,
  };
}

function toSegment(s: SegmentDbRow): Segment {
  return {
    marketingCarrier: s.marketing_carrier, ...(s.operating_carrier === null ? {} : { operatingCarrier: s.operating_carrier }), flightNumber: s.flight_number,
    from: s.from_iata as Iata, to: s.to_iata as Iata, departLocal: s.depart_local, arriveLocal: s.arrive_local,
    ...(s.depart_utc === null ? {} : { departUtc: s.depart_utc as IsoDateTime }), ...(s.arrive_utc === null ? {} : { arriveUtc: s.arrive_utc as IsoDateTime }),
    ...(s.duration_minutes === null ? {} : { durationMinutes: s.duration_minutes }), ...(s.aircraft === null ? {} : { aircraft: s.aircraft }),
  };
}

const OBSERVATION_JOIN = `FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id JOIN itineraries i ON i.hash = f.itinerary_hash JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id`;
const OBSERVATION_COLUMNS = 'o.*, f.itinerary_hash, f.provider_id, f.seller';

const RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL = `
UPDATE fare_offers SET
  first_seen_at = (SELECT MIN(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  last_seen_at = (SELECT MAX(fetched_at) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  first_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at ASC, id ASC LIMIT 1),
  lowest_price_pence = (SELECT MIN(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  highest_price_pence = (SELECT MAX(price_pence) FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id),
  latest_price_pence = (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE offer_id = @id ORDER BY fetched_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1)
WHERE id = @id`;

/** Spec §7 itineraries, segments, fare offers and price observations. */
export class ItinerariesRepo {
  constructor(
    protected readonly db: Db,
    protected readonly clock: Clock,
    protected readonly opts: RepoOptions,
  ) {}

  /** Insert-or-ignore on hash, with its segments and layovers, in one transaction (spec §7). */
  upsertItinerary(it: NormalisedItinerary): void {
    if (it.hash.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('hash', 'itinerary hash is empty');
    for (const [f, v] of [['nights', it.nights], ['stops_out', it.stopsOut], ['stops_in', it.stopsIn]] as const) assertInt(f, v);
    assertNullableInt('outbound_minutes', it.outboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertNullableInt('inbound_minutes', it.inboundMinutes ?? null);
    assertIsoDate('departure_date', it.departureDate);
    assertIsoDate('return_date', it.returnDate);
    const segs = [...it.outbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'outbound', position })), ...it.inbound.map((s, position) => ({ s, direction: 'inbound', position }))];
    for (const { s } of segs) {
      assertInt('flight_number', s.flightNumber);
      assertNullableInt('duration_minutes', s.durationMinutes ?? null);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `INSERT INTO itineraries (hash, kind, origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, nights, outbound_minutes, inbound_minutes, stops_out, stops_in, airport_change, carriers_json, layovers_json, first_seen_at)
           VALUES (@hash, 'flight', @origin, @destination, @departureDate, @returnDate, @nights, @outboundMinutes, @inboundMinutes, @stopsOut, @stopsIn, @airportChange, @carriers, @layovers, @now)
           ON CONFLICT(hash) DO NOTHING`,
        )
        .run({
          hash: it.hash, origin: it.origin, destination: it.destination, departureDate: it.departureDate, returnDate: it.returnDate, nights: it.nights,
          outboundMinutes: it.outboundMinutes ?? null, inboundMinutes: it.inboundMinutes ?? null, stopsOut: it.stopsOut, stopsIn: it.stopsIn,
          airportChange: it.airportChange === undefined ? null : it.airportChange ? 1 : 0, carriers: JSON.stringify(it.marketingCarriers), layovers: JSON.stringify(it.layovers), now: this.clock.now(),
        });
      const insertSegment = this.db.raw.prepare(
        `INSERT INTO segments (itinerary_hash, direction, position, mode, marketing_carrier, operating_carrier, flight_number, from_iata, to_iata, depart_local, arrive_local, depart_utc, arrive_utc, duration_minutes, aircraft)
         VALUES (@hash, @direction, @position, 'air', @marketingCarrier, @operatingCarrier, @flightNumber, @from, @to, @departLocal, @arriveLocal, @departUtc, @arriveUtc, @durationMinutes, @aircraft)
         ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, direction, position) DO NOTHING`,
      );
      for (const { s, direction, position } of segs) {
        insertSegment.run({
          hash: it.hash, direction, position, marketingCarrier: s.marketingCarrier, operatingCarrier: s.operatingCarrier ?? null, flightNumber: s.flightNumber, from: s.from, to: s.to,
          departLocal: s.departLocal, arriveLocal: s.arriveLocal, departUtc: s.departUtc ?? null, arriveUtc: s.arriveUtc ?? null, durationMinutes: s.durationMinutes ?? null, aircraft: s.aircraft ?? null,
        });
      }
    });
  }

  /** The stored itinerary with its segments, or null. */
  get(hash: string): (NormalisedItinerary & { firstSeenAt: IsoDateTime }) | null {
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ItineraryDbRow>('SELECT * FROM itineraries WHERE hash = ?').get(hash);
    if (r === undefined) return null;
    const segs = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], SegmentDbRow>('SELECT * FROM segments WHERE itinerary_hash = ? ORDER BY direction DESC, position').all(hash);
    return {
      hash: r.hash, origin: r.origin as Iata, destination: r.destination as Iata, departureDate: r.departure_date as IsoDate, returnDate: r.return_date as IsoDate, nights: r.nights,
      outbound: segs.filter((s) => s.direction === 'outbound').map(toSegment), inbound: segs.filter((s) => s.direction === 'inbound').map(toSegment),
      ...(r.outbound_minutes === null ? {} : { outboundMinutes: r.outbound_minutes }), ...(r.inbound_minutes === null ? {} : { inboundMinutes: r.inbound_minutes }),
      stopsOut: r.stops_out, stopsIn: r.stops_in, layovers: JSON.parse(r.layovers_json) as Layover[], ...(r.airport_change === null ? {} : { airportChange: r.airport_change === 1 }),
      marketingCarriers: JSON.parse(r.carriers_json) as string[], firstSeenAt: r.first_seen_at as IsoDateTime,
    };
  }

  /** Returns the offer id for (itinerary, provider, seller, cabin), creating it if absent. */
  upsertOffer(offer: Offer): number {
    if (offer.seller.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('seller', 'seller is required (spec §7 NOT NULL)');
    const now = this.clock.now();
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(`INSERT INTO fare_offers (itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin, first_seen_at, last_seen_at) VALUES (@itineraryHash, @providerId, @seller, @cabin, @now, @now) ON CONFLICT(itinerary_hash, provider_id, seller, cabin) DO NOTHING`)
      .run({ ...offer, now });
    const r = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { id: number }>('SELECT id FROM fare_offers WHERE itinerary_hash = @itineraryHash AND provider_id = @providerId AND seller = @seller AND cabin = @cabin').get({ ...offer });
    if (r === undefined) throw new Error('fare offer vanished after upsert');
    return r.id;
  }

  /** Appends one observation and recomputes the offer's first/lowest/highest/latest (latest = max fetched_at, tie id). */
  insertObservation(obs: Omit<Observation, 'id'>): number {
    const offer = this.db.raw.prepare<[number], { provider_id: ProviderId; cabin: Cabin }>('SELECT provider_id, cabin FROM fare_offers WHERE id = ?').get(obs.offerId);
    if (offer === undefined) throw new RowRejected('missing', `fare offer ${obs.offerId} does not exist`);
    const p = obs.provenance;
    assertProviderKind(p.providerId, p.sourceKind, this.opts.allowSynthetic);
    if (p.providerId !== offer.provider_id || p.cabin !== offer.cabin) throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `observation ${p.providerId}/${p.cabin} does not match offer ${offer.provider_id}/${offer.cabin}`);
    if (JSON.stringify(p.fx) !== JSON.stringify(obs.fx)) throw new RowRejected('fx-mismatch', 'observation fx and provenance fx disagree');
    assertPriceMoney(obs);
    assertIsoDateTime('fetched_at', p.fetchedAt);
    if (p.providerObservedAt !== undefined) assertIsoDateTime('provider_observed_at', p.providerObservedAt);
    assertNullableIsoDateTime('booking_expires_at', obs.bookingExpiresAt);
    if (p.maxAgeHours !== 'unknown') assertInt('max_age_hours', p.maxAgeHours);
    assertPointOfSale(p.pointOfSale);
    const fx = obs.fx;
    return this.db.transaction(() => {
      const id = Number(
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(
            `INSERT INTO price_observations (offer_id, execution_id, source_kind, cabin, fetched_at, provider_observed_at, price_pence, price_native_minor, currency_native,
              fx_rate_nano, fx_rate_date, fx_source, fx_converted, point_of_sale, max_age_hours, taxes_included, self_transfer, segment_cabins_json, baggage_json,
              fare_conditions_json, booking_json, booking_expires_at, supplied_fields_json, partial_expansion)
             VALUES (@offerId, @executionId, @sourceKind, @cabin, @fetchedAt, @providerObservedAt, @pricePence, @priceNativeMinor, @currencyNative,
              @fxRateNano, @fxRateDate, @fxSource, @fxConverted, @pointOfSale, @maxAgeHours, @taxesIncluded, @selfTransfer, @segmentCabins, @baggage,
              @fareConditions, @booking, @bookingExpiresAt, @suppliedFields, @partialExpansion)`,
          )
          .run({
            offerId: obs.offerId, executionId: obs.executionId, sourceKind: p.sourceKind, cabin: p.cabin, fetchedAt: p.fetchedAt, providerObservedAt: p.providerObservedAt ?? null,
            pricePence: obs.pricePence, priceNativeMinor: obs.priceNativeMinor, currencyNative: obs.currencyNative,
            fxRateNano: fx.converted ? fx.rateNano : null, fxRateDate: fx.converted ? fx.rateDate : null, fxSource: fx.converted ? fx.source : null, fxConverted: fx.converted ? 1 : 0,
            pointOfSale: p.pointOfSale,
            // why: spec §7 "max_age_hours NULL (NULL = unknown; written explicitly from CapabilityInfo.maxAgeHours)".
            maxAgeHours: p.maxAgeHours === 'unknown' ? null : p.maxAgeHours,
            taxesIncluded: boolOrNull(obs.taxesIncluded), selfTransfer: boolOrNull(obs.selfTransfer),
            segmentCabins: obs.segmentCabins === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.segmentCabins), baggage: obs.baggage === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.baggage),
            fareConditions: obs.fareConditions === null ? null : JSON.stringify(obs.fareConditions), booking: JSON.stringify(obs.booking),
            bookingExpiresAt: obs.bookingExpiresAt, suppliedFields: JSON.stringify(obs.suppliedFields), partialExpansion: obs.partialExpansion ? 1 : 0,
          }).lastInsertRowid,
      );
      this.db.raw.prepare(RECOMPUTE_OFFER_SQL).run({ id: obs.offerId });
      return id;
    });
  }

  /**
   * Observations from this definition's executions (children when asked, spec §8.1), restricted to the
   * current resolved space (null = no restriction) and cabin. Cheapest first.
   */
  observationsForDefinition(definitionId: string, opts: { executionIds: string[] | 'all'; space: ResolvedSpace | null; cabin: Cabin; includeChildren: boolean }): ObservationRow[] {
    const s = opts.space;
    return this.rows(
      `SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN}
       WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND (@children = 1 OR e.is_child = 0)
         AND (@ids IS NULL OR o.execution_id IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@ids)))
         AND f.cabin = @cabin
         AND (@spaced = 0 OR (i.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND i.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
              AND i.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND i.nights BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights))
       ORDER BY o.price_pence, o.id`,
      {
        definitionId, children: opts.includeChildren ? 1 : 0, ids: opts.executionIds === 'all' ? null : JSON.stringify(opts.executionIds), cabin: opts.cabin, spaced: s === null ? 0 : 1,
        origins: JSON.stringify(s?.origins ?? []), destinations: JSON.stringify(s?.destinations ?? []), from: s?.departureFrom ?? '', to: s?.departureTo ?? '', minNights: s?.minNights ?? 0, maxNights: s?.maxNights ?? 0,
      },
    );
  }

  /** Observations this execution wrote: the verified-basis alert candidates (spec §6.8). */
  writtenBy(executionId: string): ObservationRow[] {
    return this.rows(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} ${OBSERVATION_JOIN} WHERE o.execution_id = @executionId ORDER BY o.id`, { executionId });
  }

  /** The newest observation of the offer fetched strictly before `before` (spec §6.8 drop rules). */
  previousObservation(offerId: number, before: IsoDateTime): Observation | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ObservationDbRow>(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id WHERE o.offer_id = @offerId AND o.fetched_at < @before ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, o.id DESC LIMIT 1`)
      .get({ offerId, before });
    return r === undefined ? null : toObservation(r);
  }

  /** Every observation of every offer of one itinerary, grouped per offer, oldest first (History view). */
  offerHistory(hash: string): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }> {
    const rows = this.db.raw.prepare<[string], ObservationDbRow>(`SELECT ${OBSERVATION_COLUMNS} FROM price_observations o JOIN fare_offers f ON f.id = o.offer_id WHERE f.itinerary_hash = ? ORDER BY f.id, o.fetched_at, o.id`).all(hash);
    const out = new Map<number, { providerId: ProviderId; seller: string; cabin: Cabin; points: HistoryPoint[] }>();
    for (const r of rows) {
      const series = out.get(r.offer_id) ?? { providerId: r.provider_id, seller: r.seller, cabin: r.cabin, points: [] };
      const o = toObservation(r);
      series.points.push({ at: o.provenance.fetchedAt, pricePence: o.pricePence, provenance: o.provenance });
      out.set(r.offer_id, series);
    }
    return [...out.values()];
  }

  /** Spec §8.1 "not seen in latest search": the newest priced|empty verification outcome for the cell across this definition's executions. */
  latestVerificationOutcomeForCell(definitionId: string, cell: CellKey): CellOutcome | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(
        `SELECT o.* FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND o.stage = 'verification' AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty')
           AND o.origin = @origin AND o.destination = @destination AND o.departure_date = @departureDate AND o.return_date = @returnDate
         ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.created_at DESC, e.rowid DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, ...cell });
    return r === undefined ? null : toOutcome(r);
  }

  /** The cell's `best_observation_id` in this execution (the cheapest observation written, decision 14). */
  bestObservationForCell(executionId: string, cell: CellKey): number | null {
    return this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, { id: number | null }>(`SELECT best_observation_id AS id FROM search_cells WHERE ${CELL_WHERE}`).get({ executionId, ...cell })?.id ?? null;
  }

  private rows(sql: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): ObservationRow[] {
    const itineraries = new Map<string, NormalisedItinerary>();
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, ObservationDbRow>(sql)
      .all(params)
      .map((r) => {
        let it = itineraries.get(r.itinerary_hash);
        if (it === undefined) {
          const full = this.get(r.itinerary_hash);
          if (full === null) throw new Error(`observation ${r.id} points at missing itinerary ${r.itinerary_hash}`);
          const { firstSeenAt: _f, ...itinerary } = full;
          it = itinerary;
          itineraries.set(r.itinerary_hash, it);
        }
        const o = toObservation(r);
        return {
          ...o, offer: { id: r.offer_id, itineraryHash: r.itinerary_hash, providerId: r.provider_id, seller: r.seller, cabin: r.cabin }, itinerary: it, seller: r.seller,
          cell: { origin: it.origin, destination: it.destination, departureDate: it.departureDate, returnDate: it.returnDate, nights: it.nights },
        };
      });
  }
}
```

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (imports: `ItinerariesRepo` from `./itineraries.js`; `VerifiedCellWrite` from `../types.js`):

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit: one verified cell's itineraries, segments, offers, observations, outcome,
   * `best_observation_id` and roll-up. A cell is `priced` only if ≥ 1 offer is written (spec §5.4);
   * its `minPence` equals the cheapest observation written. An `error` outcome counts an engine pass.
   */
  writeVerifiedCell(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, w: VerifiedCellWrite): void {
    const key = cellKeyString(w.cell);
    const offers = w.itineraries.flatMap((i) => i.offers.map((o) => ({ ...o, itinerary: i.itinerary })));
    if (cellKeyString(w.outcome) !== key) throw new RowRejected('cell-mismatch', `outcome is for ${cellKeyString(w.outcome)}, not ${key}`);
    if (!['priced', 'empty', 'error'].includes(w.outcome.status)) throw new RowRejected('verified-status', `a verified cell is priced, empty or error; got ${w.outcome.status}`);
    if (w.outcome.status === 'priced' && offers.length === 0) throw new RowRejected('priced-needs-offer', `${key} is priced with no offer (spec §5.4)`);
    if (w.outcome.status !== 'priced' && offers.length > 0) throw new RowRejected('offers-need-priced', `${key} has offers but status ${w.outcome.status}`);
    for (const o of offers) {
      if (cellKeyString(o.itinerary) !== key) throw new RowRejected('cell-mismatch', `itinerary ${o.itinerary.hash} is for ${cellKeyString(o.itinerary)}, not ${key}`);
      if (o.offer.itineraryHash !== o.itinerary.hash || o.offer.providerId !== providerId) throw new RowRejected('offer-mismatch', `offer does not belong to itinerary ${o.itinerary.hash} from ${providerId}`);
    }
    const cheapest = offers.length === 0 ? null : Math.min(...offers.map((o) => o.observation.pricePence));
    if (w.outcome.status === 'priced' && w.outcome.minPence !== cheapest) throw new RowRejected('min-pence', `${key} min_pence ${String(w.outcome.minPence)} differs from the cheapest offer ${String(cheapest)}`);
    assertNullableIsoDateTime('fetched_at', w.outcome.fetchedAt);
    const itineraries = new ItinerariesRepo(this.db, this.clock, this.opts);
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      let best: { id: number; pence: number } | null = null;
      for (const o of offers) {
        itineraries.upsertItinerary(o.itinerary);
        const offerId = itineraries.upsertOffer(o.offer);
        const id = itineraries.insertObservation({ ...o.observation, offerId, executionId });
        if (best === null || o.observation.pricePence < best.pence) best = { id, pence: o.observation.pricePence };
      }
      const params = { ...keyParams(executionId, w.cell), providerId };
      const changes = this.db.raw
        .prepare(
          `UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, last_error = @lastError, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence,
             cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, attempts = attempts + @attempt
           WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = 'verification' AND provider_id = @providerId`,
        )
        .run({
          ...params, status: w.outcome.status, reason: w.outcome.reason, lastError: w.outcome.lastError === null ? null : stripUrlsInText(w.outcome.lastError),
          fetchedAt: w.outcome.fetchedAt, minPence: best?.pence ?? null, cabin: w.outcome.cabin, pointOfSale: w.outcome.pointOfSale, sourceKind: w.outcome.sourceKind,
          attempt: w.outcome.status === 'error' ? 1 : 0,
        }).changes;
      if (changes === 0) throw new RowRejected('missing', `no seeded verification outcome for ${providerId} at ${key}`);
      if (best !== null) {
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(`UPDATE search_cells SET best_observation_id = @bestId WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND (best_observation_id IS NULL OR (SELECT price_pence FROM price_observations WHERE id = search_cells.best_observation_id) > @bestPence)`)
          .run({ ...params, bestId: best.id, bestPence: best.pence });
      }
      this.recompute(executionId, 'verification', w.cell);
      if (w.outcome.status !== 'error') this.db.raw.prepare('UPDATE search_executions SET auto_resume_count = 0 WHERE id = ?').run(executionId);
    });
  }
```

```ts
import { type CellKey, type IsoDateTime, cellKeyString, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { CellsRepo } from '../src/repositories/cells.js';
import { ExecutionsRepo } from '../src/repositories/executions.js';
import type { TileWrite } from '../src/types.js';
import { T0, count, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { EWR, calendarPrice, cell, newExecution, snapshotOf, withNights } from './helpers/factories.js';

const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;
const P = cell(); // priced
const E = cell(undefined, EWR); // empty
const N = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-15', '2027-03-29'); // no_data
const X = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-16', '2027-03-30'); // error
const R = cell(undefined, undefined, '2027-03-17', '2027-03-31'); // left not_run
const ALL = [P, E, N, X, R];
const opts = { allowSynthetic: false };
type TileOutcome = TileWrite['outcomes'][number];
const out = (c: CellKey, status: TileOutcome['status'], fetchedAt: IsoDateTime, minPence: number | null = null): TileOutcome => ({
  ...c, status, reason: null, lastError: null, fetchedAt, minPence, cabin: 'economy', pointOfSale: 'unknown', sourceKind: 'authorised-cached',
});
const pricedTile = (c: CellKey, pence: number, fetchedAt: IsoDateTime): TileWrite => ({
  tileIndex: 0, cells: [c], prices: [calendarPrice(c, { pricePence: pence, priceNativeMinor: pence, fetchedAt })], outcomes: [out(c, 'priced', fetchedAt, pence)], discards: 0,
});
const ask = (prices: CalendarPricesRepo, definitionId: string, cells: CellKey[], newerThan = '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z') =>
  prices.reuseCandidates(definitionId, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', cells, at(newerThan));

function world() {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const cells = new CellsRepo(db, clock, opts);
  const prices = new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts);
  const { definition, execution: a } = newExecution(db, clock, { status: 'completed' });
  cells.seed(a.id, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
  cells.writeTile(a.id, 'travelpayouts', {
    tileIndex: 0, cells: [P, E, N], prices: [calendarPrice(P, { pricePence: 40_000, priceNativeMinor: 40_000 })], outcomes: [out(P, 'priced', T0, 40_000), out(E, 'empty', T0), out(N, 'no_data', T0)], discards: 0,
  });
  cells.writeTileError(a.id, 'travelpayouts', [X], 'upstream', null);
  return { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a: a.id };
}

describe('reuse (spec §8.1 "reuse, per cell", §7 atomic unit "each reused cell")', () => {
  it('candidates: newest priced|empty|no_data per cell for the same provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind; never error or not_run', () => {
    const { prices, definition, a } = world();
    const copies = ask(prices, definition.id, ALL);
    expect(copies.map((c) => cellKeyString(c.cell)).sort()).toEqual([P, E, N].map(cellKeyString).sort());
    expect(copies.find((c) => cellKeyString(c.cell) === cellKeyString(P))).toMatchObject({ outcome: { executionId: a, status: 'priced', minPence: 40_000 }, price: { executionId: a, pricePence: 40_000 } });
    expect(copies.find((c) => cellKeyString(c.cell) === cellKeyString(E))?.price).toBeNull();
    expect(ask(prices, definition.id, [P])).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('refuses on a cabin, point-of-sale or source-kind change, and on age (fetched_at must be newer than the cutoff)', () => {
    const { prices, definition } = world();
    const n = (cabin: 'economy' | 'business', pos: string, kind: 'authorised-cached' | 'scraped-aggregate', newerThan: string) => prices.reuseCandidates(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', cabin, pos, kind, ALL, at(newerThan)).length;
    expect(n('business', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'GB', 'authorised-cached', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'unknown', 'scraped-aggregate', '2026-10-14T11:00:00.000Z')).toBe(0);
    expect(n('economy', 'unknown', 'authorised-cached', T0)).toBe(0);
  });

  it('a newer execution wins; child executions are never reuse sources', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(b, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile(P, 39_000, clock.now()));
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const child = newExecution(db, clock, { definition, cell: P, status: 'completed' }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(child, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(child, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile(P, 1, clock.now()));
    expect(ask(prices, definition.id, [P])[0]?.outcome.executionId).toBe(b);
  });

  it('copyReused copies outcome and price with the original fetched_at and reused_from = the fetching execution, even from a reused copy', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, ALL));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [P] })[0]).toMatchObject({ status: 'priced', fetchedAt: T0, minPence: 40_000, reusedFromExecutionId: a });
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [X] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(cells.rollUps(c).get(cellKeyString(P))?.discovery).toEqual({ status: 'priced', reason: null, minPence: 40_000 });
    expect(cells.buckets(c, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts')).toEqual({ priced: 1, empty: 1, no_data: 1, error: 0, skipped: 0, not_run: 2, reused: 3 });
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT fetched_at AS f, reused_from_execution_id AS r FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').all(c)).toEqual([{ f: T0, r: a }]);
    expect(prices.fetchedBy(c)).toEqual([]); // a reused copy is not "fetched by" c
    new ExecutionsRepo(db, clock, opts).setStatus(c, 'completed');
    clock.advance(60_000);
    const d = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(d, [withNights(P)], ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(d, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P]));
    expect(cells.outcomes(d, { cells: [P] })[0]?.reusedFromExecutionId).toBe(a);
  });

  it('copyReused refuses error or not_run sources, another provider, and targets no longer not_run, writing nothing', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    const errorOutcome = cells.outcomes(a, { cells: [X] })[0];
    if (errorOutcome === undefined) throw new Error('fixture');
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', [{ cell: X, outcome: errorOutcome, price: null }])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    const copies = ask(prices, definition.id, ALL);
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'searchapi', copies)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-source' }));
    cells.setOutcome(c, E, 'discovery', 'travelpayouts', { status: 'error', reason: 'network' });
    expect(() => cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', copies)).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'reuse-target' }));
    expect(cells.outcomes(c, { cells: [P] })[0]?.status).toBe('not_run');
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });

  it('supersedeReused deletes only the reused row', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition } = world();
    const c = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(c, ALL.map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.copyReused(c, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [P]));
    prices.supersedeReused(c, 'travelpayouts', P);
    expect(db.raw.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = ?').get(c)).toEqual({ n: 0 });
    expect(count(db, 'calendar_prices')).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe('calendar-basis reads (spec §6.9, §6.8) and history (spec §7 "reused_from_execution_id IS NULL")', () => {
  it('latestPerCell, belowVerified, previousFor and history ignore reused copies and apply cabin and space', () => {
    const { db, clock, cells, prices, definition, a } = world();
    clock.advance(3_600_000);
    const b = newExecution(db, clock, { definition }).execution.id;
    cells.seed(b, [P, N].map(withNights), ['travelpayouts']);
    cells.writeTile(b, 'travelpayouts', pricedTile(P, 41_000, clock.now()));
    cells.copyReused(b, 'travelpayouts', ask(prices, definition.id, [N]));
    const space = snapshotOf(definition).resolved;
    const rows = prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', space);
    expect(rows.map((r) => [r.executionId, r.pricePence, r.reusedFromExecutionId])).toEqual([[b, 41_000, null]]);
    expect(rows[0]?.provenance).toMatchObject({ providerId: 'travelpayouts', fetchedAt: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', maxAgeHours: 48, fx: { converted: false } });
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'business', space)).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.latestPerCell(definition.id, 'economy', { ...space, minNights: 15 })).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.belowVerified(definition.id, 'economy', space, 41_001).map((r) => r.pricePence)).toEqual([41_000]);
    expect(prices.belowVerified(definition.id, 'economy', space, 41_000)).toEqual([]);
    expect(prices.previousFor(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', P, at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), at('2026-10-07T13:00:00.000Z'))).toMatchObject({ executionId: a, pricePence: 40_000 });
    expect(prices.previousFor(definition.id, 'travelpayouts', 'economy', P, at('2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z'), at('2026-10-14T12:00:00.001Z'))).toBeNull();
    expect(prices.history(P.origin, P.destination, 'economy')).toEqual([
      { providerId: 'travelpayouts', points: [{ at: T0, pricePence: 40_000, provenance: expect.objectContaining({ fetchedAt: T0 }) }, { at: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z', pricePence: 41_000, provenance: expect.objectContaining({ fetchedAt: '2026-10-14T13:00:00.000Z' }) }] },
    ]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/calendarPrices.ts` (imports: `Cabin`, `CellKey`, `HistoryPoint`, `Iata`, `IsoDateTime`, `PointOfSale`, `ProviderId`, `ResolvedSpace`, `SourceKind`, `cellKeyString` from `@gmt/core`; `type OutcomeDbRow, toOutcome` from `../internal/outcomeRow.js`; `assertIsoDateTime` from `../internal/validate.js`; `ReusedCopy` from `../types.js`):

```ts
/**
 * Spec §8.1 reuse source: per cell, the newest priced|empty|no_data discovery outcome for the same
 * definition, provider, cabin, point of sale and source kind, from a non-child execution, whose
 * original `fetched_at` is newer than the cutoff (the engine passes now − 0.9 × discoveryRefreshHours).
 */
const REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT o.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY o.origin, o.destination, o.departure_date, o.return_date ORDER BY o.fetched_at DESC, e.created_at DESC, e.rowid DESC) AS rn
  FROM cell_outcomes o JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = o.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND e.is_child = 0 AND o.stage = 'discovery' AND o.provider_id = @providerId
    AND o.status IN ('priced', 'empty', 'no_data') AND o.cabin = @cabin AND o.point_of_sale = @pointOfSale AND o.source_kind = @sourceKind AND o.fetched_at > @newerThan
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date`;

const PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL = `SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId
  AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate`;

/** Spec §6.9 calendar basis: latest fetched per (provider, cell) in the cabin, reused copies ignored, inside the current space when given. */
const LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL = `
WITH ranked AS (
  SELECT p.*, ROW_NUMBER() OVER (PARTITION BY p.provider_id, p.origin, p.destination, p.departure_date, p.return_date ORDER BY p.fetched_at DESC, p.id DESC) AS rn
  FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
  WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL
    AND (@spaced = 0 OR (p.origin IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@origins)) AND p.destination IN (SELECT value FROM json_each(@destinations))
         AND p.departure_date BETWEEN @from AND @to AND CAST(julianday(p.return_date) - julianday(p.departure_date) AS INTEGER) BETWEEN @minNights AND @maxNights))
)
SELECT * FROM ranked WHERE rn = 1 ORDER BY origin, destination, departure_date, return_date, provider_id`;
```

```ts
  reuseCandidates(definitionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cabin: Cabin, pointOfSale: PointOfSale, sourceKind: SourceKind, cells: CellKey[], newerThan: IsoDateTime): ReusedCopy[] {
    assertIsoDateTime('newerThan', newerThan);
    const wanted = new Set(cells.map(cellKeyString));
    const priceStmt = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(PRICE_FOR_OUTCOME_SQL);
    const out: ReusedCopy[] = [];
    for (const r of this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(REUSE_CANDIDATES_SQL).all({ definitionId, providerId, cabin, pointOfSale, sourceKind, newerThan })) {
      const outcome = toOutcome(r);
      const cell: CellKey = { origin: outcome.origin, destination: outcome.destination, departureDate: outcome.departureDate, returnDate: outcome.returnDate };
      if (!wanted.has(cellKeyString(cell))) continue;
      let price: CalendarPriceRow | null = null;
      if (outcome.status === 'priced') {
        const p = priceStmt.get({ executionId: outcome.executionId, providerId, ...cell });
        // why: writeTile enforces a price for every priced outcome; a missing one is corruption, not a reusable cell.
        if (p === undefined) throw new Error(`priced outcome ${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${outcome.executionId} has no calendar price`);
        price = toCalendarPriceRow(p);
      }
      out.push({ cell, outcome, price });
    }
    return out;
  }

  /** Spec §7 "a fresh price superseding a reused row deletes that row in the same transaction" (writeTile does it inline). Fresh rows are never deleted. */
  supersedeReused(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cell: CellKey): void {
    this.db.raw
      .prepare(`DELETE FROM calendar_prices WHERE execution_id = @executionId AND provider_id = @providerId AND origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND departure_date = @departureDate AND return_date = @returnDate AND reused_from_execution_id IS NOT NULL`)
      .run({ executionId, providerId, ...cell });
  }

  latestPerCell(definitionId: string, cabin: Cabin, space: ResolvedSpace | null): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(LATEST_PER_CELL_SQL)
      .all({
        definitionId, cabin, spaced: space === null ? 0 : 1, origins: JSON.stringify(space?.origins ?? []), destinations: JSON.stringify(space?.destinations ?? []),
        from: space?.departureFrom ?? '', to: space?.departureTo ?? '', minNights: space?.minNights ?? 0, maxNights: space?.maxNights ?? 0,
      })
      .map(toCalendarPriceRow);
  }

  /** Calendar-gap source (spec §10): latest calendar prices strictly below the cheapest verified price. */
  belowVerified(definitionId: string, cabin: Cabin, space: ResolvedSpace, thresholdPence: number): CalendarPriceRow[] {
    return this.latestPerCell(definitionId, cabin, space).filter((r) => r.pricePence < thresholdPence);
  }

  /** Spec §6.8 calendar drops: the newest fetched (not reused) price for the same (provider, cabin, cell) with fetched_at in [notOlderThan, before). */
  previousFor(definitionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, cabin: Cabin, cell: CellKey, before: IsoDateTime, notOlderThan: IsoDateTime): CalendarPriceRow | null {
    const r = this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(
        `SELECT p.* FROM calendar_prices p JOIN search_executions e ON e.id = p.execution_id
         WHERE e.definition_id = @definitionId AND p.provider_id = @providerId AND p.cabin = @cabin AND p.reused_from_execution_id IS NULL
           AND p.origin = @origin AND p.destination = @destination AND p.departure_date = @departureDate AND p.return_date = @returnDate
           AND p.fetched_at < @before AND p.fetched_at >= @notOlderThan
         ORDER BY p.fetched_at DESC, p.id DESC LIMIT 1`,
      )
      .get({ definitionId, providerId, cabin, ...cell, before, notOlderThan });
    return r === undefined ? null : toCalendarPriceRow(r);
  }

  /** Route calendar history per provider, fetched rows only (reused copies excluded), oldest first. */
  history(origin: Iata, destination: Iata, cabin: Cabin): Array<{ providerId: ProviderId; points: HistoryPoint[] }> {
    const out = new Map<ProviderId, HistoryPoint[]>();
    for (const r of this.db.raw
      .prepare<Record<string, unknown>, CalendarPriceDbRow>(`SELECT * FROM calendar_prices WHERE origin = @origin AND destination = @destination AND cabin = @cabin AND reused_from_execution_id IS NULL ORDER BY provider_id, fetched_at, id`)
      .all({ origin, destination, cabin })) {
      const row = toCalendarPriceRow(r);
      const points = out.get(row.providerId) ?? [];
      points.push({ at: row.fetchedAt, pricePence: row.pricePence, provenance: row.provenance });
      out.set(row.providerId, points);
    }
    return [...out.entries()].map(([providerId, points]) => ({ providerId, points }));
  }
```

Add to `packages/db/src/repositories/cells.ts` (import `ReusedCopy` from `../types.js`):

```ts
  /**
   * Spec §7 atomic unit "each reused cell's outcome + copied prices + roll-up" (one transaction for the batch).
   * Copies keep the original `fetched_at` and provenance; `reused_from_execution_id` is always the
   * execution that fetched the price, even when the source is itself a reused copy.
   */
  copyReused(executionId: string, providerId: ProviderId, copies: ReusedCopy[]): void {
    for (const { outcome, price } of copies) {
      if (outcome.status !== 'priced' && outcome.status !== 'empty' && outcome.status !== 'no_data') throw new RowRejected('reuse-source', `only priced, empty or no_data outcomes are reused, got ${outcome.status} (spec §8.1)`);
      if (outcome.providerId !== providerId || outcome.stage !== 'discovery') throw new RowRejected('reuse-source', `a ${outcome.providerId} ${outcome.stage} outcome cannot be reused for ${providerId} discovery`);
      if ((outcome.status === 'priced') !== (price !== null)) throw new RowRejected('reuse-source', `reused ${outcome.status} outcome with${price === null ? 'out' : ''} a price`);
    }
    this.db.transaction(() => {
      for (const { cell, outcome, price } of copies) {
        const key = { ...keyParams(executionId, cell), stage: 'discovery', providerId };
        const target = this.db.raw.prepare<Record<string, unknown>, OutcomeDbRow>(SELECT_OUTCOME_SQL).get(key);
        if (target?.status !== 'not_run') throw new RowRejected('reuse-target', `${cellKeyString(cell)} in ${executionId} is ${target?.status ?? 'missing'}, not not_run`);
        const reusedFrom = outcome.reusedFromExecutionId ?? outcome.executionId;
        this.db.raw
          .prepare(`UPDATE cell_outcomes SET status = @status, reason = @reason, fetched_at = @fetchedAt, min_pence = @minPence, cabin = @cabin, point_of_sale = @pointOfSale, source_kind = @sourceKind, reused_from_execution_id = @reusedFrom WHERE ${CELL_WHERE} AND stage = @stage AND provider_id = @providerId`)
          .run({ ...key, status: outcome.status, reason: outcome.reason, fetchedAt: outcome.fetchedAt, minPence: outcome.minPence, cabin: outcome.cabin, pointOfSale: outcome.pointOfSale, sourceKind: outcome.sourceKind, reusedFrom });
        if (price !== null) {
          const copy = { ...price, reusedFromExecutionId: reusedFrom };
          validateCalendarPrice(copy, this.opts.allowSynthetic, false);
          insertCalendarPrice(this.db, executionId, copy);
        }
        this.recompute(executionId, 'discovery', cell);
      }
    });
  }
```

```ts
import { type IsoDateTime, asIsoDate, fixedClock } from '@gmt/core';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { RowRejected } from '../src/internal/validate.js';
import { AlertsRepo } from '../src/repositories/alerts.js';
import { CalendarPricesRepo } from '../src/repositories/calendarPrices.js';
import { NotificationsRepo } from '../src/repositories/notifications.js';
import { RoutePriceHistoryRepo } from '../src/repositories/routePriceHistory.js';
import { T0, freshDb } from './helpers/db.js';
import { JFK, LHR, calendarPrice, cell, newExecution } from './helpers/factories.js';

const at = (s: string) => s as IsoDateTime;
const opts = { allowSynthetic: false };

function setup() {
  const db = freshDb();
  const clock = fixedClock(T0);
  const { definition, execution } = newExecution(db, clock);
  const rule = { definitionId: definition.id, type: 'absolute' as const, thresholdPence: 45_000, pct: null, basis: 'verified' as const, requireAllVerified: true, cooldownHours: 336, enabled: true };
  return { db, clock, definition, executionId: execution.id, rule, alerts: new AlertsRepo(db, clock, opts) };
}

describe('AlertsRepo (spec §6.8, §7 alert_rules / alert_events)', () => {
  it('creates, gets, lists, updates and deletes rules; cooldown defaults to 336 h in SQL', () => {
    const { db, definition, rule, alerts } = setup();
    const r = alerts.create(rule);
    expect(alerts.get(r.id)).toEqual({ ...rule, id: r.id });
    expect(alerts.rules(definition.id)).toEqual([{ ...rule, id: r.id }]);
    expect(alerts.update(r.id, { thresholdPence: 40_000, enabled: false })).toMatchObject({ thresholdPence: 40_000, enabled: false });
    db.raw.prepare(`INSERT INTO alert_rules (id, definition_id, type, pct, basis, enabled) VALUES ('r2', ?, 'dropPct', 10, 'calendar', 1)`).run(definition.id);
    expect(alerts.get('r2')?.cooldownHours).toBe(336);
    alerts.delete(r.id);
    expect(alerts.rules().map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['r2']);
    expect(alerts.get(r.id)).toBeNull();
    expect(() => alerts.update('missing', {})).toThrow(RowRejected);
  });

  it('rejects requireAllVerified on a calendar rule and non-integer thresholds', () => {
    const { rule, alerts } = setup();
    expect(() => alerts.create({ ...rule, basis: 'calendar', requireAllVerified: true })).toThrow(/requireAllVerified/);
    expect(() => alerts.create({ ...rule, thresholdPence: 450.5 })).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'integer' }));
  });

  it('priorFirings: sent events for the identities within the window, with the price that fired; payload URLs stripped', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId, rule, alerts } = setup();
    const r = alerts.create({ ...rule, basis: 'calendar', requireAllVerified: null });
    const [priceId] = new CalendarPricesRepo(db, clock, opts).insert(executionId, [calendarPrice(cell(), { pricePence: 41_200, priceNativeMinor: 41_200 })]);
    if (priceId === undefined) throw new Error('fixture');
    const base = { ruleId: r.id, identityKey: `${r.id}|travelpayouts|economy|LHR|JFK|2027-03-14|2027-03-28`, observationId: null, calendarPriceId: priceId, dedupKey: `${r.id}|x|412`, reason: null };
    alerts.insertEvent({ ...base, firedAt: at('2026-10-01T05:00:00.000Z'), status: 'sent', payload: { link: 'https://www.aviasales.com/search?marker=SECRET' } });
    alerts.insertEvent({ ...base, firedAt: at('2026-10-10T05:00:00.000Z'), status: 'failed', payload: {} });
    alerts.insertEvent({ ...base, identityKey: 'other', firedAt: at('2026-10-11T05:00:00.000Z'), status: 'sent', payload: {} });
    expect(alerts.priorFirings(r.id, [base.identityKey], at('2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z'))).toEqual([{ identityKey: base.identityKey, dedupKey: base.dedupKey, firedAt: '2026-10-01T05:00:00.000Z', pricePence: 41_200 }]);
    expect(alerts.priorFirings(r.id, [base.identityKey], at('2026-10-02T00:00:00.000Z'))).toEqual([]);
    expect(alerts.priorFirings(r.id, [], at('2026-09-30T00:00:00.000Z'))).toEqual([]);
    const events = alerts.events({ definitionId: definition.id, limit: 10 });
    expect(events.map((e) => e.status)).toEqual(['sent', 'failed', 'sent']);
    expect(JSON.stringify(events.at(-1)?.payload)).not.toContain('SECRET');
    expect(alerts.events({ ruleId: r.id, limit: 1 })).toHaveLength(1);
  });
});

describe('NotificationsRepo (spec §8.1 "deduplicated per execution and kind")', () => {
  it('records and answers wasSent per (kind, execution, definition), including execution-less notices', () => {
    const { db, clock, definition, executionId } = setup();
    const n = new NotificationsRepo(db, clock, opts);
    expect(n.wasSent('paused_budget', executionId, definition.id)).toBe(false);
    n.record('paused_budget', executionId, definition.id, 'sent');
    expect(n.wasSent('paused_budget', executionId, definition.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(n.wasSent('interrupted', executionId, definition.id)).toBe(false);
    n.record('skipped_missed', null, definition.id, 'failed');
    expect(n.wasSent('skipped_missed', null, definition.id)).toBe(false); // a failed send is not "sent"
    n.record('skipped_missed', null, definition.id, 'sent');
    expect(n.wasSent('skipped_missed', null, definition.id)).toBe(true);
    expect(n.wasSent('skipped_missed', null, newExecution(db, clock).definition.id)).toBe(false); // another definition
  });
});

describe('RoutePriceHistoryRepo (spec §7 route_price_history)', () => {
  it("upserts by (origin, destination, cabin, provider, date), allows cabin 'unknown', and returns the series in date order", () => {
    const { db, clock } = setup();
    const repo = new RoutePriceHistoryRepo(db, clock, opts);
    const row = (date: string, pence: number, fetchedAt = T0) => ({ origin: LHR, destination: JFK, cabin: 'unknown' as const, providerId: 'searchapi' as const, date: asIsoDate(date), lowestPricePence: pence, fetchedAt });
    repo.upsert([row('2026-09-02', 52_000), row('2026-09-01', 51_000)]);
    repo.upsert([row('2026-09-02', 50_500, at('2026-10-15T05:00:00.000Z'))]);
    expect(repo.series(LHR, JFK, 'searchapi')).toEqual([
      { date: '2026-09-01', pricePence: 51_000, fetchedAt: T0, providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
      { date: '2026-09-02', pricePence: 50_500, fetchedAt: '2026-10-15T05:00:00.000Z', providerId: 'searchapi', sourceKind: 'scraped-search' },
    ]);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([row('2026-09-03', 1.5)])).toThrow(RowRejected);
    expect(() => repo.upsert([{ ...row('2026-09-03', 1), providerId: 'fixture' }])).toThrow(expect.objectContaining({ rule: 'synthetic-gate' }));
  });
});
```
